ملفات ShopMate مع خريطة الشرح

نسخة المصدر المحلية بتاريخ 21 أكتوبر 2026. افتح اسم الملف باش تشوف الكود كامل بأرقام السطور. قرا الشرح التفصيلي فالـPDF المرافق. بعض القيم الحساسة الثابتة محجوبة؛ ملفات البيئة والأسرار ما مدرجينش.

.github/workflows/backend-tests.yml

npm run كيشغل الأمر المسجل تحت scripts، وماشي جميع أسماء scripts اختبارات.

   1  name: Backend tests
   2  
   3  on:
   4    push:
   5      paths:
   6        - "backend/**"
   7        - ".github/workflows/backend-tests.yml"
   8    pull_request:
   9      paths:
  10        - "backend/**"
  11        - ".github/workflows/backend-tests.yml"
  12  
  13  jobs:
  14    test:
  15      runs-on: ubuntu-latest
  16      defaults:
  17        run:
  18          working-directory: backend
  19      steps:
  20        - uses: actions/checkout@v4
  21        - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
  22          with:
  23            node-version: 22
  24            cache: npm
  25            cache-dependency-path: backend/package-lock.json
  26        - run: npm ci
  27        - run: npm test
backend/docker-compose.yml

الصورة كتجمع التطبيق، وCompose كيربطه بقاعدة البيانات.

   1  services:
   2    db:
   3      image: pgvector/pgvector:pg16
   4      environment:
   5        POSTGRES_USER: ${DB_USER:-postgres}
   6        POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD:-password}
   7        POSTGRES_DB: ${DB_NAME:-shopmate}
   8      ports:
   9        - "5432:5432"
  10      volumes:
  11        - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
  12      healthcheck:
  13        test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U $${POSTGRES_USER} -d $${POSTGRES_DB}"]
  14        interval: 5s
  15        timeout: 5s
  16        retries: 10
  17        start_period: 5s
  18  
  19    api:
  20      build:
  21        context: .
  22      env_file:
  23        - ./.env.docker
  24        - path: ./.env
  25          required: false
  26      environment:
  27        NODE_ENV: production
  28        PORT: 4000
  29        DB_HOST: db
  30      ports:
  31        - "4000:4000"
  32      depends_on:
  33        db:
  34          condition: service_healthy
  35      healthcheck:
  36        test: ["CMD-SHELL", "wget -qO- http://localhost:4000/health >/dev/null 2>&1"]
  37        interval: 10s
  38        timeout: 3s
  39        retries: 5
  40        start_period: 20s
  41      restart: unless-stopped
  42  
  43  volumes:
  44    postgres_data:
backend/docker-entrypoint.sh

الصورة كتجمع التطبيق، وCompose كيربطه بقاعدة البيانات.

   1  #!/bin/sh
   2  set -eu
   3  
   4  echo "Running database migrations..."
   5  ./node_modules/.bin/sequelize-cli db:migrate \
   6    --env production \
   7    --config src/config/sequelize-cli.js \
   8    --migrations-path migrations
   9  
  10  echo "Ensuring pgvector index..."
  11  node dist/scripts/applyPgvectorSql.js
  12  
  13  if [ -n "${SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD:-}" ]; then
  14    echo "Running idempotent demo seed..."
  15    node dist/seed.js
  16  else
  17    echo "SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD is not set; skipping optional demo seed."
  18  fi
  19  
  20  echo "Starting ShopMate API..."
  21  exec node dist/server.js
backend/Dockerfile

الصورة كتجمع التطبيق، وCompose كيربطه بقاعدة البيانات.

   1  # Build TypeScript with development dependencies kept out of the final image.
   2  FROM node:22-alpine AS build
   3  WORKDIR /app
   4  COPY package*.json ./
   5  RUN npm ci
   6  COPY tsconfig.json ./
   7  COPY src ./src
   8  RUN npx tsc
   9  
  10  # Install only production packages, including sequelize-cli for startup migrations.
  11  FROM node:22-alpine AS production-dependencies
  12  WORKDIR /app
  13  COPY package*.json ./
  14  RUN npm ci --omit=dev && npm cache clean --force
  15  
  16  FROM node:22-alpine AS runtime
  17  WORKDIR /app
  18  ENV NODE_ENV=production
  19  
  20  # Do not run the API process as root.
  21  RUN addgroup -S shopmate && adduser -S shopmate -G shopmate
  22  
  23  COPY --from=production-dependencies --chown=shopmate:shopmate /app/node_modules ./node_modules
  24  COPY --from=build --chown=shopmate:shopmate /app/dist ./dist
  25  COPY --chown=shopmate:shopmate package*.json ./
  26  COPY --chown=shopmate:shopmate migrations ./migrations
  27  COPY --chown=shopmate:shopmate sql ./sql
  28  COPY --chown=shopmate:shopmate src/config/sequelize-cli.js ./src/config/sequelize-cli.js
  29  COPY --chown=shopmate:shopmate docker-entrypoint.sh /usr/local/bin/shopmate-start
  30  RUN chmod 755 /usr/local/bin/shopmate-start
  31  
  32  USER shopmate
  33  EXPOSE 4000
  34  ENTRYPOINT ["/usr/local/bin/shopmate-start"]
backend/migrations/20261009112905-create-stores.js

Migration create-stores: up كيطبق تغيير بنية القاعدة وdown كيرجعو. كتتقرا حسب ترتيب التاريخ فاسم الملف. كتخلق جدول stores مع الأعمدة والقيود المذكورة فالمقتطف.

   1  
   2  "use strict";
   3  
   4  module.exports = {
   5    async up(queryInterface, Sequelize) {
   6      await queryInterface.createTable("stores", {
   7        id: {
   8          type: Sequelize.INTEGER,
   9          autoIncrement: true,
  10          primaryKey: true,
  11          allowNull: false,
  12        },
  13  
  14        name: {
  15          type: Sequelize.STRING,
  16          allowNull: false,
  17        },
  18  
  19        createdAt: {
  20          type: Sequelize.DATE,
  21          allowNull: false,
  22        },
  23  
  24        updatedAt: {
  25          type: Sequelize.DATE,
  26          allowNull: false,
  27        },
  28      });
  29    },
  30  
  31    async down(queryInterface) {
  32      await queryInterface.dropTable("stores");
  33    },
  34  };

Migration align-model-constraints: up كيطبق تغيير بنية القاعدة وdown كيرجعو. كتتقرا حسب ترتيب التاريخ فاسم الملف. كتعدل uniqueness وربط OrderItem بCustomer/Order؛ migration اللي بعدها كتراجع قيد المستخدم الواحد لكل متجر.

   2  
   3  module.exports = {
   4    async up(queryInterface, Sequelize) {
   5      await queryInterface.removeConstraint("users", "users_email_key");

   7        unique: true,
   8        name: "users_storeId_email_unique",
   9      });
  10      await queryInterface.addIndex("users", ["storeId"], {
  11        unique: true,
  12        name: "users_storeId_unique",
  13      });
  14

  16        unique: true,
  17        name: "orders_customerId_id_unique",
  18      });

  20        type: Sequelize.INTEGER,
  21        allowNull: true,
  22      });
  23      await queryInterface.sequelize.query(
  24        'UPDATE "order_items" AS item SET "customerId" = "orders"."customerId" FROM "orders" WHERE item."orderId" = "orders"."id"'
  25      );
  26      await queryInterface.changeColumn("order_items", "customerId", {
  27        type: Sequelize.INTEGER,
  28        allowNull: false,
  29        references: {
  30          model: "customers",
  31          key: "id",
  32        },
  33        onUpdate: "CASCADE",
  34        onDelete: "RESTRICT",
  35      });
  36      await queryInterface.addConstraint("order_items", {
  37        fields: ["customerId", "orderId"],
  38        type: "foreign key",
  39        name: "order_items_customer_order_fkey",
  40        references: {
  41          table: "orders",
  42          fields: ["customerId", "id"],
  43        },
  44        onUpdate: "CASCADE",
  45        onDelete: "CASCADE",
  46      });
  47      await queryInterface.addIndex(
  48        "order_items",

backend/sql/001_init_pgvector.sql

البحث كيقارن تمثيل السؤال العددي بتمثيلات المقاطع داخل متجر واحد.

   1  
   2  -- Enable pgvector extension
   3  CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector;
   4  
   5  -- Create HNSW index for cosine similarity
   6  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "chunks_embedding_hnsw_idx"
   7  ON "chunks"
   8  USING hnsw ("embedding" vector_cosine_ops);
backend/src/app.ts

هاد الملف كيبني تطبيق Express ويربط كل مجموعة URLs بالـrouter ديالها.

   1  import express, { Request, Response } from "express";
   2  import cors from "cors";
   3  import helmet from "helmet";
   4  import { corsOptions } from "./config/cors";
   5  import { errorHandler, globalRateLimiter } from "./middlewares";
   6  import authRouter from "./routes/auth";
   7  import productsRouter from "./routes/products";
   8  import categoriesRouter from "./routes/categories";
   9  import ordersRouter from "./routes/orders";
  10  import documentsRouter from "./routes/documents";
  11  import agentRouter from "./routes/agent";
  12  import swaggerRouter from "./docs/swagger";
  13  
  14  const appserver = express();
  15  
  16  // 1. Security Headers via Helmet
  17  appserver.use(helmet());
  18  
  19  // 2. Restrictive CORS
  20  appserver.use(cors(corsOptions));
  21  
  22  // 3. Global Rate Limiting
  23  appserver.use(globalRateLimiter);
  24  
  25  // 4. Body parsing
  26  appserver.use(express.json());
  27  
  28  // API Documentation & Specs (Swagger UI at /api/docs)
  29  appserver.use("/api", swaggerRouter);
  30  
  31  // API Routes
  32  appserver.use("/api/auth", authRouter);
  33  appserver.use("/api/products", productsRouter);
  34  appserver.use("/api/categories", categoriesRouter);
  35  appserver.use("/api/orders", ordersRouter);
  36  appserver.use("/api/documents", documentsRouter);
  37  appserver.use("/api/agent", agentRouter);
  38  
  39  appserver.get('/health', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  40      res.status(200).json({ message: ' oki Server is healthy' });
  41  });
  42  
  43  // Centralized error handler (must be last)
  44  appserver.use(errorHandler);
  45  
  46  export default appserver;
backend/src/config/cors.ts

كيقرأ origins المسموحين؛ الطلب بلا Origin مسموح لnative/curl. CORS ماشي بديل JWT.

   1  import { CorsOptions } from "cors";
   2  
   3  const allowedOrigins: string[] = process.env.ALLOWED_ORIGINS
   4    ? process.env.ALLOWED_ORIGINS.split(",").map((origin) => origin.trim())
   5    : [
   6        "http://localhost:3000",
   7        "http://localhost:8081",
   8        "http://localhost:19006",
   9        "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
  10        "http://127.0.0.1:8081",
  11      ];
  12  
  13  export const corsOptions: CorsOptions = {
  14    origin: (origin, callback) => {
  15      // Allow requests with no origin (such as mobile apps or curl)
  16      if (!origin) {
  17        return callback(null, true);
  18      }
  19  
  20      if (allowedOrigins.includes(origin)) {
  21        return callback(null, true);
  22      }
  23  
  24      return callback(new Error("Not allowed by restrictive CORS policy"));
  25    },
  26    credentials: true,
  27    methods: ["GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "PATCH", "OPTIONS"],
  28    allowedHeaders: ["Content-Type", "Authorization", "X-Requested-With"],
  29  };
backend/src/config/database.ts

كيخلق Sequelize PostgreSQL connection من DB_HOST/PORT/NAME/USER/PASSWORD. logging:false كيوقف طباعة SQL الاعتيادي.

   1  
   2  "use strict";
   3  
   4  import {Sequelize} from "sequelize";
   5  import 'dotenv/config';
   6  
   7  const sequelize = new Sequelize({
   8  
   9      dialect: 'postgres',
  10      host: process.env.DB_HOST,
  11      port: Number(process.env.DB_PORT)||5432,
  12      database: process.env.DB_NAME,
  13      username: process.env.DB_USER,
  14      password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD,
  15  
  16      logging: false, 
  17  });
  18  
  19  export default sequelize;
  20

backend/src/config/jwt.ts

Access token بطاقة موقعة للاستعمال مع الطلبات. Refresh token سر عشوائي لتجديد الجلسة.

   1  import crypto from "crypto";
   2  import jwt from "jsonwebtoken";
   3  import { UserRole } from "../models/User";
   4  
   5  const JWT_SECRET: string =
   6    process.env.JWT_SECRET || "<LOCAL_SECRET_FALLBACK_REDACTED>";
   7  const JWT_EXPIRES_IN: string = process.env.JWT_EXPIRES_IN || "15m";
   8  
   9  // 7 days TTL in milliseconds
  10  export const REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_MS: number = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  11  
  12  export interface JwtPayload {
  13    userId: number;
  14    storeId: number;
  15    role: UserRole;
  16    email: string;
  17  }
  18  
  19  export function signAccessToken(payload: JwtPayload): string {
  20    return jwt.sign(payload, JWT_SECRET, {
  21      expiresIn: JWT_EXPIRES_IN as jwt.SignOptions["expiresIn"],
  22    });
  23  }
  24  
  25  export function verifyAccessToken(token: string): JwtPayload {
  26    return jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET) as JwtPayload;
  27  }
  28  
  29  export function generateRefreshToken(): string {
  30    return crypto.randomBytes(40).toString("hex");
  31  }
  32  
  33  export function hashToken(token: string): string {
  34    return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");
  35  }

backend/src/mcp/shipmentServer.ts

getShipmentStatusTool كيتأكد من صاحب الطلب قبل التواصل مع خدمة التتبع.

   1  import { createServer } from "node:http";
   2  import { Readable } from "node:stream";
   3  import { createMcpHandler, McpServer } from "@modelcontextprotocol/server";
   4  import { z } from "zod";
   5  
   6  const PORT = Number(process.env.MCP_PORT) || 4010;
   7  
   8  const handler = createMcpHandler(() => {
   9    const server = new McpServer({ name: "shopmate-shipment", version: "1.0.0" });
  10    server.registerTool(
  11      "getShipmentStatus",
  12      {
  13        description: "Retourne un statut transporteur de démonstration pour une commande déjà vérifiée par le backend ShopMate.",
  14        inputSchema: z.object({
  15          orderId: z.number().int().positive(),
  16          orderStatus: z.enum(["PENDING", "CONFIRMED", "SHIPPED", "DELIVERED", "CANCELLED"]),
  17        }),
  18      },
  19      async ({ orderId, orderStatus }) => {
  20        const statusByOrder: Record<string, { status: string; message: string; estimatedDelivery: string | null }> = {
  21          PENDING: { status: "En préparation", message: "La commande est enregistrée et en attente de préparation.", estimatedDelivery: null },
  22          CONFIRMED: { status: "En préparation", message: "La commande est confirmée et en cours de préparation.", estimatedDelivery: new Date(Date.now() + 2 * 86_400_000).toISOString() },
  23          SHIPPED: { status: "Expédiée", message: "Le colis a été remis au transporteur et est en cours d'acheminement.", estimatedDelivery: new Date(Date.now() + 86_400_000).toISOString() },
  24          DELIVERED: { status: "Livrée", message: "Le transporteur indique que le colis a été livré.", estimatedDelivery: null },
  25          CANCELLED: { status: "Annulée", message: "Cette commande a été annulée; aucun colis n'est en cours d'acheminement.", estimatedDelivery: null },
  26        };
  27        const tracking = statusByOrder[orderStatus];
  28        const result = {
  29          orderId,
  30          orderStatus: tracking.status,
  31          carrier: "ShopMate Express (simulation)",
  32          trackingNumber: `SM-${String(orderId).padStart(6, "0")}`,
  33          lastUpdate: new Date().toISOString(),
  34          estimatedDelivery: tracking.estimatedDelivery,
  35          message: tracking.message,
  36        };
  37        return { content: [{ type: "text", text: JSON.stringify(result) }] };
  38      }
  39    );
  40    return server;
  41  });
  42  
  43  const httpServer = createServer(async (request, response) => {
  44    try {
  45      const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
  46      for await (const chunk of request) chunks.push(Buffer.from(chunk));
  47      const body = Buffer.concat(chunks);
  48      const url = new URL(request.url ?? "/", `http://${request.headers.host ?? "127.0.0.1"}`);
  49      const webRequest = new Request(url, {
  50        method: request.method,
  51        headers: request.headers as HeadersInit,
  52        body: body.length > 0 ? body : undefined,
  53      });
  54      const webResponse = await handler.fetch(webRequest);
  55      response.writeHead(webResponse.status, Object.fromEntries(webResponse.headers.entries()));
  56      if (!webResponse.body) {
  57        response.end();
  58        return;
  59      }
  60      Readable.fromWeb(webResponse.body as import("node:stream/web").ReadableStream).pipe(response);
  61    } catch (error) {
  62      console.error("MCP request failed:", error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknown error");
  63      if (!response.headersSent) response.writeHead(500, { "content-type": "application/json" });
  64      response.end(JSON.stringify({ error: "MCP request failed" }));
  65    }
  66  });
  67  
  68  httpServer.listen(PORT, "0.0.0.0", () => {
  69    console.log(`ShopMate shipment MCP server listening on port ${PORT}`);
  70  });
  71  
  72  async function shutdown(): Promise<void> {
  73    await handler.close();
  74    httpServer.close(() => process.exit(0));
  75  }
  76  
  77  process.on("SIGINT", () => void shutdown());
  78  process.on("SIGTERM", () => void shutdown());
backend/src/middlewares/auth.ts

Middleware دالة كتدخل بين الطلب والتنفيذ. إما تكمل بـnext، وإما تجاوب وترجع.

   1  import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
   2  import { verifyAccessToken } from "../config/jwt";
   3  
   4  export function authenticate(
   5    req: Request,
   6    res: Response,
   7    next: NextFunction
   8  ): void {
   9    const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
  10  
  11    if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
  12      res.status(401).json({ error: "Authentication token required" });
  13      return;
  14    }
  15  
  16    const token = authHeader.substring(7).trim();
  17  
  18    try {
  19      const payload = verifyAccessToken(token);
  20      req.user = payload;
  21      next();
  22    } catch (error) {
  23      res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid or expired authentication token" });
  24    }
  25  }
  26  
  27
backend/src/middlewares/errorHandler.ts

كيحول الأخطاء لأجوبة HTTP: JSON خاطئ400، duplicate409، JWT401، و500 عام. console logging هنا منفصل عن sanitizeLogs.

   1  import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
   2  import { ZodError } from "zod";
   3  
   4  export function errorHandler(
   5    err: any,
   6    _req: Request,
   7    res: Response,
   8    _next: NextFunction
   9  ): void {
  10    // Malformed JSON payload
  11    if (err instanceof SyntaxError && "body" in err) {
  12      res.status(400).json({ error: "Malformed JSON body" });
  13      return;
  14    }
  15  
  16    // Zod validation error
  17    if (err instanceof ZodError) {
  18      res.status(400).json({
  19        error: "Validation failed",
  20        details: err.flatten().fieldErrors,
  21      });
  22      return;
  23    }
  24  
  25    // Sequelize Unique Constraint error
  26    if (err.name === "SequelizeUniqueConstraintError") {
  27      res.status(409).json({ error: "Resource conflict: duplicate entry" });
  28      return;
  29    }
  30  
  31    // Sequelize Validation error
  32    if (err.name === "SequelizeValidationError") {
  33      res.status(400).json({ error: err.message || "Database validation failed" });
  34      return;
  35    }
  36  
  37    // JWT errors
  38    if (err.name === "JsonWebTokenError" || err.name === "TokenExpiredError") {
  39      res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid or expired authentication token" });
  40      return;
  41    }
  42  
  43    // Explicit status code on error
  44    const statusCode = err.status || err.statusCode || 500;
  45    const message = statusCode < 500 ? err.message : "Internal server error";
  46  
  47    // Safe logging: log error message without sensitive user data
  48    console.error("Unhandled error:", err.message || err);
  49  
  50    res.status(statusCode).json({ error: message });
  51  }
backend/src/middlewares/index.ts

Barrel exports: كيجمع تصدير middlewares باش imports فالمسارات يكونو مختصرين.

   1  export * from "./auth";
   2  export * from "./tenant";
   3  export * from "./roles";
   4  export * from "./validate";
   5  export * from "./errorHandler";
   6  export * from "./rateLimiter";
   7  export * from "./sanitizeLogs";
backend/src/middlewares/rateLimiter.ts

حد عام افتراضي100 طلب/15 دقيقة، وحد AI20/دقيقة حسب userId. default memory store ماشي عداد موزع.

   1  import { rateLimit } from "express-rate-limit";
   2  
   3  const windowMs = 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 minutes
   4  const maxRequests = Number(process.env.RATE_LIMIT_MAX) || 100;
   5  
   6  export const globalRateLimiter = rateLimit({
   7    windowMs,
   8    limit: maxRequests,
   9    statusCode: 429,
  10    standardHeaders: true, // Return standard RateLimit-* headers
  11    legacyHeaders: false, // Disable X-RateLimit-* headers
  12    message: {
  13      error: "Too many requests, please try again later.",
  14    },
  15  });
  16  
  17  // Helper for testing or custom strict route rate limits
  18  export function createRateLimiter(options: {
  19    windowMs: number;
  20    limit: number;
  21    message?: string;
  22  }) {
  23    return rateLimit({
  24      windowMs: options.windowMs,
  25      limit: options.limit,
  26      statusCode: 429,
  27      standardHeaders: true,
  28      legacyHeaders: false,
  29      message: {
  30        error: options.message || "Too many requests, please try again later.",
  31      },
  32    });
  33  }
  34  
  35  /**
  36   * AI Agent Rate Limiter: max 20 messages per minute per user (or IP if unauthenticated).
  37   * Enforces the ShopMate security rule: 20 agent msg/min/user.
  38   */
  39  export const agentRateLimiter = rateLimit({
  40    windowMs: 60 * 1000, // 1 minute
  41    limit: 20,
  42    statusCode: 429,
  43    standardHeaders: true,
  44    legacyHeaders: false,
  45    keyGenerator: (req) => {
  46      return (req as any).user?.userId
  47        ? `user_${(req as any).user.userId}`
  48        : req.ip || "unknown";
  49    },
  50    message: {
  51      error: "Trop de requêtes vers l'assistant IA (max 20 messages / minute).",
  52    },
  53  });
backend/src/middlewares/roles.ts

Middleware دالة كتدخل بين الطلب والتنفيذ. إما تكمل بـnext، وإما تجاوب وترجع.

   1  import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
   2  import { UserRole } from "../models/User";
   3  
   4  export function requireRole(...allowedRoles: UserRole[]) {
   5    return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
   6      if (!req.user) {
   7        res.status(401).json({ error: "Authentication required" });
   8        return;
   9      }
  10  
  11      if (!allowedRoles.includes(req.user.role)) {
  12        res.status(403).json({ error: "Forbidden: insufficient permissions" });
  13        return;
  14      }
  15  
  16      next();
  17    };
  18  }
backend/src/middlewares/sanitizeLogs.ts

السجل مخصص لتتبع دور المساعد، والرسائل مخصصة لتاريخ الحوار.

   1  const SENSITIVE_KEYS = new Set([
   2    "password",
   3    "passwordhash",
   4    "passwd",
   5    "passphrase",
   6    "token",
   7    "accesstoken",
   8    "refreshtoken",
   9    "authorization",
  10    "cookie",
  11    "apikey",
  12    "secret",
  13    "jwtsecret",
  14    "seedadminpassword",
  15    "privatekey",
  16  ]);
  17  
  18  export function sanitizeForLogging(data: unknown): unknown {
  19    if (data === null || data === undefined) {
  20      return data;
  21    }
  22  
  23    if (typeof data === "string") {
  24      return data
  25        .replace(/\bBearer\s+[^\s"'<>]+/gi, "Bearer [REDACTED]")
  26        .replace(/\b(?:sk|rk|pk)-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\b/g, "[REDACTED_API_KEY]")
  27        .replace(/\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\b/g, "[REDACTED_JWT]")
  28        .replace(/\b[a-f0-9]{64,}\b/gi, "[REDACTED_OPAQUE_TOKEN]")
  29        .replace(/((?:api[_ -]?key|access[_ -]?token|refresh[_ -]?token|password|passwd|secret|authorization)(?:\s+is\s+|\s*[:=]\s*))("[^"]*"|'[^']*'|[^\s,;]+)/gi, "$1[REDACTED]");
  30    }
  31  
  32    if (Array.isArray(data)) {
  33      return data.map((item) => sanitizeForLogging(item));
  34    }
  35  
  36    if (typeof data === "object") {
  37      const sanitized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  38  
  39      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(data as Record<string, unknown>)) {
  40        const normalizedKey = key.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
  41        const isTokenMetric = ["tokenusage", "prompttokens", "completiontokens", "totaltokens"].includes(normalizedKey);
  42        const isSensitiveKey = SENSITIVE_KEYS.has(normalizedKey) ||
  43          /password|passwd|passphrase|token|secret|apikey|authorization|cookie|privatekey/.test(normalizedKey);
  44        if (isSensitiveKey && !isTokenMetric) {
  45          sanitized[key] = "[REDACTED]";
  46        } else {
  47          sanitized[key] = sanitizeForLogging(value);
  48        }
  49      }
  50  
  51      return sanitized;
  52    }
  53  
  54    return data;
  55  }
backend/src/middlewares/tenant.ts

Middleware دالة كتدخل بين الطلب والتنفيذ. إما تكمل بـnext، وإما تجاوب وترجع.

   1  import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
   2  import { verifyAccessToken } from "../config/jwt";
   3  import { Store } from "../models";
   4  import { DEMO_STORE_NAME } from "../config/demo";
   5  
   6  export function injectTenant(
   7    req: Request,
   8    res: Response,
   9    next: NextFunction
  10  ): void {
  11    if (!req.user || typeof req.user.storeId !== "number") {
  12      res.status(401).json({ error: "User authentication required for tenant access" });
  13      return;
  14    }
  15  
  16    // Inject storeId server-side strictly from the verified JWT token
  17    req.storeId = req.user.storeId;
  18  
  19    // Enforce rule: client-sent storeId is ignored and overwritten
  20    if (req.body && typeof req.body === "object") {
  21      req.body.storeId = req.storeId;
  22    }
  23  
  24    if (req.query && typeof req.query === "object") {
  25      delete (req.query as Record<string, unknown>).storeId;
  26    }
  27  
  28    next();
  29  }
  30  
  31  /**
  32   * Resolves the tenant storeId for public or semi-public store catalog endpoints:
  33   * 1. If valid JWT provided: scopes strictly to user's storeId from token
  34   * 2. If unauthenticated: defaults safely to DEMO_STORE_NAME
  35   * Never accepts client-sent storeId.
  36   */
  37  export async function resolveStoreTenant(
  38    req: Request,
  39    res: Response,
  40    next: NextFunction
  41  ): Promise<void> {
  42    // Strip any client-sent storeId in query or body immediately
  43    if (req.body && typeof req.body === "object") {
  44      delete (req.body as Record<string, unknown>).storeId;
  45    }
  46    if (req.query && typeof req.query === "object") {
  47      delete (req.query as Record<string, unknown>).storeId;
  48    }
  49  
  50    // 1. If user already authenticated
  51    if (req.user && typeof req.user.storeId === "number") {
  52      req.storeId = req.user.storeId;
  53      return next();
  54    }
  55  
  56    // 2. Check if Authorization header is present
  57    const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
  58    if (authHeader && authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
  59      try {
  60        const token = authHeader.substring(7).trim();
  61        const payload = verifyAccessToken(token);
  62        req.user = payload;
  63        req.storeId = payload.storeId;
  64        return next();
  65      } catch {
  66        // If token is explicitly malformed/expired, reject with 401
  67        res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid or expired authentication token" });
  68        return;
  69      }
  70    }
  71  
  72    // 3. Fallback to default demo store for public catalog browsing
  73    try {
  74      const demoStore = await Store.findOne({ where: { name: DEMO_STORE_NAME } });
  75      if (!demoStore) {
  76        res.status(404).json({ error: "Demo store is not initialized" });
  77        return;
  78      }
  79  
  80      req.storeId = demoStore.id;
  81      next();
  82    } catch (err) {
  83      next(err);
  84    }
  85  }
backend/src/middlewares/validate.ts

الطلب الخارجي ما نثقوش فالشكل ديالو. Zod كيرجع بيانات مقبولة أو أخطاء قبل الوصول للخدمة.

   1  import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
   2  import { ZodTypeAny } from "zod";
   3  
   4  export function validate(
   5    schema: ZodTypeAny,
   6    source: "body" | "query" | "params" = "body"
   7  ) {
   8    return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
   9      const parseResult = schema.safeParse(req[source]);
  10  
  11      if (!parseResult.success) {
  12        res.status(400).json({
  13          error: "Validation failed",
  14          details: parseResult.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
  15        });
  16        return;
  17      }
  18  
  19      // Replace with typed, sanitized data
  20      // Express 5 makes req.query and req.params getter-only properties on the prototype
  21      if (source === "body") {
  22        req.body = parseResult.data;
  23      } else {
  24        Object.defineProperty(req, source, {
  25          value: parseResult.data,
  26          writable: true,
  27          configurable: true,
  28          enumerable: true,
  29        });
  30      }
  31  
  32      next();
  33    };
  34  }

backend/src/models/Document.ts

metadata ديال الوثيقة: الاسم، uploader، mimeType، status، errorMessage وfileUrl وصفي.

   1  
   2  import { DataTypes, Model, Optional } from "sequelize";
   3  import sequelize from "../config/database";
   4  
   5  type DocumentStatus = "pending" | "processing" | "completed" | "failed";
   6  
   7  interface DocumentData {
   8    id: number;
   9    storeId: number;
  10    uploadedBy: number;
  11    name: string;
  12    fileUrl: string;
  13    mimeType: string;

  18  }

  26  > {

  28    declare storeId: number;

  30    declare name: string;
  31    declare fileUrl: string;
  32    declare mimeType: string;

  45      },
  46      storeId: {
  47        type: DataTypes.INTEGER,
  48        allowNull: false,
  49      },

  52        allowNull: false,
  53      },
  54      name: {
  55        type: DataTypes.STRING,
  56        allowNull: false,
  57      },
  58      fileUrl: {
  59        type: DataTypes.TEXT,
  60        allowNull: false,
  61      },
  62      mimeType: {
  63        type: DataTypes.STRING,
  64        allowNull: false,
  65      },
  66      status: {
  67        type: DataTypes.ENUM(
  68          "pending",
  69          "processing",

  71          "failed"
  72        ),
  73        allowNull: false,
  74        defaultValue: "pending",
  75      },
  76      errorMessage: {
  77        type: DataTypes.STRING,
  78        allowNull: true,
  79      },
  80    },
  81    {
  82      sequelize,
  83      tableName: "documents",
  84      timestamps: true,
  85      indexes: [
  86        {
  87          fields: ["storeId"],
  88        },
  89        {
  90          fields: ["storeId", "status"],
  91        },
  92      ],
  93    }
  94  );
  95  
  96  export default Document;

   1  
   2  import { DataTypes, Model, Optional } from "sequelize";
   3  import sequelize from "../config/database";
   4  
   5  type MessageRole = "user" | "assistant" | "system";
   6  
   7  interface MessageData {
   8    id: number;
   9    storeId: number;
  10    conversationId: number;
  11    role: MessageRole;
  12    content: string;
  13    createdAt?: Date;
  14    updatedAt?: Date;
  15  }
  16  
  17  class Message extends Model<
  18    MessageData,
  19    Optional<MessageData, "id">
  20  > {
  21    declare id: number;
  22    declare storeId: number;
  23    declare conversationId: number;
  24    declare role: MessageRole;
  25    declare content: string;
  26    declare readonly createdAt: Date;
  27    declare readonly updatedAt: Date;
  28  }
  29  
  30  Message.init(
  31    {
  32      id: {
  33        type: DataTypes.INTEGER,
  34        autoIncrement: true,
  35        primaryKey: true,
  36      },
  37      storeId: {
  38        type: DataTypes.INTEGER,
  39        allowNull: false,
  40      },
  41      conversationId: {
  42        type: DataTypes.INTEGER,
  43        allowNull: false,
  44      },
  45      role: {
  46        type: DataTypes.ENUM("user", "assistant", "system"),
  47        allowNull: false,
  48      },
  49      content: {
  50        type: DataTypes.TEXT,
  51        allowNull: false,
  52      },
  53    },
  54    {
  55      sequelize,
  56      tableName: "messages",
  57      timestamps: true,
  58      indexes: [
  59        {
  60          fields: ["storeId", "conversationId", "createdAt"],
  61        },
  62      ],
  63    }
  64  );
  65  
  66  export default Message;

رأس الطلب: storeId وcustomerId والحالة والمجموع. ما فيهش عمود عنوان الشحن فهاد النسخة.

   1  import { DataTypes, Model, Optional } from "sequelize";
   2  import sequelize from "../config/database";
   3  
   4  export type OrderStatus =
   5    | "PENDING"
   6    | "CONFIRMED"
   7    | "SHIPPED"
   8    | "DELIVERED"
   9    | "CANCELLED";
  10  
  11  export interface OrderData {
  12    id: number;
  13    storeId: number;
  14    customerId: number;
  15    status: OrderStatus | string;
  16    total: number | string;
  17    createdAt?: Date;
  18    updatedAt?: Date;
  19  }
  20  
  21  class Order extends Model<OrderData, Optional<OrderData, "id" | "status" | "total">> {
  22    declare id: number;
  23    declare storeId: number;
  24    declare customerId: number;
  25    declare status: OrderStatus | string;
  26    declare total: number | string;
  27    declare readonly createdAt: Date;
  28    declare readonly updatedAt: Date;
  29  }
  30  
  31  Order.init(
  32    {
  33      id: {
  34        type: DataTypes.INTEGER,
  35        autoIncrement: true,
  36        primaryKey: true,
  37      },
  38      storeId: {
  39        type: DataTypes.INTEGER,
  40        allowNull: false,
  41      },
  42      customerId: {
  43        type: DataTypes.INTEGER,
  44        allowNull: false,
  45      },
  46      status: {
  47        type: DataTypes.STRING,
  48        allowNull: false,
  49        defaultValue: "PENDING",
  50      },
  51      total: {
  52        type: DataTypes.DECIMAL(10, 2),
  53        allowNull: false,
  54        defaultValue: 0,
  55      },
  56    },
  57    {
  58      sequelize,
  59      tableName: "orders",
  60      timestamps: true,
  61      indexes: [
  62        {
  63          fields: ["storeId"],
  64        },
  65        {
  66          fields: ["customerId"],
  67        },
  68        {
  69          fields: ["storeId", "status"],
  70        },
  71      ],
  72    }
  73  );
  74  
  75  export default Order;

   1

  23    declare orderId: number;
  24    declare customerId: number;
  25    declare productId: number;

backend/src/models/tenantScope.ts

Model كيوصف التعامل مع سجل فالكود. Migration كتبدل بنية قاعدة البيانات. Association كتربط النماذج ببعضياتها.

   1  import type { Model, ModelStatic } from "sequelize";
   2  
   3  type TenantModel = ModelStatic<Model>;
   4  
   5  function validateStoreId(storeId: number): number {
   6    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(storeId) || storeId < 1) {
   7      throw new TypeError("A valid storeId is required for tenant queries");
   8    }
   9  
  10    return storeId;
  11  }
  12  
  13  export function addStoreScope(model: TenantModel): void {
  14    model.addScope(
  15      "forStore",
  16      (storeId: number) => ({ where: { storeId: validateStoreId(storeId) } }),
  17      { override: true }
  18    );
  19  }
  20  
  21  export function forStore(model: TenantModel, storeId: number): TenantModel {
  22    return model.scope({ method: ["forStore", validateStoreId(storeId)] });
  23  }
backend/src/models/User.ts

حساب الدخول: الاسم والبريد وhash كلمة السر والدور. uniqueness ديال البريد داخل المتجر، ماشي ضروري عبر جميع المتاجر.

   1  import { DataTypes, Model, Optional } from "sequelize";
   2  import sequelize from "../config/database";
   3  
   4  export type UserRole = "ADMIN" | "CUSTOMER";
   5  
   6  interface UserData {
   7    id: number;
   8    storeId: number;
   9    name: string;
  10    email: string;
  11    password: string;
  12    role: UserRole;
  13    createdAt?: Date;
  14    updatedAt?: Date;
  15  }
  16  
  17  class User
  18    extends Model<UserData, Optional<UserData, "id" | "role">>
  19  {
  20    declare id: number;
  21    declare storeId: number;
  22    declare name: string;
  23    declare email: string;
  24    declare password: string;
  25    declare role: UserRole;
  26    declare readonly createdAt: Date;
  27    declare readonly updatedAt: Date;
  28  }
  29  
  30  User.init(
  31    {
  32      id: {
  33        type: DataTypes.INTEGER,
  34        autoIncrement: true,
  35        primaryKey: true,
  36      },
  37  
  38      storeId: {
  39        type: DataTypes.INTEGER,
  40        allowNull: false,
  41      },
  42  
  43      name: {
  44        type: DataTypes.STRING,
  45        allowNull: false,
  46      },
  47  
  48      email: {
  49        type: DataTypes.STRING,
  50        allowNull: false,
  51      },
  52  
  53      password: {
  54        type: DataTypes.STRING,
  55        allowNull: false,
  56      },
  57  
  58      role: {
  59        type: DataTypes.STRING,
  60        allowNull: false,
  61        defaultValue: "CUSTOMER",
  62      },
  63    },
  64    {
  65      sequelize,
  66      tableName: "users",
  67      timestamps: true,
  68  
  69      indexes: [
  70        {
  71          unique: true,
  72          fields: ["storeId", "email"],
  73          name: "users_storeId_email_unique",
  74        },
  75      ],
  76    }
  77  );
  78  
  79  export default User;
backend/src/routes/agent.ts

هاد route هو المنظم: هوية → محادثة → حمايات → مصادر → نموذج وأدوات → جواب وسجل.

   1  import { Router, Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
   2  import { AgentLog, Conversation, Message, forStore } from "../models";
   3  import {
   4    authenticate,
   5    injectTenant,
   6    requireRole,
   7    validate,
   8    agentRateLimiter,
   9  } from "../middlewares";
  10  import { retrieveContext } from "../services/rag";
  11  import {
  12    streamChat,
  13    buildSystemPrompt,
  14    getLlmClient,
  15    LLM_MODEL,
  16    MAX_TOKENS_PER_TURN,
  17    type LLMMessage,
  18  } from "../services/llm";
  19  import {
  20    AGENT_TOOLS,
  21    executeToolCall,
  22    canCallAnotherTool,
  23    validateToolCall,
  24    type ToolExecutionContext,
  25  } from "../services/agentTools";
  26  import {
  27    hasPendingConfirmation,
  28    executeConfirmedAction,
  29    getPendingConfirmation,
  30    isAffirmativeConfirmation,
  31  } from "../services/orderConfirmation";
  32  import {
  33    checkPromptInjection,
  34    checkProhibitedIntent,
  35    sanitizeContextData,
  36    moderateAgentOutput,
  37    STANDARD_REFUSALS,
  38  } from "../services/guardrails";
  39  import { z } from "zod";
  40  import { Op } from "sequelize";
  41  import { sanitizeForLogging } from "../middlewares/sanitizeLogs";
  42  import AgentLogRecord from "../models/AgentLog";
  43  import { notifyManagerRequest } from "../services/n8nNotifications";
  44  
  45  const agentRouter = Router();
  46  
  47  agentRouter.use(authenticate, injectTenant);
  48  
  49  const chatMessageSchema = z.object({
  50    message: z.string().trim().min(1).max(2000),
  51    conversationId: z.coerce.number().int().positive().optional(),
  52  });
  53  
  54  const logDateSchema = z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).refine((value) => {
  55    const parsed = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00.000Z`);
  56    return !Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) && parsed.toISOString().slice(0, 10) === value;
  57  }, "Date must be a valid calendar date");
  58  
  59  const agentLogsQuerySchema = z.object({
  60    page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  61    limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(25),
  62    conversationId: z.coerce.number().int().positive().optional(),
  63    customerId: z.coerce.number().int().positive().optional(),
  64    fromDate: logDateSchema.optional(),
  65    toDate: logDateSchema.optional(),
  66    disposition: z.enum(["refused", "blocked"]).optional(),
  67    action: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100).optional(),
  68    status: z.enum(["success", "error"]).optional(),
  69  });
  70  
  71  // ─── POST /api/agent/chat ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  72  /**
  73   * ShopMate AI chat endpoint.
  74   *
  75   * Supports two response modes driven by the Accept header:
  76   *   - text/event-stream  → SSE (real-time streaming, token by token)
  77   *   - application/json   → single JSON response (Postman / testing)
  78   *
  79   * Agentic pipeline per turn:
  80   *   1.  Get / create Conversation
  81   *   2.  Persist user Message
  82   *   2.5 Short-circuit: if "Oui" + pending confirmation → execute + respond
  83   *   3.  Retrieve RAG context (storeId-scoped, never cross-tenant)
  84   *   4.  Build message history: system prompt + history + RAG context + user message
  85   *   5.  Tool loop: LLM → tool_call → execute → LLM (max MAX_TOOL_CALLS_PER_TURN)
  86   *   6.  Stream final answer token by token
  87   *   7.  Persist assistant Message + AgentLog audit entry
  88   *
  89   * SSE event types emitted:
  90   *   { type: "start",        conversationId }
  91   *   { type: "source",       sources: [{name, score}] }
  92   *   { type: "tool_call",    tool, args }
  93   *   { type: "tool_result",  tool, result }
  94   *   { type: "token",        token }
  95   *   { type: "confirmation", confirmed, orderId? }
  96   *   { type: "done",         conversationId, messageId, ragUsed, sourcesUsed, toolsUsed }
  97   *   { type: "error",        message }
  98   */
  99  agentRouter.post(
 100    "/chat",
 101    agentRateLimiter,
 102    validate(chatMessageSchema, "body"),
 103    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 104      const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 105      const user = req.user!;
 106      const { message: userText, conversationId } = req.body as {
 107        message: string;
 108        conversationId?: number;
 109      };
 110  
 111      const isSSE = (req.headers.accept ?? "").includes("text/event-stream");
 112      const turnStartedAt = Date.now();
 113      const toolsCalled: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
 114      let ragSources: Array<{ name: string; score: number }> = [];
 115      let tokenUsage: { promptTokens: number; completionTokens: number; totalTokens: number } | null = null;
 116      let refusalCategory: string | null = null;
 117      let traceMessageId: number | null = null;
 118      let conversation: Conversation | null = null;
 119  
 120      const logText = (value: unknown): string => {
 121        const sanitized = sanitizeForLogging(value);
 122        return typeof sanitized === "string" ? sanitized : JSON.stringify(sanitized);
 123      };
 124      const recordTurn = async (entry: {
 125        action: string;
 126        status: "success" | "error";
 127        output?: unknown;
 128        errorMessage?: unknown;
 129      }): Promise<void> => {
 130        await AgentLog.create({
 131          storeId,
 132          agentName: logText(process.env.LLM_MODEL ?? LLM_MODEL),
 133          action: entry.action,
 134          status: entry.status,
 135          conversationId: conversation?.id ?? null,
 136          messageId: traceMessageId,
 137          input: logText(userText).slice(0, 4000),
 138          output: entry.output === undefined ? null : logText(entry.output).slice(0, 4000),
 139          errorMessage: entry.errorMessage === undefined ? null : logText(entry.errorMessage).slice(0, 2000),
 140          toolsCalled: sanitizeForLogging(toolsCalled) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>,
 141          ragSources: sanitizeForLogging(ragSources) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>,
 142          tokenUsage,
 143          latencyMs: Date.now() - turnStartedAt,
 144          refusalCategory,
 145        });
 146      };
 147      const addTokenUsage = (usage: { promptTokens: number; completionTokens: number; totalTokens: number }): void => {
 148        tokenUsage = {
 149          promptTokens: (tokenUsage?.promptTokens ?? 0) + usage.promptTokens,
 150          completionTokens: (tokenUsage?.completionTokens ?? 0) + usage.completionTokens,
 151          totalTokens: (tokenUsage?.totalTokens ?? 0) + usage.totalTokens,
 152        };
 153      };
 154  
 155      // ── Client disconnect guard ───────────────────────────────────────────────
 156      const abortController = new AbortController();
 157      let clientConnected = true;
 158      res.on("close", () => {
 159        if (!res.writableEnded) {
 160          clientConnected = false;
 161          abortController.abort();
 162        }
 163      });
 164  
 165      /** Write a JSON SSE event only when client is still connected. */
 166      const safeSend = (data: object): void => {
 167        if (isSSE && clientConnected && !res.writableEnded) {
 168          res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`);
 169        }
 170      };
 171  
 172      try {
 173        // ── 1. Get or create Conversation ────────────────────────────────────────
 174        if (conversationId) {
 175          conversation = (await forStore(Conversation, storeId).findOne({
 176            where: { id: conversationId, userId: user.userId },
 177          })) as Conversation | null;
 178        }
 179        if (!conversation) {
 180          conversation = await Conversation.create({
 181            storeId,
 182            userId: user.userId,
 183            title: userText.slice(0, 40),
 184            status: "active",
 185          });
 186        }
 187  
 188        // ── 2. Persist user Message & bump conversation timestamp ────────────────
 189        const userMessage = await Message.create({
 190          storeId,
 191          conversationId: conversation.id,
 192          role: "user",
 193          content: userText,
 194        });
 195        await conversation.update({ updatedAt: new Date() });
 196  
 197        // ── 2.6 Guardrails: Prompt Injection Detection & Systematic Refusals ─────
 198        const activeUserText = userText;
 199        const injectionCheck = checkPromptInjection(userText);
 200        if (injectionCheck.isSuspicious) {
 201          // Keep the original in the audit log, but never replay hostile text as
 202          // trusted conversation history on the next turn.
 203          await userMessage.update({
 204            content: "[Message masqué : tentative de contournement des règles détectée.]",
 205          });
 206          refusalCategory = "prompt_injection";
 207          const replyMessage = STANDARD_REFUSALS.promptInjection;
 208          const assistantMsg = await Message.create({
 209            storeId,
 210            conversationId: conversation.id,
 211            role: "assistant",
 212            content: replyMessage,
 213          });
 214          traceMessageId = assistantMsg.id;
 215          await recordTurn({ action: "prompt_injection_flagged", status: "error", output: replyMessage });
 216          if (isSSE) {
 217            res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
 218            res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
 219            res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
 220            res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
 221            res.flushHeaders();
 222            safeSend({ type: "start", conversationId: conversation.id });
 223            safeSend({ type: "token", token: replyMessage });
 224            safeSend({ type: "done", conversationId: conversation.id, messageId: assistantMsg.id, ragUsed: false, sourcesUsed: [], toolsUsed: [] });
 225            return res.end();
 226          }
 227          return res.json({ conversationId: conversation.id, messageId: assistantMsg.id, response: replyMessage, ragUsed: false, sourcesUsed: [], toolsUsed: [] });
 228        }
 229  
 230        // Systematic refusals (discount negotiations, refunds, cross-tenant data, medical/legal)
 231        const refusalCheck = checkProhibitedIntent(userText);
 232        if (refusalCheck.shouldRefuse && refusalCheck.refusalMessage) {
 233          refusalCategory = refusalCheck.category ?? "prohibited_request";
 234          const replyMessage = refusalCheck.refusalMessage;
 235          if (refusalCheck.category === "discount" || refusalCheck.category === "refund") {
 236            // This is a notification only; the manager decides whether to act.
 237            void notifyManagerRequest({
 238              storeId,
 239              customer: { userId: user.userId, email: user.email },
 240              category: refusalCheck.category,
 241              message: userText,
 242            });
 243          }
 244  
 245          const assistantMsg = await Message.create({
 246            storeId,
 247            conversationId: conversation.id,
 248            role: "assistant",
 249            content: replyMessage,
 250          });
 251  
 252          traceMessageId = assistantMsg.id;
 253          await recordTurn({ action: `refusal_${refusalCheck.category}`, status: "success", output: replyMessage });
 254  
 255          if (isSSE) {
 256            res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
 257            res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
 258            res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
 259            res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
 260            res.flushHeaders();
 261            safeSend({ type: "start", conversationId: conversation.id });
 262            safeSend({ type: "token", token: replyMessage });
 263            safeSend({
 264              type: "done",
 265              conversationId: conversation.id,
 266              messageId: assistantMsg.id,
 267              ragUsed: false,
 268              sourcesUsed: [],
 269              toolsUsed: [],
 270            });
 271            return res.end();
 272          }
 273  
 274          return res.json({
 275            conversationId: conversation.id,
 276            messageId: assistantMsg.id,
 277            response: replyMessage,
 278            ragUsed: false,
 279            sourcesUsed: [],
 280            toolsUsed: [],
 281          });
 282        }
 283  
 284        // A prohibited or adversarial message must never confirm an earlier write.
 285        if (isAffirmativeConfirmation(userText) && hasPendingConfirmation(conversation.id)) {
 286          const pendingAction = getPendingConfirmation(conversation.id);
 287          if (pendingAction) {
 288            toolsCalled.push({
 289              name: pendingAction.action,
 290              args: sanitizeForLogging(pendingAction.args),
 291              validationPassed: true,
 292              source: "explicit_customer_confirmation",
 293            });
 294          }
 295          const executionResult = await executeConfirmedAction(conversation.id, {
 296            storeId,
 297            userId: user.userId,
 298            email: user.email,
 299            conversationId: conversation.id,
 300          });
 301          const replyMessage = executionResult.message || (executionResult.success
 302            ? "✅ Votre commande a été validée avec succès !"
 303            : `❌ ${executionResult.error}`);
 304          const assistantMsg = await Message.create({
 305            storeId,
 306            conversationId: conversation.id,
 307            role: "assistant",
 308            content: replyMessage,
 309          });
 310          traceMessageId = assistantMsg.id;
 311          await recordTurn({ action: "order_confirmation", status: executionResult.success ? "success" : "error", output: replyMessage });
 312          if (isSSE) {
 313            res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
 314            res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
 315            res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
 316            res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
 317            res.flushHeaders();
 318            safeSend({ type: "start", conversationId: conversation.id });
 319            safeSend({ type: "confirmation", confirmed: executionResult.success, orderId: executionResult.orderId });
 320            safeSend({ type: "token", token: replyMessage });
 321            safeSend({ type: "done", conversationId: conversation.id, messageId: assistantMsg.id, orderConfirmed: true, orderId: executionResult.orderId });
 322            return res.end();
 323          }
 324          return res.json({ conversationId: conversation.id, messageId: assistantMsg.id, response: replyMessage, orderConfirmed: true, orderId: executionResult.orderId });
 325        }
 326  
 327        // ── 3. RAG context (storeId-scoped, injected as data not instructions) ────
 328        const retrievedChunks = await retrieveContext(activeUserText, storeId);
 329        const ragChunks = retrievedChunks.filter((chunk) => {
 330          const suspicious = checkPromptInjection(`${chunk.source}\n${chunk.content}`).isSuspicious;
 331          return !suspicious;
 332        });
 333        ragSources = retrievedChunks.map((chunk) => ({
 334          name: chunk.source,
 335          score: Number(chunk.score),
 336          omitted: !ragChunks.some((safeChunk) => safeChunk.chunkId === chunk.chunkId),
 337        }));
 338  
 339        // ── 4. Build initial message history ─────────────────────────────────────
 340        const history = await Message.findAll({
 341          where: { conversationId: conversation.id },
 342          order: [["createdAt", "ASC"]],
 343          limit: 10,
 344          attributes: ["role", "content"],
 345        });
 346  
 347        const initialMessages: LLMMessage[] = [
 348          // 1. System prompt: role, language, guardrails, refusal rules
 349          { role: "system", content: buildSystemPrompt("ShopMate") },
 350          // 2. Conversation history (all turns except the just-added user message)
 351          ...history.slice(0, -1).map((m) => ({
 352            role: m.role as "user" | "assistant",
 353            content: m.content,
 354          })),
 355          // 3. RAG context — injected as DATA, never as instructions (prompt injection guard)
 356          ...(ragChunks.length > 0
 357            ? [{
 358              role: "system" as const,
 359              content:
 360                `<context>\n` +
 361                ragChunks
 362                  .map((c) => `[${sanitizeContextData(c.source)}] (similarité: ${(c.score * 100).toFixed(0)}%)\n${sanitizeContextData(c.content)}`)
 363                  .join("\n\n---\n\n") +
 364                `\n</context>`,
 365            }]
 366            : [{
 367              role: "system" as const,
 368              content: `<context>\nAucun document pertinent trouvé dans la base documentaire pour cette requête (similarité sous le seuil).\n</context>`,
 369            }]),
 370          // 4. Latest user message
 371          { role: "user", content: activeUserText },
 372        ];
 373  
 374        // ── 5. Tool execution context (storeId + userId ALWAYS from JWT) ──────────
 375        const toolContext: ToolExecutionContext = {
 376          storeId,
 377          userId: user.userId,
 378          email: user.email,
 379          conversationId: conversation.id,
 380        };
 381  
 382        // ── 6. SSE mode ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 383        if (isSSE) {
 384          res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
 385          res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
 386          res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
 387          res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
 388          res.flushHeaders();
 389  
 390          safeSend({ type: "start", conversationId: conversation.id });
 391  
 392          // Emit source event immediately so the client knows what docs were used
 393          if (ragChunks.length > 0) {
 394            safeSend({
 395              type: "source",
 396              sources: ragChunks.map((c) => ({
 397                name: c.source,
 398                score: parseFloat((c.score * 100).toFixed(1)),
 399              })),
 400            });
 401          }
 402  
 403          // ── Agentic tool loop state ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 404          let toolCallCount = 0;
 405          const allToolsUsed: string[] = [];
 406          let currentMessages: LLMMessage[] = [...initialMessages];
 407          let toolCallsDetectedThisTurn = false;
 408          let fullResponse = "";
 409  
 410          /**
 411           * One recursive streaming call.
 412           * - If the LLM responds with tool calls: onToolCalls executes them,
 413           *   updates currentMessages, then onDone calls runStreamTurn() again.
 414           * - If the LLM responds with text: onDone persists and closes the stream.
 415           */
 416          const runStreamTurn = async (): Promise<void> => {
 417            toolCallsDetectedThisTurn = false;
 418  
 419            await streamChat(
 420              currentMessages,
 421              canCallAnotherTool(toolCallCount) ? AGENT_TOOLS : undefined,
 422              {
 423                onToken: (token) => {
 424                  // Filter internal tool-call chunk markers — not meant for the client
 425                  if (!token.startsWith("\n__TOOL_CALL__:")) {
 426                    // Buffer until the complete answer passes output moderation.
 427                    fullResponse += token;
 428                  }
 429                },
 430                onUsage: addTokenUsage,
 431  
 432                // ── Tool call interception ─────────────────────────────────────────
 433                onToolCalls: async (toolCalls) => {
 434                  toolCallsDetectedThisTurn = true;
 435  
 436                  // Append the assistant's tool-call message to history
 437                  currentMessages.push({
 438                    role: "assistant",
 439                    content: null,
 440                    tool_calls: toolCalls,
 441                  } as any);
 442  
 443                  // Execute each tool (Zod-validated, storeId from JWT — never from model)
 444                  for (const tc of toolCalls) {
 445                    if (!canCallAnotherTool(toolCallCount)) break;
 446  
 447                    const toolName = tc.function.name;
 448                    let parsedArgs: any = {};
 449                    try { parsedArgs = tc.function.arguments ? JSON.parse(tc.function.arguments) : {}; } catch { }
 450  
 451                    // Emit tool_call event BEFORE execution so client can show "thinking…"
 452                    safeSend({ type: "tool_call", tool: toolName, args: parsedArgs });
 453  
 454                    const validation = validateToolCall(toolName, tc.function.arguments, toolContext);
 455                    const result = await executeToolCall(toolName, tc.function.arguments, toolContext);
 456                    toolsCalled.push({
 457                      name: toolName,
 458                      args: validation.success ? sanitizeForLogging(validation.args) : null,
 459                      validationPassed: validation.success,
 460                      ...(validation.success ? {} : { validationError: validation.error }),
 461                    });
 462                    allToolsUsed.push(toolName);
 463                    toolCallCount++;
 464  
 465                    // Emit tool_result event — treated as data by client, not instructions
 466                    safeSend({ type: "tool_result", tool: toolName, result });
 467  
 468                    // Append tool result to messages — LLM will summarise it next turn (sanitized as data)
 469                    currentMessages.push({
 470                      role: "tool",
 471                      tool_call_id: tc.id,
 472                      content: sanitizeContextData(JSON.stringify(result)),
 473                    } as any);
 474                  }
 475                },
 476  
 477                // ── Turn done ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 478                onDone: async (text) => {
 479                  if (toolCallsDetectedThisTurn && canCallAnotherTool(toolCallCount)) {
 480                    // Tools were called this turn → run another LLM turn with results
 481                    await runStreamTurn();
 482                    return;
 483                  }
 484  
 485                  // Final text answer (or max tool calls reached)
 486                  fullResponse = text || fullResponse;
 487  
 488                  // Output moderation pass (detect prompt leaks, unauthorized discounts)
 489                  const moderation = moderateAgentOutput(fullResponse);
 490                  if (!moderation.passed) {
 491                    fullResponse = moderation.sanitizedOutput;
 492                    refusalCategory = "output_moderation";
 493                  }
 494  
 495                  // Enforce the RAG abstention in code as well as in the prompt.
 496                  // Live DB tools remain valid sources for stock, price, and order actions.
 497                  if (ragChunks.length === 0 && allToolsUsed.length === 0) {
 498                    fullResponse = "Je n'ai pas cette information.";
 499                    refusalCategory = "no_rag_context";
 500                  }
 501  
 502                  if (!clientConnected) return; // client disconnected mid-stream
 503  
 504                  safeSend({ type: "token", token: fullResponse });
 505  
 506                  const assistantMsg = await Message.create({
 507                    storeId,
 508                    conversationId: conversation!.id,
 509                    role: "assistant",
 510                    content: fullResponse,
 511                  });
 512                  traceMessageId = assistantMsg.id;
 513  
 514                  await recordTurn({ action: "chat_turn", status: "success", output: fullResponse });
 515  
 516                  safeSend({
 517                    type: "done",
 518                    conversationId: conversation!.id,
 519                    messageId: assistantMsg.id,
 520                    ragUsed: ragChunks.length > 0,
 521                    sourcesUsed: ragChunks.map((c) => c.source),
 522                    toolsUsed: allToolsUsed,
 523                  });
 524                  if (!res.writableEnded) res.end();
 525                },
 526  
 527                onError: async (err) => {
 528                  await recordTurn({ action: "chat_turn", status: "error", errorMessage: err.message });
 529                  safeSend({ type: "error", message: "Une erreur est survenue. Réessayez." });
 530                  if (!res.writableEnded) res.end();
 531                },
 532              },
 533              abortController.signal
 534            );
 535          };
 536  
 537          await runStreamTurn();
 538          return;
 539        }
 540  
 541        // ── 7. JSON mode (Postman / testing) ─────────────────────────────────────
 542        //    Non-streaming tool loop: run until no tool calls or max reached.
 543        const allToolsUsed: string[] = [];
 544        let toolCallCount = 0;
 545        let currentMessages: LLMMessage[] = [...initialMessages];
 546  
 547        while (canCallAnotherTool(toolCallCount)) {
 548          const client = getLlmClient();
 549          const model = process.env.LLM_MODEL ?? LLM_MODEL;
 550  
 551          const response = await client.chat.completions.create({
 552            model,
 553            messages: currentMessages as any,
 554            tools: canCallAnotherTool(toolCallCount) ? (AGENT_TOOLS as any) : undefined,
 555            tool_choice: "auto",
 556            stream: false,
 557            max_tokens: MAX_TOKENS_PER_TURN,
 558            temperature: 0.3,
 559          });
 560  
 561          if (response.usage) {
 562            addTokenUsage({
 563              promptTokens: response.usage.prompt_tokens,
 564              completionTokens: response.usage.completion_tokens,
 565              totalTokens: response.usage.total_tokens,
 566            });
 567          }
 568  
 569          const choice = response.choices[0];
 570          if (!choice) break;
 571  
 572          // No tool calls → we have the final text answer
 573          if (!choice.message.tool_calls || choice.message.tool_calls.length === 0) {
 574            currentMessages.push({ role: "assistant", content: choice.message.content ?? "" });
 575            break;
 576          }
 577  
 578          // Append assistant message with tool calls
 579          currentMessages.push({
 580            role: "assistant",
 581            content: choice.message.content ?? null,
 582            tool_calls: choice.message.tool_calls,
 583          } as any);
 584  
 585          // Execute each tool call (Zod + allowlist, storeId from JWT)
 586          for (const tc of choice.message.tool_calls) {
 587            if (!canCallAnotherTool(toolCallCount)) break;
 588            if (tc.type !== "function") continue;
 589            const validation = validateToolCall(tc.function.name, tc.function.arguments, toolContext);
 590            const result = await executeToolCall(tc.function.name, tc.function.arguments, toolContext);
 591            toolsCalled.push({
 592              name: tc.function.name,
 593              args: validation.success ? sanitizeForLogging(validation.args) : null,
 594              validationPassed: validation.success,
 595              ...(validation.success ? {} : { validationError: validation.error }),
 596            });
 597            allToolsUsed.push(tc.function.name);
 598            toolCallCount++;
 599            currentMessages.push({
 600              role: "tool",
 601              tool_call_id: tc.id,
 602              content: sanitizeContextData(JSON.stringify(result)),
 603            } as any);
 604          }
 605        }
 606  
 607        // Final answer is the last assistant message in the loop
 608        const lastMsg = currentMessages[currentMessages.length - 1];
 609        let finalResponse =
 610          lastMsg.role === "assistant" && lastMsg.content
 611            ? (lastMsg.content as string)
 612            : "";
 613  
 614        // Output moderation pass (detect prompt leaks, unauthorized discounts)
 615        const moderation = moderateAgentOutput(finalResponse);
 616        if (!moderation.passed) {
 617          finalResponse = moderation.sanitizedOutput;
 618          refusalCategory = "output_moderation";
 619        }
 620  
 621        if (ragChunks.length === 0 && allToolsUsed.length === 0) {
 622          finalResponse = "Je n'ai pas cette information.";
 623          refusalCategory = "no_rag_context";
 624        }
 625  
 626        const assistantMsg = await Message.create({
 627          storeId,
 628          conversationId: conversation.id,
 629          role: "assistant",
 630          content: finalResponse,
 631        });
 632        traceMessageId = assistantMsg.id;
 633  
 634        await recordTurn({ action: "chat_turn", status: "success", output: finalResponse });
 635  
 636        return res.json({
 637          conversationId: conversation.id,
 638          messageId: assistantMsg.id,
 639          response: finalResponse,
 640          ragUsed: ragChunks.length > 0,
 641          sourcesUsed: ragChunks.map((c) => c.source),
 642          toolsUsed: allToolsUsed,
 643        });
 644  
 645      } catch (err) {
 646        try {
 647          await recordTurn({ action: "chat_turn", status: "error", errorMessage: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
 648        } catch { /* ignore secondary log failure */ }
 649  
 650        if (isSSE && !res.writableEnded) {
 651          res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify({ type: "error", message: "Erreur interne." })}\n\n`);
 652          return res.end();
 653        }
 654        next(err);
 655      }
 656    }
 657  );
 658  
 659  // ─── GET /api/agent/conversations ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 660  /**
 661   * List conversation history for the current authenticated user.
 662   * Returns conversations ordered by most recent, each with its messages.
 663   */
 664  agentRouter.get(
 665    "/conversations",
 666    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 667      try {
 668        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 669        const user = req.user!;
 670  
 671        const conversations = await forStore(Conversation, storeId).findAll({
 672          where: { userId: user.userId },
 673          include: [{
 674            association: "messages",
 675            attributes: ["id", "role", "content", "createdAt"],
 676          }],
 677          order: [
 678            ["updatedAt", "DESC"],
 679            [{ model: Message, as: "messages" }, "createdAt", "ASC"],
 680          ],
 681        });
 682  
 683        return res.json({ conversations });
 684      } catch (err) {
 685        next(err);
 686      }
 687    }
 688  );
 689  
 690  // ─── GET /api/agent/conversations/:id ─────────────────────────────────────────
 691  /**
 692   * Get a single conversation and its full history for the current user.
 693   */
 694  agentRouter.get(
 695    "/conversations/:id",
 696    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 697      try {
 698        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 699        const user = req.user!;
 700        const convId = Number(req.params.id);
 701  
 702        const conversation = await forStore(Conversation, storeId).findOne({
 703          where: { id: convId, userId: user.userId },
 704          include: [{
 705            association: "messages",
 706            attributes: ["id", "role", "content", "createdAt"],
 707          }],
 708          order: [[{ model: Message, as: "messages" }, "createdAt", "ASC"]],
 709        });
 710  
 711        if (!conversation) {
 712          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Conversation introuvable" });
 713        }
 714  
 715        return res.json({ conversation });
 716      } catch (err) {
 717        next(err);
 718      }
 719    }
 720  );
 721  
 722  // ─── GET /api/agent/logs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 723  /**
 724   * Paginated, store-scoped audit traces for AI interactions (ADMIN only).
 725   */
 726  agentRouter.get(
 727    "/logs",
 728    requireRole("ADMIN"),
 729    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 730      try {
 731        const parsed = agentLogsQuerySchema.safeParse(req.query);
 732        if (!parsed.success) {
 733          return res.status(400).json({
 734            error: "Invalid log filters",
 735            details: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
 736          });
 737        }
 738        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 739        const { page, limit, conversationId, customerId, fromDate, toDate, disposition, action, status } = parsed.data;
 740        const where: Record<PropertyKey, unknown> = {};
 741        if (conversationId !== undefined) where.conversationId = conversationId;
 742        if (action) where.action = action;
 743        if (status) where.status = status;
 744        if (customerId !== undefined) {
 745          const customerConversations = (await forStore(Conversation, storeId).findAll({
 746            where: { userId: customerId },
 747            attributes: ["id"],
 748            raw: true,
 749          })) as unknown as Array<{ id: number }>;
 750          where.conversationId = { [Op.in]: customerConversations.map(({ id }) => id) };
 751        }
 752        if (fromDate || toDate) {
 753          const createdAt: Record<PropertyKey, Date> = {};
 754          if (fromDate) createdAt[Op.gte] = new Date(`${fromDate}T00:00:00.000Z`);
 755          if (toDate) {
 756            const exclusiveEnd = new Date(`${toDate}T00:00:00.000Z`);
 757            exclusiveEnd.setUTCDate(exclusiveEnd.getUTCDate() + 1);
 758            createdAt[Op.lt] = exclusiveEnd;
 759          }
 760          where.createdAt = createdAt;
 761        }
 762        if (disposition === "blocked") {
 763          where.refusalCategory = { [Op.in]: ["prompt_injection", "output_moderation"] };
 764        } else if (disposition === "refused") {
 765          where.refusalCategory = {
 766            [Op.ne]: null,
 767            [Op.notIn]: ["prompt_injection", "output_moderation"],
 768          };
 769        }
 770  
 771        const { rows: rawLogs, count: total } = await forStore(AgentLog, storeId).findAndCountAll({
 772          where,
 773          order: [["createdAt", "DESC"]],
 774          limit,
 775          offset: (page - 1) * limit,
 776        });
 777        const logs = rawLogs as AgentLogRecord[];
 778        const conversationIds = Array.from(
 779          new Set(logs.map((log) => log.conversationId).filter((id): id is number => id !== null))
 780        );
 781        const conversations = conversationIds.length
 782          ? await forStore(Conversation, storeId).findAll({
 783              where: { id: { [Op.in]: conversationIds } },
 784              attributes: ["id", "userId"],
 785              include: [{ association: "user", attributes: ["id", "name", "email"], where: { storeId }, required: true }],
 786            })
 787          : [];
 788        const customersByConversation = new Map(
 789          conversations.map((row) => {
 790            const conversation = row as Conversation;
 791            const relatedUser = (row as Conversation & { user?: { id: number; name: string; email: string } }).user;
 792            return [conversation.id, relatedUser ? { id: relatedUser.id, name: relatedUser.name, email: relatedUser.email } : null] as const;
 793          })
 794        );
 795        return res.json({
 796          logs: logs.map((log) => ({
 797            ...log.toJSON(),
 798            customer: log.conversationId ? customersByConversation.get(log.conversationId) ?? null : null,
 799          })),
 800          pagination: { total, page, limit, totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit) },
 801        });
 802      } catch (err) {
 803        next(err);
 804      }
 805    }
 806  );
 807  
 808  // ─── GET /api/agent/logs/:id ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
 809  /** Return the full audit trace for one turn, restricted to the current store. */
 810  agentRouter.get(
 811    "/logs/:id",
 812    requireRole("ADMIN"),
 813    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 814      try {
 815        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 816        const logId = Number(req.params.id);
 817        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(logId) || logId < 1) {
 818          return res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid log ID" });
 819        }
 820        const log = (await forStore(AgentLog, storeId).findOne({ where: { id: logId } })) as AgentLogRecord | null;
 821        if (!log) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Agent log not found" });
 822  
 823        let customer = null;
 824        if (log.conversationId) {
 825          const conversation = await forStore(Conversation, storeId).findOne({
 826            where: { id: log.conversationId },
 827            attributes: ["id", "userId"],
 828            include: [{ association: "user", attributes: ["id", "name", "email"], where: { storeId }, required: true }],
 829          });
 830          const relatedUser = (conversation as (Conversation & { user?: { id: number; name: string; email: string } }) | null)?.user;
 831          if (relatedUser) customer = { id: relatedUser.id, name: relatedUser.name, email: relatedUser.email };
 832        }
 833        return res.json({ log, customer });
 834      } catch (err) {
 835        next(err);
 836      }
 837    }
 838  );
 839  
 840  export default agentRouter;
backend/src/routes/auth.ts

الواجهة كترسل الاسم والبريد وكلمة السر، والخادم هو اللي كيحدد المتجر والدور.

   1  import { Router, Request, Response } from "express";
   2  import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";
   3  import { DEMO_STORE_NAME } from "../config/demo";
   4  import {
   5    generateRefreshToken,
   6    hashToken,
   7    REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_MS,
   8    signAccessToken,
   9  } from "../config/jwt";
  10  import { Customer, RefreshToken, Store, User } from "../models";
  11  import {
  12    authenticate,
  13    injectTenant,
  14    requireRole,
  15    validate,
  16  } from "../middlewares";
  17  import {
  18    loginSchema,
  19    logoutSchema,
  20    refreshSchema,
  21    registerSchema,
  22  } from "../schemas/auth";
  23  
  24  const authRouter = Router();
  25  
  26  // Helper to issue access + rotating refresh token pair
  27  async function issueTokenPair(user: User): Promise<{ accessToken: string; refreshToken: string }> {
  28    const accessToken = signAccessToken({
  29      userId: user.id,
  30      storeId: user.storeId,
  31      role: user.role,
  32      email: user.email,
  33    });
  34  
  35    const rawRefreshToken = generateRefreshToken();
  36    const hashedRefreshToken = hashToken(rawRefreshToken);
  37    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_MS);
  38  
  39    await RefreshToken.create({
  40      storeId: user.storeId,
  41      userId: user.id,
  42      token: hashedRefreshToken,
  43      expiresAt,
  44    });
  45  
  46    return { accessToken, refreshToken: rawRefreshToken };
  47  }
  48  
  49  // POST /api/auth/register
  50  authRouter.post("/register", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  51    try {
  52      const parseResult = registerSchema.safeParse(req.body);
  53      if (!parseResult.success) {
  54        return res.status(400).json({
  55          error: "Validation failed",
  56          details: parseResult.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
  57        });
  58      }
  59  
  60      const { name, email, password } = parseResult.data;
  61  
  62      const store = await Store.findOne({ where: { name: DEMO_STORE_NAME } });
  63      if (!store) {
  64        return res.status(404).json({ error: "Store not found" });
  65      }
  66  
  67      const existingUser = await User.findOne({
  68        where: { storeId: store.id, email },
  69      });
  70      if (existingUser) {
  71        return res.status(409).json({ error: "A user with this email already exists" });
  72      }
  73  
  74      const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10);
  75  
  76      const user = await User.create({
  77        storeId: store.id,
  78        name,
  79        email,
  80        password: passwordHash,
  81        role: "CUSTOMER",
  82      });
  83  
  84      // Ensure customer record exists for store orders
  85      await Customer.findOrCreate({
  86        where: { storeId: store.id, email },
  87        defaults: {
  88          storeId: store.id,
  89          name,
  90          email,
  91        },
  92      });
  93  
  94      const { accessToken, refreshToken } = await issueTokenPair(user);
  95  
  96      return res.status(201).json({
  97        message: "User registered successfully",
  98        user: {
  99          id: user.id,
 100          storeId: user.storeId,
 101          name: user.name,
 102          email: user.email,
 103          role: user.role,
 104        },
 105        accessToken,
 106        refreshToken,
 107      });
 108    } catch (error) {
 109      console.error("Register error:", error);
 110      return res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });
 111    }
 112  });
 113  
 114  // POST /api/auth/login
 115  authRouter.post("/login", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
 116    try {
