 117      const parseResult = loginSchema.safeParse(req.body);
 118      if (!parseResult.success) {
 119        return res.status(400).json({
 120          error: "Validation failed",
 121          details: parseResult.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
 122        });
 123      }
 124  
 125      const { email, password } = parseResult.data;
 126  
 127      const store = await Store.findOne({ where: { name: DEMO_STORE_NAME } });
 128      if (!store) {
 129        return res.status(404).json({ error: "Store not found" });
 130      }
 131  
 132      const user = await User.findOne({
 133        where: { storeId: store.id, email },
 134      });
 135  
 136      if (!user) {
 137        return res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid email or password" });
 138      }
 139  
 140      const isMatch = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password);
 141      if (!isMatch) {
 142        return res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid email or password" });
 143      }
 144  
 145      const { accessToken, refreshToken } = await issueTokenPair(user);
 146  
 147      return res.status(200).json({
 148        message: "Login successful",
 149        user: {
 150          id: user.id,
 151          storeId: user.storeId,
 152          name: user.name,
 153          email: user.email,
 154          role: user.role,
 155        },
 156        accessToken,
 157        refreshToken,
 158      });
 159    } catch (error) {
 160      console.error("Login error:", error);
 161      return res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });
 162    }
 163  });
 164  
 165  // POST /api/auth/refresh
 166  authRouter.post("/refresh", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
 167    try {
 168      const parseResult = refreshSchema.safeParse(req.body);
 169      if (!parseResult.success) {
 170        return res.status(400).json({
 171          error: "Validation failed",
 172          details: parseResult.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
 173        });
 174      }
 175  
 176      const { refreshToken: rawToken } = parseResult.data;
 177      const tokenHash = hashToken(rawToken);
 178  
 179      const tokenRecord = await RefreshToken.findOne({
 180        where: { token: tokenHash },
 181      });
 182  
 183      if (!tokenRecord) {
 184        // Reuse of old or invalid refresh token is rejected
 185        return res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid or expired refresh token" });
 186      }
 187  
 188      // Check expiration
 189      if (new Date() > tokenRecord.expiresAt) {
 190        await tokenRecord.destroy();
 191        return res.status(401).json({ error: "Refresh token has expired" });
 192      }
 193  
 194      const user = await User.findByPk(tokenRecord.userId);
 195      if (!user) {
 196        await tokenRecord.destroy();
 197        return res.status(401).json({ error: "User not found" });
 198      }
 199  
 200      // ROTATION: Invalidate the previous refresh token
 201      await tokenRecord.destroy();
 202  
 203      // Issue a brand-new token pair
 204      const { accessToken, refreshToken } = await issueTokenPair(user);
 205  
 206      return res.status(200).json({
 207        message: "Token refreshed successfully",
 208        accessToken,
 209        refreshToken,
 210      });
 211    } catch (error) {
 212      console.error("Refresh error:", error);
 213      return res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });
 214    }
 215  });
 216  
 217  // POST /api/auth/logout
 218  authRouter.post("/logout", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
 219    try {
 220      const parseResult = logoutSchema.safeParse(req.body);
 221      if (!parseResult.success) {
 222        return res.status(400).json({
 223          error: "Validation failed",
 224          details: parseResult.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
 225        });
 226      }
 227  
 228      const { refreshToken: rawToken } = parseResult.data;
 229      const tokenHash = hashToken(rawToken);
 230  
 231      // Invalidate refresh token so it cannot be used again
 232      await RefreshToken.destroy({
 233        where: { token: tokenHash },
 234      });
 235  
 236      return res.status(200).json({
 237        message: "Logged out successfully",
 238      });
 239    } catch (error) {
 240      console.error("Logout error:", error);
 241      return res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });
 242    }
 243  });
 244  
 245  // GET /api/auth/me (Protected - Any authenticated user, scoped to storeId from token)
 246  authRouter.get(
 247    "/me",
 248    authenticate,
 249    injectTenant,
 250    (req: Request, res: Response) => {
 251      res.status(200).json({
 252        user: req.user,
 253        storeId: req.storeId,
 254      });
 255    }
 256  );
 257  
 258  // GET /api/auth/admin-only (Protected - ADMIN role only)
 259  authRouter.get(
 260    "/admin-only",
 261    authenticate,
 262    injectTenant,
 263    requireRole("ADMIN"),
 264    (req: Request, res: Response) => {
 265      res.status(200).json({
 266        message: "Welcome to admin dashboard",
 267        user: req.user,
 268        storeId: req.storeId,
 269      });
 270    }
 271  );
 272  
 273  export default authRouter;
backend/src/routes/categories.ts

الإدارة كتحتاج التحقق من الدور والمتجر والمدخلات، حتى إلا الواجهة عطلات الأزرار لغير المسؤول.

   1  import { Router, Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
   2  import { Category, forStore } from "../models";
   3  import {
   4    authenticate,
   5    injectTenant,
   6    requireRole,
   7    resolveStoreTenant,
   8    validate,
   9  } from "../middlewares";
  10  import {
  11    categoryIdParamSchema,
  12    createCategorySchema,
  13    updateCategorySchema,
  14  } from "../schemas/category";
  15  
  16  const categoriesRouter = Router();
  17  
  18  /**
  19   * GET /api/categories
  20   * List all categories for the current store.
  21   * Scoped by storeId.
  22   */
  23  categoriesRouter.get(
  24    "/",
  25    resolveStoreTenant,
  26    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  27      try {
  28        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
  29        const categories = await forStore(Category, storeId).findAll({
  30          order: [["name", "ASC"]],
  31        });
  32  
  33        return res.json({ categories });
  34      } catch (err) {
  35        next(err);
  36      }
  37    }
  38  );
  39  
  40  /**
  41   * GET /api/categories/:id
  42   * Retrieve a single category.
  43   * Scoped by storeId.
  44   */
  45  categoriesRouter.get(
  46    "/:id",
  47    resolveStoreTenant,
  48    validate(categoryIdParamSchema, "params"),
  49    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  50      try {
  51        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
  52        const categoryId = Number(req.params.id);
  53  
  54        const category = await forStore(Category, storeId).findOne({
  55          where: { id: categoryId },
  56        });
  57  
  58        if (!category) {
  59          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Category not found" });
  60        }
  61  
  62        return res.json({ category });
  63      } catch (err) {
  64        next(err);
  65      }
  66    }
  67  );
  68  
  69  /**
  70   * POST /api/categories
  71   * Create a new category (ADMIN only).
  72   * Scoped by storeId.
  73   */
  74  categoriesRouter.post(
  75    "/",
  76    authenticate,
  77    injectTenant,
  78    requireRole("ADMIN"),
  79    validate(createCategorySchema, "body"),
  80    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  81      try {
  82        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
  83        const { name, description } = req.body;
  84  
  85        // Check unique category name within this store
  86        const existing = await forStore(Category, storeId).findOne({
  87          where: { name },
  88        });
  89  
  90        if (existing) {
  91          return res
  92            .status(409)
  93            .json({ error: "Category already exists in this store" });
  94        }
  95  
  96        const category = await Category.create({
  97          name,
  98          description: description || null,
  99          storeId,
 100        });
 101  
 102        return res.status(201).json({
 103          message: "Category created successfully",
 104          category,
 105        });
 106      } catch (err) {
 107        next(err);
 108      }
 109    }
 110  );
 111  
 112  /**
 113   * PUT /api/categories/:id
 114   * Update category (ADMIN only).
 115   * Scoped by storeId.
 116   */
 117  categoriesRouter.put(
 118    "/:id",
 119    authenticate,
 120    injectTenant,
 121    requireRole("ADMIN"),
 122    validate(categoryIdParamSchema, "params"),
 123    validate(updateCategorySchema, "body"),
 124    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 125      try {
 126        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 127        const categoryId = Number(req.params.id);
 128  
 129        const category = (await forStore(Category, storeId).findOne({
 130          where: { id: categoryId },
 131        })) as Category | null;
 132  
 133        if (!category) {
 134          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Category not found" });
 135        }
 136  
 137        // Check name uniqueness if updated
 138        if (req.body.name && req.body.name !== category.name) {
 139          const existing = await forStore(Category, storeId).findOne({
 140            where: { name: req.body.name },
 141          });
 142  
 143          if (existing) {
 144            return res
 145              .status(409)
 146              .json({ error: "Category name already in use in this store" });
 147          }
 148        }
 149  
 150        await category.update({
 151          ...(req.body.name !== undefined ? { name: req.body.name } : {}),
 152          ...(req.body.description !== undefined
 153            ? { description: req.body.description }
 154            : {}),
 155        });
 156  
 157        return res.json({
 158          message: "Category updated successfully",
 159          category,
 160        });
 161      } catch (err) {
 162        next(err);
 163      }
 164    }
 165  );
 166  
 167  /**
 168   * DELETE /api/categories/:id
 169   * Delete category (ADMIN only).
 170   * Scoped by storeId.
 171   */
 172  categoriesRouter.delete(
 173    "/:id",
 174    authenticate,
 175    injectTenant,
 176    requireRole("ADMIN"),
 177    validate(categoryIdParamSchema, "params"),
 178    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 179      try {
 180        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 181        const categoryId = Number(req.params.id);
 182  
 183        const category = (await forStore(Category, storeId).findOne({
 184          where: { id: categoryId },
 185        })) as Category | null;
 186  
 187        if (!category) {
 188          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Category not found" });
 189        }
 190  
 191        await category.destroy();
 192  
 193        return res.json({ message: "Category deleted successfully" });
 194      } catch (err) {
 195        next(err);
 196      }
 197    }
 198  );
 199  
 200  export default categoriesRouter;
backend/src/routes/documents.ts

الملف المرفوع كيتحول لنص، ثم مقاطع، ثم embeddings، ثم سجلات قابلة للبحث.

   1  import { Router, Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
   2  import multer from "multer";
   3  import { Document, Chunk, forStore } from "../models";
   4  import Document_Model from "../models/Document";
   5  import { authenticate, injectTenant, requireRole, validate } from "../middlewares";
   6  import { chunkText, embedChunks, retrieveContext, SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD } from "../services/rag";
   7  import sequelize from "../config/database";
   8  import { z } from "zod";
   9  
  10  const documentsRouter = Router();
  11  
  12  // ─── Multer: accept md/txt in memory (max 2 MB) ──────────────────────────────
  13  const upload = multer({
  14    storage: multer.memoryStorage(),
  15    limits: { fileSize: 2 * 1024 * 1024 },
  16    fileFilter: (_req, file, cb) => {
  17      const allowed = ["text/plain", "text/markdown"];
  18      if (allowed.includes(file.mimetype) || file.originalname.match(/\.(md|txt)$/i)) {
  19        cb(null, true);
  20      } else {
  21        cb(new Error("Only .md and .txt files are accepted"));
  22      }
  23    },
  24  });
  25  
  26  // ─── All routes: ADMIN only ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  27  documentsRouter.use(authenticate, injectTenant, requireRole("ADMIN"));
  28  
  29  // ─── Validation schemas ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  30  const createDocumentSchema = z.object({
  31    name: z.string().trim().min(1, "Document name is required"),
  32  });
  33  
  34  // ─── GET /api/documents ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  35  /**
  36   * List all RAG knowledge documents for the current store.
  37   */
  38  documentsRouter.get(
  39    "/",
  40    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  41      try {
  42        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
  43        const documents = await forStore(Document, storeId).findAll({
  44          order: [["createdAt", "DESC"]],
  45          attributes: ["id", "name", "mimeType", "status", "errorMessage", "createdAt", "updatedAt"],
  46        });
  47        const chunkCounts = documents.length
  48          ? ((await Chunk.findAll({
  49              where: {
  50                storeId,
  51                documentId: documents.map((document) => (document as Document_Model).id),
  52              },
  53              attributes: [
  54                "documentId",
  55                [sequelize.fn("COUNT", sequelize.col("id")), "chunksCreated"],
  56              ],
  57              group: ["documentId"],
  58              raw: true,
  59            })) as unknown as Array<{ documentId: number; chunksCreated: string }>)
  60          : [];
  61        const chunksByDocument = new Map(
  62          chunkCounts.map(({ documentId, chunksCreated }) => [
  63            documentId,
  64            Number(chunksCreated),
  65          ])
  66        );
  67        return res.json({
  68          documents: documents.map((document) => ({
  69            ...document.toJSON(),
  70            chunksCreated: chunksByDocument.get((document as Document_Model).id) ?? 0,
  71          })),
  72        });
  73      } catch (err) {
  74        next(err);
  75      }
  76    }
  77  );
  78  
  79  // ─── GET /api/documents/:id ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  80  /**
  81   * Retrieve a single document with its chunk count.
  82   */
  83  documentsRouter.get(
  84    "/:id",
  85    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  86      try {
  87        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
  88        const documentId = Number(req.params.id);
  89  
  90        const document = await forStore(Document, storeId).findOne({
  91          where: { id: documentId },
  92          include: [
  93            {
  94              association: "chunks",
  95              attributes: ["id", "content"],
  96            },
  97          ],
  98        });
  99  
 100        if (!document) {
 101          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Document not found" });
 102        }
 103  
 104        return res.json({ document });
 105      } catch (err) {
 106        next(err);
 107      }
 108    }
 109  );
 110  
 111  // ─── POST /api/documents ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 112  /**
 113   * Upload an md/txt knowledge document, chunk it, embed it, and store all
 114   * chunks with their 1536-dim embeddings in pgvector — all in one transaction.
 115   *
 116   * Body (multipart/form-data):
 117   *   - file  : .md or .txt file  (required)
 118   *   - name  : human-readable document name  (required)
 119   */
 120  documentsRouter.post(
 121    "/",
 122    upload.single("file"),
 123    validate(createDocumentSchema, "body"),
 124    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 125      if (!req.file) {
 126        return res.status(400).json({ error: "A .md or .txt file is required" });
 127      }
 128  
 129      const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 130      const userId = req.user!.userId;
 131      const { name } = req.body as { name: string };
 132  
 133      // Decode uploaded file to UTF-8 text
 134      const rawText = req.file.buffer.toString("utf-8");
 135  
 136      const transaction = await sequelize.transaction();
 137  
 138      try {
 139        // 1. Create the Document record (status = processing)
 140        const document = await Document.create(
 141          {
 142            storeId,
 143            uploadedBy: userId,
 144            name,
 145            fileUrl: `upload:${Date.now()}-${req.file.originalname}`,
 146            mimeType: req.file.mimetype || "text/plain",
 147            status: "processing",
 148          },
 149          { transaction }
 150        );
 151  
 152        // 2. Split into ~800-char chunks
 153        const textChunks = chunkText(rawText);
 154  
 155        if (textChunks.length === 0) {
 156          await transaction.rollback();
 157          return res.status(422).json({ error: "Document has no usable text content" });
 158        }
 159  
 160        // 3. Generate embeddings (batched, single API call for ≤100 chunks)
 161        const embeddings = await embedChunks(textChunks);
 162  
 163        // 4. Bulk-insert all chunks with their embeddings
 164        await Chunk.bulkCreate(
 165          textChunks.map((content, i) => ({
 166            storeId,
 167            documentId: document.id,
 168            content,
 169            embedding: embeddings[i],
 170          })),
 171          { transaction }
 172        );
 173  
 174        // 5. Mark document as completed
 175        await document.update({ status: "completed", errorMessage: null }, { transaction });
 176  
 177        await transaction.commit();
 178  
 179        return res.status(201).json({
 180          message: `Document indexed successfully`,
 181          document: {
 182            id: document.id,
 183            name: document.name,
 184            status: document.status,
 185            chunksCreated: textChunks.length,
 186          },
 187        });
 188      } catch (err) {
 189        await transaction.rollback();
 190  
 191        // The transaction removes the in-progress document. Keep a separate,
 192        // safe failure record so the admin can see why this upload did not index.
 193        try {
 194          await Document.create({
 195            storeId,
 196            uploadedBy: userId,
 197            name,
 198            fileUrl: `upload:${Date.now()}-${req.file.originalname}`,
 199            mimeType: req.file.mimetype || "text/plain",
 200            status: "failed",
 201            errorMessage: "L’indexation a échoué. Vérifiez la configuration d’embeddings puis réessayez.",
 202          });
 203        } catch {
 204          console.error("Could not record document ingestion failure.");
 205        }
 206        return res.status(502).json({
 207          error: "L’indexation du document a échoué. Réessayez dans quelques instants.",
 208        });
 209      }
 210    }
 211  );
 212  
 213  // ─── PUT /api/documents/:id/reingest ─────────────────────────────────────────
 214  /**
 215   * Re-upload a document file and re-run the full chunking + embedding pipeline.
 216   * All old chunks for this document are deleted first (atomic transaction).
 217   *
 218   * Body (multipart/form-data):
 219   *   - file  : new .md or .txt file  (required)
 220   */
 221  documentsRouter.put(
 222    "/:id/reingest",
 223    upload.single("file"),
 224    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 225      if (!req.file) {
 226        return res.status(400).json({ error: "A .md or .txt file is required for re-ingestion" });
 227      }
 228  
 229      const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 230      const documentId = Number(req.params.id);
 231  
 232      const document = (await forStore(Document, storeId).findOne({
 233        where: { id: documentId },
 234      })) as Document_Model | null;
 235  
 236      if (!document) {
 237        return res.status(404).json({ error: "Document not found" });
 238      }
 239  
 240      const rawText = req.file.buffer.toString("utf-8");
 241      const transaction = await sequelize.transaction();
 242  
 243      try {
 244        // 1. Mark as processing
 245        await document.update({ status: "processing" }, { transaction });
 246  
 247        // 2. Delete all existing chunks for this document
 248        await Chunk.destroy({ where: { documentId }, transaction });
 249  
 250        // 3. Chunk + embed the new content
 251        const textChunks = chunkText(rawText);
 252        if (textChunks.length === 0) {
 253          await transaction.rollback();
 254          return res.status(422).json({ error: "Document has no usable text content" });
 255        }
 256  
 257        const embeddings = await embedChunks(textChunks);
 258  
 259        // 4. Insert new chunks
 260        await Chunk.bulkCreate(
 261          textChunks.map((content, i) => ({
 262            storeId,
 263            documentId,
 264            content,
 265            embedding: embeddings[i],
 266          })),
 267          { transaction }
 268        );
 269  
 270        // 5. Update file metadata + mark completed
 271        await document.update(
 272          {
 273            fileUrl: `upload:${Date.now()}-${req.file!.originalname}`,
 274            mimeType: req.file!.mimetype || "text/plain",
 275            status: "completed",
 276            errorMessage: null,
 277          },
 278          { transaction }
 279        );
 280  
 281        await transaction.commit();
 282  
 283        return res.json({
 284          message: "Document re-ingested successfully",
 285          document: {
 286            id: document.id,
 287            name: document.name,
 288            status: "completed",
 289            chunksCreated: textChunks.length,
 290          },
 291        });
 292      } catch (err) {
 293        await transaction.rollback();
 294        next(err);
 295      }
 296    }
 297  );
 298  
 299  // ─── DELETE /api/documents/:id ────────────────────────────────────────────────
 300  /**
 301   * Delete a document and all its chunks (cascade in transaction).
 302   */
 303  documentsRouter.delete(
 304    "/:id",
 305    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 306      try {
 307        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 308        const documentId = Number(req.params.id);
 309  
 310        const document = await forStore(Document, storeId).findOne({
 311          where: { id: documentId },
 312        });
 313  
 314        if (!document) {
 315          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Document not found" });
 316        }
 317  
 318        const transaction = await sequelize.transaction();
 319        try {
 320          // Delete chunks first (no DB-level CASCADE to guarantee cross-tenant safety)
 321          await Chunk.destroy({ where: { storeId, documentId }, transaction });
 322          await document.destroy({ transaction });
 323          await transaction.commit();
 324        } catch (err) {
 325          await transaction.rollback();
 326          throw err;
 327        }
 328  
 329        return res.json({ message: "Document and all its chunks deleted" });
 330      } catch (err) {
 331        next(err);
 332      }
 333    }
 334  );
 335  
 336  // ─── GET /api/documents/search ───────────────────────────────────────────────
 337  /**
 338   * Semantic search endpoint — useful for testing RAG retrieval from Postman
 339   * before the full agent is wired up.
 340   *
 341   * Any authenticated user can call this (customer or admin); the agent will
 342   * use retrieveContext() directly, but this route lets you verify results.
 343   *
 344   * GET /api/documents/search?q=Vous+livrez+%C3%A0+Casablanca+%3F
 345   *
 346   * Response when chunks found:
 347   *   { hasContext: true, threshold: 0.75, chunks: [...] }
 348   *
 349   * Response when no chunk crosses the threshold:
 350   *   { hasContext: false, threshold: 0.75, chunks: [], message: "Je n'ai pas cette information" }
 351   */
 352  documentsRouter.get(
 353    "/search",
 354    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 355      try {
 356        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 357        const question = (req.query.q as string | undefined)?.trim();
 358  
 359        if (!question || question.length === 0) {
 360          return res
 361            .status(400)
 362            .json({ error: "Query parameter `q` is required" });
 363        }
 364  
 365        const chunks = await retrieveContext(question, storeId);
 366  
 367        if (chunks.length === 0) {
 368          return res.json({
 369            hasContext: false,
 370            threshold: SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD,
 371            chunks: [],
 372            message: "Je n'ai pas cette information",
 373          });
 374        }
 375  
 376        return res.json({
 377          hasContext: true,
 378          threshold: SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD,
 379          count: chunks.length,
 380          chunks,
 381        });
 382      } catch (err) {
 383        next(err);
 384      }
 385    }
 386  );
 387  
 388  export default documentsRouter;
backend/src/routes/orders.ts

POST /api/orders كيحتاج confirmed:true وكيحسب الثمن من القاعدة.

   1  import { Router, Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
   2  import sequelize from "../config/database";
   3  import { Customer, Order, OrderItem, Product, forStore } from "../models";
   4  import { authenticate, injectTenant, requireRole, validate } from "../middlewares";
   5  import {
   6    createOrderSchema,
   7    orderIdParamSchema,
   8    orderConfirmationSchema,
   9    updateOrderStatusSchema,
  10    type CreateOrderInput,
  11    type UpdateOrderStatusInput,
  12  } from "../schemas/order";
  13  import { notifyOrderCreated } from "../services/n8nNotifications";
  14  
  15  const ordersRouter = Router();
  16  
  17  // All order routes require authentication and store tenant injection
  18  ordersRouter.use(authenticate, injectTenant);
  19  
  20  /**
  21   * Helper: Find or create the Customer record corresponding to the authenticated User.
  22   */
  23  async function resolveCustomer(storeId: number, email: string, name?: string) {
  24    const [customer] = await Customer.findOrCreate({
  25      where: { storeId, email },
  26      defaults: {
  27        storeId,
  28        email,
  29        name: name || email.split("@")[0],
  30      },
  31    });
  32    return customer;
  33  }
  34  
  35  /**
  36   * POST /api/orders (COD)
  37   * Validates items, checks stock, decrements stock, and creates Order + OrderItems
  38   * all within a single ACID transaction with row-level locking (concurrency-safe).
  39   */
  40  ordersRouter.post(
  41    "/",
  42    validate(createOrderSchema, "body"),
  43    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  44      try {
  45        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
  46        const user = req.user!;
  47        const { items, customerName, customerPhone } = req.body as CreateOrderInput;
  48  
  49        const customer = await resolveCustomer(
  50          storeId,
  51          user.email,
  52          customerName || user.email.split("@")[0]
  53        );
  54  
  55        if (customerPhone && customer.phone !== customerPhone) {
  56          await customer.update({ phone: customerPhone });
  57        }
  58  
  59        // Execute in a database transaction with exclusive row-level locking
  60        const result = await sequelize.transaction(async (t) => {
  61          let total = 0;
  62          const orderItemsData: Array<{
  63            storeId: number;
  64            customerId: number;
  65            productId: number;
  66            quantity: number;
  67            unitPrice: number;
  68            name: string;
  69          }> = [];
  70  
  71          // Check stock and lock product rows
  72          for (const item of items) {
  73            const product = (await Product.findOne({
  74              where: { id: item.productId, storeId },
  75              transaction: t,
  76              lock: t.LOCK.UPDATE, // Exclusive lock: guarantees concurrency safety
  77            })) as Product | null;
  78  
  79            if (!product) {
  80              const err = new Error(`Product #${item.productId} not found in this store`);
  81              (err as any).statusCode = 404;
  82              throw err;
  83            }
  84  
  85            if (product.stock < item.quantity) {
  86              const err = new Error(
  87                `Insufficient stock for "${product.name}". Available: ${product.stock}, requested: ${item.quantity}`
  88              );
  89              (err as any).statusCode = 400;
  90              throw err;
  91            }
  92  
  93            const unitPrice = Number(product.price);
  94            total += unitPrice * item.quantity;
  95  
  96            // Decrement stock
  97            await product.decrement("stock", { by: item.quantity, transaction: t });
  98  
  99            orderItemsData.push({
 100              storeId,
 101              customerId: customer.id,
 102              productId: product.id,
 103              quantity: item.quantity,
 104              unitPrice,
 105              name: product.name ?? "Produit",
 106            });
 107          }
 108  
 109          // Create Order
 110          const order = await Order.create(
 111            {
 112              storeId,
 113              customerId: customer.id,
 114              status: "PENDING",
 115              total,
 116            },
 117            { transaction: t }
 118          );
 119  
 120          // Create OrderItems
 121          const createdItems = await OrderItem.bulkCreate(
 122            orderItemsData.map((data) => ({
 123              storeId: data.storeId,
 124              customerId: data.customerId,
 125              productId: data.productId,
 126              quantity: data.quantity,
 127              unitPrice: data.unitPrice,
 128              orderId: order.id,
 129            })),
 130            { transaction: t }
 131          );
 132  
 133          return { order, items: createdItems, notificationItems: orderItemsData };
 134        });
 135  
 136        // The order is committed. Notify asynchronously so n8n cannot delay or
 137        // roll back a successful COD order.
 138        void notifyOrderCreated({
 139          storeId,
 140          order: {
 141            id: result.order.id,
 142            status: result.order.status,
 143            total: Number(result.order.total),
 144          },
 145          customer: {
 146            id: customer.id,
 147            name: customer.name ?? "Client",
 148            email: customer.email ?? "",
 149            phone: customer.phone,
 150          },
 151          items: result.notificationItems.map((item) => ({
 152            productId: item.productId,
 153            name: item.name,
 154            quantity: item.quantity,
 155            unitPrice: item.unitPrice,
 156          })),
 157        });
 158  
 159        return res.status(201).json({
 160          message: "Order placed successfully (Cash on Delivery)",
 161          order: result.order,
 162          items: result.items,
 163        });
 164      } catch (err: any) {
 165        if (err.statusCode) {
 166          return res.status(err.statusCode).json({ error: err.message });
 167        }
 168        next(err);
 169      }
 170    }
 171  );
 172  
 173  /**
 174   * GET /api/orders
 175   * Returns orders for the current customer only.
 176   * Admins can view all orders for the store.
 177   */
 178  ordersRouter.get(
 179    "/",
 180    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 181      try {
 182        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 183        const user = req.user!;
 184  
 185        if (user.role === "CUSTOMER") {
 186          const customer = await Customer.findOne({
 187            where: { storeId, email: user.email },
 188          });
 189  
 190          if (!customer) {
 191            return res.json({ orders: [] });
 192          }
 193  
 194          const orders = await forStore(Order, storeId).findAll({
 195            where: { customerId: customer.id },
 196            include: [
 197              {
 198                association: "items",
 199                include: [
 200                  {
 201                    association: "product",
 202                    attributes: ["id", "name", "price", "sku"],
 203                  },
 204                ],
 205              },
 206            ],
 207            order: [["createdAt", "DESC"]],
 208          });
 209  
 210          return res.json({ orders });
 211        }
 212  
 213        // ADMIN role: View all store orders
 214        const orders = await forStore(Order, storeId).findAll({
 215          include: [
 216            {
 217              association: "items",
 218              include: [
 219                {
 220                  association: "product",
 221                  attributes: ["id", "name", "price", "sku"],
 222                },
 223              ],
 224            },
 225            {
 226              association: "customer",
 227              attributes: ["id", "name", "email", "phone"],
 228            },
 229          ],
 230          order: [["createdAt", "DESC"]],
 231        });
 232  
 233        return res.json({ orders });
 234      } catch (err) {
 235        next(err);
 236      }
 237    }
 238  );
 239  
 240  /**
 241   * GET /api/orders/:id
 242   * Retrieve single order details. Customers can only view their own order.
 243   */
 244  ordersRouter.get(
 245    "/:id",
 246    validate(orderIdParamSchema, "params"),
 247    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 248      try {
 249        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 250        const user = req.user!;
 251        const orderId = Number(req.params.id);
 252  
 253        const order = (await forStore(Order, storeId).findOne({
 254          where: { id: orderId },
 255          include: [
 256            {
 257              association: "items",
 258              include: [
 259                {
 260                  association: "product",
 261                  attributes: ["id", "name", "price", "sku"],
 262                },
 263              ],
 264            },
 265            {
 266              association: "customer",
 267              attributes: ["id", "name", "email", "phone"],
 268            },
 269          ],
 270        })) as Order | null;
 271  
 272        if (!order) {
 273          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Order not found" });
 274        }
 275  
 276        // Guardrail: customers can only view their own orders
 277        if (user.role === "CUSTOMER") {
 278          const customer = await Customer.findOne({
 279            where: { storeId, email: user.email },
 280          });
 281  
 282          if (!customer || order.customerId !== customer.id) {
 283            return res.status(404).json({ error: "Order not found" });
 284          }
 285        }
 286  
 287        return res.json({ order });
 288      } catch (err) {
 289        next(err);
 290      }
 291    }
 292  );
 293  
 294  /**
 295   * POST /api/orders/:id/cancel
 296   * Cancels order and restores product stock inside a database transaction.
 297   * Customers can only cancel their own orders.
 298   */
 299  ordersRouter.post(
 300    "/:id/cancel",
 301    validate(orderIdParamSchema, "params"),
 302    validate(orderConfirmationSchema, "body"),
 303    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 304      try {
 305        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 306        const user = req.user!;
 307        const orderId = Number(req.params.id);
 308  
 309        const cancelledOrder = await sequelize.transaction(async (t) => {
 310          const order = (await Order.findOne({
 311            where: { id: orderId, storeId },
 312            transaction: t,
 313            lock: t.LOCK.UPDATE,
 314          })) as Order | null;
 315  
 316          if (!order) {
 317            const err = new Error("Order not found");
 318            (err as any).statusCode = 404;
 319            throw err;
 320          }
 321  
 322          // Ownership verification for customers
 323          if (user.role === "CUSTOMER") {
 324            const customer = await Customer.findOne({
 325              where: { storeId, email: user.email },
 326              transaction: t,
 327            });
 328  
 329            if (!customer || order.customerId !== customer.id) {
 330              const err = new Error("Order not found");
 331              (err as any).statusCode = 404;
 332              throw err;
 333            }
 334          }
 335  
 336          if (order.status === "CANCELLED") {
 337            const err = new Error("Order is already cancelled");
 338            (err as any).statusCode = 400;
 339            throw err;
 340          }
 341  
 342          if (order.status === "SHIPPED" || order.status === "DELIVERED") {
 343            const err = new Error(
 344              `Cannot cancel an order that has already been ${order.status.toLowerCase()}`
 345            );
 346            (err as any).statusCode = 400;
 347            throw err;
 348          }
 349  
 350          // Retrieve items for this order
 351          const items = await OrderItem.findAll({
 352            where: { orderId: order.id, storeId },
 353            transaction: t,
 354          });
 355  
 356          // Restore stock for all items
 357          for (const item of items) {
 358            const product = (await Product.findOne({
 359              where: { id: item.productId, storeId },
 360              transaction: t,
 361              lock: t.LOCK.UPDATE,
 362            })) as Product | null;
 363  
 364            if (product) {
 365              await product.increment("stock", {
 366                by: item.quantity,
 367                transaction: t,
 368              });
 369            }
 370          }
 371  
 372          await order.update({ status: "CANCELLED" }, { transaction: t });
 373  
 374          return order;
 375        });
 376  
 377        return res.json({
 378          message: "Order cancelled successfully and stock restored",
 379          order: cancelledOrder,
 380        });
 381      } catch (err: any) {
 382        if (err.statusCode) {
 383          return res.status(err.statusCode).json({ error: err.message });
 384        }
 385        next(err);
 386      }
 387    }
 388  );
 389  
 390  /**
 391   * PATCH /api/orders/:id/status
 392   * Update order status (ADMIN only).
 393   * If transitioning to CANCELLED, stock is safely restored in a transaction.
 394   */
 395  ordersRouter.patch(
 396    "/:id/status",
 397    requireRole("ADMIN"),
 398    validate(orderIdParamSchema, "params"),
 399    validate(updateOrderStatusSchema, "body"),
 400    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 401      try {
 402        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 403        const orderId = Number(req.params.id);
 404        const { status: newStatus } = req.body as UpdateOrderStatusInput;
 405  
 406        const updatedOrder = await sequelize.transaction(async (t) => {
 407          const order = (await Order.findOne({
 408            where: { id: orderId, storeId },
 409            transaction: t,
 410            lock: t.LOCK.UPDATE,
 411          })) as Order | null;
 412  
 413          if (!order) {
 414            const err = new Error("Order not found");
 415            (err as any).statusCode = 404;
 416            throw err;
 417          }
 418  
 419          // If transitioning from non-cancelled to CANCELLED, restore stock
 420          if (newStatus === "CANCELLED" && order.status !== "CANCELLED") {
 421            const items = await OrderItem.findAll({
 422              where: { orderId: order.id, storeId },
 423              transaction: t,
 424            });
 425  
 426            for (const item of items) {
 427              const product = (await Product.findOne({
 428                where: { id: item.productId, storeId },
 429                transaction: t,
 430                lock: t.LOCK.UPDATE,
 431              })) as Product | null;
 432  
 433              if (product) {
 434                await product.increment("stock", {
 435                  by: item.quantity,
 436                  transaction: t,
 437                });
 438              }
 439            }
 440          }
 441  
 442          await order.update({ status: newStatus }, { transaction: t });
 443          return order;
 444        });
 445  
 446        return res.json({
 447          message: `Order status updated to ${newStatus}`,
 448          order: updatedOrder,
 449        });
 450      } catch (err: any) {
 451        if (err.statusCode) {
 452          return res.status(err.statusCode).json({ error: err.message });
 453        }
 454        next(err);
 455      }
 456    }
 457  );
 458  
 459  export default ordersRouter;
backend/src/routes/products.ts

هاد المسار كيحول الفلاتر اللي فالواجهة لشروط استعلام محددة.

   1  import { Router, Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
   2  import { Op, WhereOptions } from "sequelize";
   3  import { Product, Category, forStore } from "../models";
   4  import {
   5    authenticate,
   6    injectTenant,
   7    requireRole,
   8    resolveStoreTenant,
   9    validate,
  10  } from "../middlewares";
  11  import {
  12    createProductSchema,
  13    productIdParamSchema,
  14    productQuerySchema,
  15    updateProductSchema,
  16    type ProductQueryInput,
  17  } from "../schemas/product";
  18  
  19  const productsRouter = Router();
  20  
  21  /**
  22   * GET /api/products
  23   * Search, filter by category/price, and paginate products.
  24   * Scoped strictly by storeId.
  25   */
  26  productsRouter.get(
  27    "/",
  28    resolveStoreTenant,
  29    validate(productQuerySchema, "query"),
  30    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  31      try {
  32        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
  33        const query = req.query as unknown as ProductQueryInput;
  34  
  35        const page = query.page || 1;
  36        const limit = query.limit || 20;
  37        const offset = (page - 1) * limit;
  38  
  39        const whereClause: any = {};
  40  
  41        // Keyword search on name or description (case-insensitive)
  42        const searchTerm = query.q || query.search;
  43        if (searchTerm) {
  44          whereClause[Op.or] = [
  45            { name: { [Op.iLike]: `%${searchTerm}%` } },
  46            { description: { [Op.iLike]: `%${searchTerm}%` } },
  47          ];
  48        }
  49  
  50        // Category filter
  51        if (query.categoryId) {
  52          whereClause.categoryId = query.categoryId;
  53        }
  54  
  55        // Price range filters
  56        if (query.minPrice !== undefined || query.maxPrice !== undefined) {
  57          const priceFilter: any = {};
  58          if (query.minPrice !== undefined) {
  59            priceFilter[Op.gte] = query.minPrice;
  60          }
  61          if (query.maxPrice !== undefined) {
  62            priceFilter[Op.lte] = query.maxPrice;
  63          }
  64          whereClause.price = priceFilter;
  65        }
  66  
  67        const scopedProduct = forStore(Product, storeId);
  68        const { rows: products, count: total } = await scopedProduct.findAndCountAll({
  69          where: whereClause,
  70          include: [
  71            {
  72              association: "category",
  73              attributes: ["id", "name"],
  74              required: false,
  75            },
  76          ],
  77          order: [["id", "ASC"]],
  78          limit,
  79          offset,
  80        });
  81  
  82        return res.json({
  83          storeId,
  84          products,
  85          pagination: {
  86            total,
  87            page,
  88            limit,
  89            totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit),
  90          },
  91        });
  92      } catch (err) {
  93        next(err);
  94      }
  95    }
  96  );
  97  
  98  /**
  99   * GET /api/products/:id/stock
 100   * Live stock and price directly from the database (never hallucinated).
 101   * Scoped by storeId.
 102   */
 103  productsRouter.get(
 104    "/:id/stock",
 105    resolveStoreTenant,
 106    validate(productIdParamSchema, "params"),
 107    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 108      try {
 109        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 110        const productId = Number(req.params.id);
 111  
 112        const product = (await forStore(Product, storeId).findOne({
 113          where: { id: productId },
 114          attributes: ["id", "name", "price", "stock"],
 115        })) as Product | null;
 116  
 117        if (!product) {
 118          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Product not found" });
 119        }
 120  
 121        return res.json({
 122          id: product.id,
 123          name: product.name,
 124          price: Number(product.price),
 125          stock: product.stock,
 126          inStock: product.stock > 0,
 127        });
 128      } catch (err) {
 129        next(err);
 130      }
 131    }
 132  );
 133  
 134  /**
 135   * GET /api/products/:id
 136   * Retrieve single product details with category.
 137   * Scoped by storeId.
 138   */
 139  productsRouter.get(
 140    "/:id",
 141    resolveStoreTenant,
 142    validate(productIdParamSchema, "params"),
 143    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 144      try {
 145        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 146        const productId = Number(req.params.id);
 147  
 148        const product = await forStore(Product, storeId).findOne({
 149          where: { id: productId },
 150          include: [
 151            {
 152              association: "category",
 153              attributes: ["id", "name", "description"],
 154              required: false,
 155            },
 156          ],
 157        });
 158  
 159        if (!product) {
 160          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Product not found" });
 161        }
 162  
 163        return res.json({ product });
 164      } catch (err) {
 165        next(err);
 166      }
 167    }
 168  );
 169  
 170  /**
 171   * POST /api/products
 172   * Create product (ADMIN only).
 173   * storeId injected server-side from JWT.
 174   */
 175  productsRouter.post(
 176    "/",
 177    authenticate,
 178    injectTenant,
 179    requireRole("ADMIN"),
 180    validate(createProductSchema, "body"),
 181    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 182      try {
 183        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 184        const { name, description, imageUrl, price, stock, sku, categoryId } = req.body;
 185  
 186        // Verify category belongs to this store if provided
 187        if (categoryId) {
 188          const category = await forStore(Category, storeId).findByPk(categoryId);
 189          if (!category) {
 190            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Category not found in this store" });
 191          }
 192        }
 193  
 194        // Check unique SKU per store if SKU provided
 195        if (sku) {
 196          const existingSku = await forStore(Product, storeId).findOne({
 197            where: { sku },
 198          });
 199          if (existingSku) {
 200            return res.status(409).json({ error: "SKU already exists in this store" });
 201          }
 202        }
 203  
 204        const product = await Product.create({
 205          name,
 206          description: description || null,
 207          imageUrl: imageUrl || null,
 208          price,
 209          stock: stock ?? 0,
 210          sku: sku || null,
 211          categoryId: categoryId || null,
 212          storeId,
 213        });
 214  
 215        return res.status(201).json({
 216          message: "Product created successfully",
 217          product,
 218        });
 219      } catch (err) {
 220        next(err);
 221      }
 222    }
 223  );
 224  
 225  /**
 226   * PUT /api/products/:id
 227   * Update product (ADMIN only).
 228   * Scoped by storeId.
 229   */
 230  productsRouter.put(
 231    "/:id",
 232    authenticate,
 233    injectTenant,
 234    requireRole("ADMIN"),
 235    validate(productIdParamSchema, "params"),
 236    validate(updateProductSchema, "body"),
 237    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 238      try {
 239        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 240        const productId = Number(req.params.id);
 241  
 242        const product = (await forStore(Product, storeId).findOne({
 243          where: { id: productId },
 244        })) as Product | null;
 245  
 246        if (!product) {
 247          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Product not found" });
 248        }
 249  
 250        // Verify category belongs to this store if categoryId is updated
 251        if (req.body.categoryId) {
 252          const category = await forStore(Category, storeId).findByPk(
 253            req.body.categoryId
 254          );
 255          if (!category) {
 256            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Category not found in this store" });
 257          }
 258        }
 259  
 260        // Check unique SKU if sku is updated and different
 261        if (req.body.sku && req.body.sku !== product.sku) {
 262          const existingSku = await forStore(Product, storeId).findOne({
 263            where: { sku: req.body.sku },
 264          });
 265          if (existingSku) {
 266            return res.status(409).json({ error: "SKU already exists in this store" });
 267          }
 268        }
 269  
 270        await product.update({
 271          ...(req.body.name !== undefined ? { name: req.body.name } : {}),
 272          ...(req.body.description !== undefined
 273            ? { description: req.body.description }
 274            : {}),
 275          ...(req.body.imageUrl !== undefined ? { imageUrl: req.body.imageUrl } : {}),
 276          ...(req.body.price !== undefined ? { price: req.body.price } : {}),
 277          ...(req.body.stock !== undefined ? { stock: req.body.stock } : {}),
 278          ...(req.body.sku !== undefined ? { sku: req.body.sku } : {}),
 279          ...(req.body.categoryId !== undefined
 280            ? { categoryId: req.body.categoryId }
 281            : {}),
 282        });
 283  
 284        return res.json({
 285          message: "Product updated successfully",
 286          product,
 287        });
 288      } catch (err) {
 289        next(err);
 290      }
 291    }
 292  );
 293  
 294  /**
 295   * DELETE /api/products/:id
 296   * Delete product (ADMIN only).
 297   * Scoped by storeId.
 298   */
 299  productsRouter.delete(
 300    "/:id",
 301    authenticate,
 302    injectTenant,
 303    requireRole("ADMIN"),
 304    validate(productIdParamSchema, "params"),
 305    async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
 306      try {
 307        const storeId = req.storeId as number;
 308        const productId = Number(req.params.id);
 309  
 310        const product = (await forStore(Product, storeId).findOne({
 311          where: { id: productId },
 312        })) as Product | null;
 313  
 314        if (!product) {
 315          return res.status(404).json({ error: "Product not found" });
 316        }
 317  
 318        await product.destroy();
 319  
 320        return res.json({ message: "Product deleted successfully" });
 321      } catch (err) {
 322        next(err);
 323      }
 324    }
 325  );
 326  
 327  export default productsRouter;
backend/src/schemas/agentTools.ts

هاد الطبقة كتحدد شنو يقدر المساعد يدير وبأي مدخلات.

   1  import { z } from "zod";
   2  
   3  /**
   4   * Zod schemas for the 8 ShopMate AI agent tools.
   5   * All arguments from LLM tool-calls MUST be strictly validated
   6   * against these schemas before any database or service execution.
   7   */
   8  
   9  // 1. searchProducts
  10  export const searchProductsToolSchema = z.object({
  11    query: z.string().trim().min(1, "Le mot-clé de recherche ne peut pas être vide").optional(),
  12    categoryId: z.coerce.number().int().positive("L'ID de catégorie doit être un entier positif").optional(),
  13    minPrice: z.coerce.number().min(0, "Le prix minimum doit être positif").optional(),
  14    maxPrice: z.coerce.number().min(0, "Le prix maximum doit être positif").optional(),
  15  });
  16  
  17  // 2. getProductDetails
  18  export const getProductDetailsToolSchema = z.object({
  19    productId: z.coerce.number().int().positive("L'identifiant du produit (productId) doit être un entier positif"),
  20  });
  21  
  22  // 3. checkStock
  23  export const checkStockToolSchema = z.object({
  24    productId: z.coerce.number().int().positive("L'identifiant du produit (productId) doit être un entier positif"),
  25  });
  26  
  27  // 4. createOrder (Requires explicit client confirmation)
  28  export const createOrderToolSchema = z.object({
  29    items: z
  30      .array(
  31        z.object({
  32          productId: z.coerce.number().int().positive("L'identifiant du produit doit être un entier positif"),
  33          quantity: z.coerce.number().int().min(1, "La quantité doit être au moins 1"),
  34        })
  35      )
  36      .min(1, "La commande doit contenir au moins un article"),
  37    customerName: z.string().trim().min(2, "Le nom du client est trop court").optional(),
  38    customerPhone: z.string().trim().min(6, "Numéro de téléphone invalide").optional(),
  39    shippingAddress: z.string().trim().min(5, "Adresse de livraison requise").optional(),
  40    confirmed: z.boolean().describe("Confirmation explicite requise du client avant de valider la commande"),
  41  });
  42  
  43  // 5. getOrderStatus
  44  export const getOrderStatusToolSchema = z.object({
  45    orderId: z.coerce.number().int().positive("L'identifiant de commande (orderId) doit être un entier positif"),
  46  });
  47  
  48  // 6. listMyOrders
  49  export const listMyOrdersToolSchema = z.object({
  50    limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(20).optional().default(5),
  51  });
  52  
  53  // 7. cancelOrder (Requires explicit client confirmation)
  54  export const cancelOrderToolSchema = z.object({
  55    orderId: z.coerce.number().int().positive("L'identifiant de commande (orderId) doit être un entier positif"),
  56    confirmed: z.boolean().describe("Confirmation explicite requise du client avant d'annuler la commande"),
  57  });
  58  
  59  // 8. getShipmentStatus (Via carrier / MCP)
  60  export const getShipmentStatusToolSchema = z
  61    .object({
  62      orderId: z.coerce.number().int().positive().optional(),
  63    })
  64    .describe("orderId est facultatif : si absent, le serveur retrouve la dernière commande du client connecté.");
  65  
  66  // Inferred TypeScript input types
  67  export type SearchProductsArgs = z.infer<typeof searchProductsToolSchema>;
  68  export type GetProductDetailsArgs = z.infer<typeof getProductDetailsToolSchema>;
  69  export type CheckStockArgs = z.infer<typeof checkStockToolSchema>;
  70  export type CreateOrderArgs = z.infer<typeof createOrderToolSchema>;
  71  export type GetOrderStatusArgs = z.infer<typeof getOrderStatusToolSchema>;
  72  export type ListMyOrdersArgs = z.infer<typeof listMyOrdersToolSchema>;
  73  export type CancelOrderArgs = z.infer<typeof cancelOrderToolSchema>;
  74  export type GetShipmentStatusArgs = z.infer<typeof getShipmentStatusToolSchema>;
