mobile/app/(auth)/register.tsx

ملف مساعد داخل (auth). قرا declarations والمقتطف باش تربط الدور ديالو بالمسار المشروح قبل.

   1  import { useState } from "react";
   2  import {
   3    ActivityIndicator,
   4    KeyboardAvoidingView,
   5    Platform,
   6    Pressable,
   7    ScrollView,
   8    StyleSheet,
   9    Text,
  10    TextInput,
  11    View,
  12  } from "react-native";
  13  import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
  14  import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
  15  import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/stores/authStore";
  16  import {
  17    colorTokens,
  18    radiusTokens,
  19    spacingTokens,
  20    typographyTokens,
  21  } from "@/src/theme/tokens";
  22  
  23  export default function RegisterScreen() {
  24    const router = useRouter();
  25    const register = useAuthStore((state) => state.register);
  26    const isLoading = useAuthStore((state) => state.isLoading);
  27    const authError = useAuthStore((state) => state.error);
  28    const clearError = useAuthStore((state) => state.clearError);
  29  
  30    const [name, setName] = useState("");
  31    const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  32    const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  33    const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  34    const [clientError, setClientError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  35  
  36    const validate = (): boolean => {
  37      setClientError(null);
  38      clearError();
  39  
  40      if (!name.trim() || name.trim().length < 2) {
  41        setClientError("Le nom complet doit comporter au moins 2 caractères.");
  42        return false;
  43      }
  44  
  45      if (!email.trim()) {
  46        setClientError("L'adresse email est requise.");
  47        return false;
  48      }
  49  
  50      const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
  51      if (!emailRegex.test(email.trim())) {
  52        setClientError("Format d'adresse email invalide.");
  53        return false;
  54      }
  55  
  56      if (!password || password.length < 6) {
  57        setClientError("Le mot de passe doit comporter au moins 6 caractères.");
  58        return false;
  59      }
  60  
  61      if (password !== confirmPassword) {
  62        setClientError("Les mots de passe ne correspondent pas.");
  63        return false;
  64      }
  65  
  66      return true;
  67    };
  68  
  69    const handleRegister = async () => {
  70      if (!validate()) return;
  71  
  72      const success = await register(
  73        name.trim(),
  74        email.trim().toLowerCase(),
  75        password
  76      );
  77  
  78      if (success) {
  79        router.replace("/(tabs)");
  80      }
  81    };
  82  
  83    const errorMessage = clientError || authError;
  84  
  85    return (
  86      <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea}>
  87        <KeyboardAvoidingView
  88          behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : "height"}
  89          style={styles.keyboardContainer}
  90        >
  91          <ScrollView
  92            contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
  93            keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
  94          >
  95            {/* Header & Logo */}
  96            <View style={styles.header}>
  97              <View style={styles.logoBadge}>
  98                <Text style={styles.logoText}>✨</Text>
  99              </View>
 100              <Text style={styles.title}>Rejoindre ShopMate AI</Text>
 101              <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
 102                Créez votre compte pour commander facilement en paiement à la livraison
 103              </Text>
 104            </View>
 105  
 106            {/* Error Banner */}
 107            {errorMessage ? (
 108              <View style={styles.errorContainer}>
 109                <Text style={styles.errorIcon}>⚠️</Text>
 110                <Text style={styles.errorText}>{errorMessage}</Text>
 111              </View>
 112            ) : null}
 113  
 114            {/* Form */}
 115            <View style={styles.form}>
 116              <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
 117                <Text style={styles.label}>Nom complet</Text>
 118                <TextInput
 119                  style={styles.input}
 120                  placeholder="ex. Omar Fahid"
 121                  placeholderTextColor={colorTokens.muted}
 122                  value={name}
 123                  onChangeText={(text) => {
 124                    setName(text);
 125                    if (clientError) setClientError(null);
 126                  }}
 127                  autoCapitalize="words"
 128                  autoComplete="name"
 129                />
 130              </View>
 131  
 132              <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
 133                <Text style={styles.label}>Adresse Email</Text>
 134                <TextInput
 135                  style={styles.input}
 136                  placeholder="votre@email.com"
 137                  placeholderTextColor={colorTokens.muted}
 138                  value={email}
 139                  onChangeText={(text) => {
 140                    setEmail(text);
 141                    if (clientError) setClientError(null);
 142                  }}
 143                  autoCapitalize="none"
 144                  keyboardType="email-address"
 145                  autoComplete="email"
 146                />
 147              </View>
 148  
 149              <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
 150                <Text style={styles.label}>Mot de passe</Text>
 151                <TextInput
 152                  style={styles.input}
 153                  placeholder="Au moins 6 caractères"
 154                  placeholderTextColor={colorTokens.muted}
 155                  value={password}
 156                  onChangeText={(text) => {
 157                    setPassword(text);
 158                    if (clientError) setClientError(null);
 159                  }}
 160                  secureTextEntry
 161                  autoCapitalize="none"
 162                />
 163              </View>
 164  
 165              <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
 166                <Text style={styles.label}>Confirmer le mot de passe</Text>
 167                <TextInput
 168                  style={styles.input}
 169                  placeholder="Répétez votre mot de passe"
 170                  placeholderTextColor={colorTokens.muted}
 171                  value={confirmPassword}
 172                  onChangeText={(text) => {
 173                    setConfirmPassword(text);
 174                    if (clientError) setClientError(null);
 175                  }}
 176                  secureTextEntry
 177                  autoCapitalize="none"
 178                />
 179              </View>
 180  
 181              {/* Submit Button */}
 182              <Pressable
 183                style={({ pressed }) => [
 184                  styles.submitButton,
 185                  pressed && styles.submitButtonPressed,
 186                  isLoading && styles.submitButtonDisabled,
 187                ]}
 188                onPress={handleRegister}
 189                disabled={isLoading}
 190              >
 191                {isLoading ? (
 192                  <ActivityIndicator color={colorTokens.surface} />
 193                ) : (
 194                  <Text style={styles.submitButtonText}>Créer mon compte</Text>
 195                )}
 196              </Pressable>
 197            </View>
 198  
 199            {/* Switch to Login */}
 200            <View style={styles.footer}>
 201              <Text style={styles.footerText}>Vous avez déjà un compte ?</Text>
 202              <Pressable
 203                onPress={() => {
 204                  clearError();
 205                  router.push("/(auth)/login");
 206                }}
 207              >
 208                <Text style={styles.footerLink}>Se connecter</Text>
 209              </Pressable>
 210            </View>
 211          </ScrollView>
 212        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
 213      </SafeAreaView>
 214    );
 215  }
 216

 218    safeArea: {
 219      flex: 1,
 220      backgroundColor: colorTokens.surface,
 221    },

 223      flex: 1,

mobile/app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx

Expo Router كيربط بنية الملفات بالشاشات، وRootLayout كيتابع حالة الجلسة.

   1  import { Tabs } from "expo-router";
   2  import { Platform, Text, View } from "react-native";
   3  import { colorTokens } from "@/src/theme/tokens";
   4  
   5  export default function TabLayout() {
   6    return (
   7      <Tabs
   8        screenOptions={{
   9          headerShown: false,

mobile/app/(tabs)/admin-agent-logs.tsx

كل شاشة كتراجع ADMIN فالواجهة وكتتواصل مع endpoints محمية فالخادم.

   1  import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
   2  import {
   3    ActivityIndicator,
   4    Pressable,
   5    ScrollView,
   6    StyleSheet,
   7    Text,
   8    TextInput,
   9    View,
  10  } from "react-native";
  11  import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
  12  import { router } from "expo-router";
  13  import { apiClient } from "@/src/api/client";
  14  import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/stores/authStore";
  15  import { colorTokens, radiusTokens, spacingTokens } from "@/src/theme/tokens";
  16  import { BrandHeader } from "@/src/components/BrandHeader";
  17  
  18  type Disposition = "all" | "refused" | "blocked";
  19  type Customer = { id: number; name: string; email: string } | null;
  20  type AgentLog = {
  21    id: number;
  22    action: string;
  23    status: "success" | "error";
  24    conversationId: number | null;
  25    messageId: number | null;
  26    input: unknown;
  27    output: unknown;
  28    toolsCalled: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  29    ragSources: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  30    tokenUsage: { promptTokens: number; completionTokens: number; totalTokens: number } | null;
  31    latencyMs: number | null;
  32    refusalCategory: string | null;
  33    errorMessage: string | null;
  34    createdAt: string;
  35    customer?: Customer;
  36  };
  37  
  38  const PAGE_SIZE = 25;
  39  
  40  export default function AdminAgentLogsScreen() {
  41    const role = useAuthStore((state) => state.user?.role);
  42    const [logs, setLogs] = useState<AgentLog[]>([]);
  43    const [selected, setSelected] = useState<AgentLog | null>(null);
  44    const [customerIdDraft, setCustomerIdDraft] = useState("");
  45    const [fromDateDraft, setFromDateDraft] = useState("");
  46    const [toDateDraft, setToDateDraft] = useState("");
  47    const [dispositionDraft, setDispositionDraft] = useState<Disposition>("all");
  48    const [filters, setFilters] = useState({ customerId: "", fromDate: "", toDate: "", disposition: "all" as Disposition });
  49    const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  50    const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(1);
  51    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  52    const [loadingDetail, setLoadingDetail] = useState(false);
  53    const [error, setError] = useState("");
  54  
  55    const loadLogs = useCallback(async (requestedPage = 1, append = false) => {
  56      setLoading(true);
  57      setError("");
  58      try {
  59        const params: Record<string, string | number> = { page: requestedPage, limit: PAGE_SIZE };
  60        if (filters.customerId) params.customerId = Number(filters.customerId);
  61        if (filters.fromDate) params.fromDate = filters.fromDate;
  62        if (filters.toDate) params.toDate = filters.toDate;
  63        if (filters.disposition !== "all") params.disposition = filters.disposition;
  64        const response = await apiClient.get<{
  65          logs: AgentLog[];
  66          pagination: { totalPages: number; page: number };
  67        }>("/api/agent/logs", { params });
  68        setLogs((current) => append ? [...current, ...response.data.logs] : response.data.logs);
  69        setPage(response.data.pagination.page);
  70        setTotalPages(response.data.pagination.totalPages);
  71      } catch (err: any) {
  72        setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible de charger le journal de l’agent.");
  73      } finally {
  74        setLoading(false);
  75      }
  76    }, [filters]);
  77  
  78    useEffect(() => {
  79      if (role !== "ADMIN") {
  80        router.replace("/(tabs)");
  81        return;
  82      }
  83      void loadLogs();
  84    }, [role, loadLogs]);
  85  
  86    const applyFilters = () => {
  87      const customerId = customerIdDraft.trim();
  88      if (customerId && (!/^\d+$/.test(customerId) || Number(customerId) < 1)) {
  89        setError("L’identifiant client doit être un nombre positif.");
  90        return;
  91      }
  92      const validDate = (value: string) => !value || (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value) && !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(`${value}T00:00:00Z`)));
  93      if (!validDate(fromDateDraft) || !validDate(toDateDraft)) {
  94        setError("Utilisez le format de date AAAA-MM-JJ.");
  95        return;
  96      }
  97      if (fromDateDraft && toDateDraft && fromDateDraft > toDateDraft) {
  98        setError("La date de début doit précéder la date de fin.");
  99        return;
 100      }
 101      setError("");
 102      setSelected(null);
 103      setFilters({ customerId, fromDate: fromDateDraft, toDate: toDateDraft, disposition: dispositionDraft });
 104    };
 105  
 106    const showDetail = async (log: AgentLog) => {
 107      setLoadingDetail(true);
 108      setError("");
 109      try {
 110        const response = await apiClient.get<{ log: AgentLog; customer: Customer }>(`/api/agent/logs/${log.id}`);
 111        setSelected({ ...response.data.log, customer: response.data.customer });
 112      } catch (err: any) {
 113        setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible de charger le détail de ce tour.");
 114      } finally {
 115        setLoadingDetail(false);
 116      }
 117    };
 118  
 119    if (role !== "ADMIN") return null;
 120  
 121    return (
 122      <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
 123        <BrandHeader />
 124        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
 125          <Pressable onPress={() => selected ? setSelected(null) : router.back()}>
 126            <Text style={styles.back}>‹ {selected ? "Journal" : "Profil"}</Text>
 127          </Pressable>
 128          {selected ? (
 129            <TurnDetail log={selected} />
 130          ) : (
 131            <>
 132              <Text style={styles.title}>Journal de l’assistant IA</Text>
 133              <Text style={styles.subtitle}>Trace des interactions de votre boutique uniquement.</Text>
 134              <View style={styles.filterCard}>
 135                <Text style={styles.filterTitle}>Filtres</Text>
 136                <Text style={styles.label}>ID client</Text>
 137                <TextInput value={customerIdDraft} onChangeText={setCustomerIdDraft} keyboardType="number-pad" placeholder="Tous les clients" placeholderTextColor={colorTokens.muted} style={styles.input} />
 138                <View style={styles.row}>
 139                  <View style={styles.dateField}><Text style={styles.label}>Du</Text><TextInput value={fromDateDraft} onChangeText={setFromDateDraft} placeholder="AAAA-MM-JJ" placeholderTextColor={colorTokens.muted} style={styles.input} /></View>
 140                  <View style={styles.dateField}><Text style={styles.label}>Au</Text><TextInput value={toDateDraft} onChangeText={setToDateDraft} placeholder="AAAA-MM-JJ" placeholderTextColor={colorTokens.muted} style={styles.input} /></View>
 141                </View>
 142                <Text style={styles.label}>Résultat</Text>
 143                <View style={styles.chips}>
 144                  <FilterChip label="Tout" active={dispositionDraft === "all"} onPress={() => setDispositionDraft("all")} />
 145                  <FilterChip label="Refusé" active={dispositionDraft === "refused"} onPress={() => setDispositionDraft("refused")} />
 146                  <FilterChip label="Bloqué" active={dispositionDraft === "blocked"} onPress={() => setDispositionDraft("blocked")} />
 147                </View>
 148                <Pressable onPress={applyFilters} style={styles.applyButton}><Text style={styles.applyText}>Appliquer les filtres</Text></Pressable>
 149              </View>
 150              {error ? <Text accessibilityRole="alert" style={styles.error}>{error}</Text> : null}
 151              {loadingDetail ? <ActivityIndicator color={colorTokens.primary} /> : null}
 152              <Text style={styles.resultCount}>{logs.length} tour(s) sur la page</Text>
 153              {loading && logs.length === 0 ? <ActivityIndicator color={colorTokens.primary} style={styles.loader} /> : logs.length === 0 ? (
 154                <Text style={styles.empty}>Aucune interaction trouvée avec ces filtres.</Text>
 155              ) : logs.map((log) => <LogCard key={log.id} log={log} onPress={() => void showDetail(log)} />)}
 156              {page < totalPages ? <Pressable disabled={loading} onPress={() => void loadLogs(page + 1, true)} style={styles.moreButton}>
 157                {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color={colorTokens.primary} /> : <Text style={styles.moreText}>Charger la page suivante</Text>}
 158              </Pressable> : null}
 159            </>
 160          )}
 161          {selected && error ? <Text accessibilityRole="alert" style={styles.error}>{error}</Text> : null}
 162        </ScrollView>
 163      </SafeAreaView>
 164    );
 165  }
 166  
 167  function LogCard({ log, onPress }: { log: AgentLog; onPress: () => void }) {
 168    const question = displayValue(log.input) || "Question indisponible";
 169    return (
 170      <Pressable onPress={onPress} style={({ pressed }) => [styles.logCard, pressed && styles.pressed]}>
 171        <View style={styles.logHeader}><Text style={styles.logCustomer}>{log.customer?.name || "Client"}{log.customer?.id ? ` · #${log.customer.id}` : ""}</Text><Badge log={log} /></View>
 172        <Text style={styles.question} numberOfLines={2}>{question}</Text>
 173        <Text style={styles.logMeta}>{formatDate(log.createdAt)} · {log.latencyMs ?? "—"} ms · {log.tokenUsage?.totalTokens ?? "—"} tokens</Text>
 174        <Text style={styles.openDetail}>Voir la trace complète ›</Text>
 175      </Pressable>
 176    );
 177  }
 178  
 179  function TurnDetail({ log }: { log: AgentLog }) {
 180    return (
 181      <>
 182        <Text style={styles.title}>Détail du tour #{log.id}</Text>
 183        <View style={styles.detailCard}>
 184          <Badge log={log} />
 185          <Text style={styles.detailMeta}>{formatDate(log.createdAt)} · conversation #{log.conversationId ?? "—"}</Text>
 186          <Text style={styles.customer}>{log.customer?.name || "Client inconnu"}{log.customer?.email ? ` · ${log.customer.email}` : ""} {log.customer?.id ? `(ID ${log.customer.id})` : ""}</Text>
 187          <DetailSection title="Question" value={log.input} />
 188          <DetailSection title="Réponse" value={log.output} />
 189          {log.refusalCategory ? <DetailSection title="Refus / blocage" value={log.refusalCategory} /> : null}
 190          <DetailSection title={`Outils appelés (${log.toolsCalled?.length ?? 0})`} value={log.toolsCalled} />
 191          <DetailSection title={`Sources RAG (${log.ragSources?.length ?? 0})`} value={log.ragSources} />
 192          <DetailSection title="Tokens (entrée / sortie / total)" value={log.tokenUsage ? `${log.tokenUsage.promptTokens} / ${log.tokenUsage.completionTokens} / ${log.tokenUsage.totalTokens}` : "Non disponible"} />
 193          <DetailSection title="Latence" value={log.latencyMs === null ? "Non disponible" : `${log.latencyMs} ms`} />
 194          {log.errorMessage ? <DetailSection title="Erreur" value={log.errorMessage} /> : null}
 195          <DetailSection title="Action" value={log.action} />
 196          <DetailSection title="Message ID" value={log.messageId ?? "Non disponible"} />
 197        </View>
 198      </>
 199    );
 200  }
 201  
 202  function DetailSection({ title, value }: { title: string; value: unknown }) {
 203    return <View style={styles.detailSection}><Text style={styles.detailLabel}>{title}</Text><Text selectable style={styles.detailValue}>{displayValue(value) || "Non disponible"}</Text></View>;
 204  }
 205  
 206  function Badge({ log }: { log: AgentLog }) {
 207    const kind = log.refusalCategory === "prompt_injection" || log.refusalCategory === "output_moderation" ? "blocked" : log.refusalCategory ? "refused" : "normal";
 208    const label = kind === "blocked" ? "Bloqué" : kind === "refused" ? "Refusé" : log.status === "error" ? "Erreur" : "Répondu";
 209    return <View style={[styles.badge, kind === "blocked" ? styles.blockedBadge : kind === "refused" || log.status === "error" ? styles.refusedBadge : styles.normalBadge]}><Text style={styles.badgeText}>{label}</Text></View>;
 210  }
 211  
 212  function FilterChip({ label, active, onPress }: { label: string; active: boolean; onPress: () => void }) {
 213    return <Pressable onPress={onPress} style={[styles.chip, active && styles.activeChip]}><Text style={[styles.chipText, active && styles.activeChipText]}>{label}</Text></Pressable>;
 214  }
 215  
 216  function displayValue(value: unknown): string {
 217    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return "";
 218    if (typeof value === "string") return value;
 219    return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);
 220  }
 221  
 222  function formatDate(value: string): string {
 223    const date = new Date(value);
 224    return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? value : date.toLocaleString();
 225  }
 226  
 227  const styles = StyleSheet.create({
 228    screen: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colorTokens.surface }, content: { padding: spacingTokens.md, paddingBottom: spacingTokens.xxl },
 229    back: { color: colorTokens.primary, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: "700", marginBottom: spacingTokens.md }, title: { color: colorTokens.ink, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: "800" },
 230    subtitle: { color: colorTokens.muted, fontSize: 13, marginTop: 5, marginBottom: spacingTokens.md },
 231    filterCard: { padding: spacingTokens.md, borderRadius: radiusTokens.lg, backgroundColor: colorTokens.surfaceContainerLow, marginBottom: spacingTokens.md },
 232    filterTitle: { color: colorTokens.ink, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: "800", marginBottom: spacingTokens.sm }, label: { color: colorTokens.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: "700", marginBottom: 5 },
 233    input: { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colorTokens.border, borderRadius: radiusTokens.md, paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 9, color: colorTokens.ink, backgroundColor: colorTokens.surfaceContainerLowest, marginBottom: spacingTokens.sm },

mobile/app/(tabs)/admin-catalogue.tsx

الفورمولير كيحتافظ بالنصوص، وكيحول الأرقام قبل الإرسال.

   1  import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
   2  import {
   3    ActivityIndicator,
   4    Alert,
   5    Pressable,
   6    ScrollView,
   7    StyleSheet,
   8    Text,
   9    TextInput,
  10    View,
  11  } from "react-native";
  12  import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
  13  import { router } from "expo-router";
  14  import { apiClient } from "@/src/api/client";
  15  import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/stores/authStore";
  16  import { Category, Product } from "@/src/stores/catalogStore";
  17  import { Button } from "@/src/components/Button";
  18  import { BrandHeader } from "@/src/components/BrandHeader";
  19  import { colorTokens, radiusTokens, spacingTokens } from "@/src/theme/tokens";
  20  
  21  type CatalogTab = "products" | "categories";
  22  type ProductDraft = { name: string; description: string; price: string; stock: string; sku: string; categoryId: number | null };
  23  type CategoryDraft = { name: string; description: string };
  24  
  25  const emptyProduct: ProductDraft = { name: "", description: "", price: "", stock: "0", sku: "", categoryId: null };
  26  
  27  export default function AdminCatalogScreen() {
  28    const role = useAuthStore((state) => state.user?.role);
  29    const [tab, setTab] = useState<CatalogTab>("products");
  30    const [products, setProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  31    const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>([]);
  32    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  33    const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  34    const [error, setError] = useState("");
  35    const [productDraft, setProductDraft] = useState<ProductDraft>(emptyProduct);
  36    const [editingProduct, setEditingProduct] = useState<number | null>(null);
  37    const [categoryDraft, setCategoryDraft] = useState<CategoryDraft>({ name: "", description: "" });
  38    const [editingCategory, setEditingCategory] = useState<number | null>(null);
  39  
  40    const loadCatalogue = useCallback(async () => {
  41      setLoading(true);
  42      setError("");
  43      try {
  44        const [productResponse, categoryResponse] = await Promise.all([
  45          apiClient.get<{ products: Product[] }>("/api/products", { params: { limit: 100 } }),
  46          apiClient.get<{ categories: Category[] }>("/api/categories"),
  47        ]);
  48        setProducts(productResponse.data.products || []);
  49        setCategories(categoryResponse.data.categories || []);
  50      } catch (err: any) {
  51        setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible de charger le catalogue.");
  52      } finally {
  53        setLoading(false);
  54      }
  55    }, []);
  56  
  57    useEffect(() => {
  58      if (role !== "ADMIN") {
  59        router.replace("/(tabs)");
  60        return;
  61      }
  62      void loadCatalogue();
  63    }, [role, loadCatalogue]);
  64  
  65    const categoryNames = useMemo(() => new Map(categories.map((category) => [category.id, category.name])), [categories]);
  66  
  67    const saveProduct = async () => {
  68      const price = Number(productDraft.price);
  69      const stock = Number(productDraft.stock);
  70      if (!productDraft.name.trim()) return setError("Le nom du produit est obligatoire.");
  71      if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price <= 0) return setError("Le prix doit être un nombre supérieur à zéro.");
  72      if (!Number.isInteger(stock) || stock < 0) return setError("Le stock doit être un entier positif ou zéro.");
  73      setSaving(true);
  74      setError("");
  75      try {
  76        const payload = {
  77          name: productDraft.name.trim(), description: productDraft.description.trim() || null,
  78          price, stock, sku: productDraft.sku.trim() || null, categoryId: productDraft.categoryId,
  79        };
  80        if (editingProduct) await apiClient.put(`/api/products/${editingProduct}`, payload);
  81        else await apiClient.post("/api/products", payload);
  82        setProductDraft(emptyProduct);
  83        setEditingProduct(null);
  84        await loadCatalogue();
  85      } catch (err: any) {
  86        setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible d'enregistrer le produit.");
  87      } finally { setSaving(false); }
  88    };
  89  
  90    const editProduct = (product: Product) => {
  91      setEditingProduct(product.id);
  92      setProductDraft({
  93        name: product.name, description: product.description || "", price: String(product.price),
  94        stock: String(product.stock), sku: product.sku || "", categoryId: product.categoryId || null,
  95      });
  96      setError("");
  97    };
  98  
  99    const deleteProduct = (product: Product) => Alert.alert(
 100      "Supprimer le produit ?", `« ${product.name} » sera retiré du catalogue.`,
 101      [{ text: "Annuler", style: "cancel" }, { text: "Supprimer", style: "destructive", onPress: async () => {
 102        try { await apiClient.delete(`/api/products/${product.id}`); await loadCatalogue(); }
 103        catch (err: any) { setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible de supprimer le produit."); }
 104      } }]
 105    );
 106  
 107    const saveCategory = async () => {
 108      if (!categoryDraft.name.trim()) return setError("Le nom de la catégorie est obligatoire.");
 109      setSaving(true);
 110      setError("");
 111      try {
 112        const payload = { name: categoryDraft.name.trim(), description: categoryDraft.description.trim() || null };
 113        if (editingCategory) await apiClient.put(`/api/categories/${editingCategory}`, payload);
 114        else await apiClient.post("/api/categories", payload);
 115        setCategoryDraft({ name: "", description: "" });
 116        setEditingCategory(null);
 117        await loadCatalogue();
 118      } catch (err: any) {
 119        setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible d'enregistrer la catégorie.");
 120      } finally { setSaving(false); }
 121    };
 122  
 123    const deleteCategory = (category: Category) => Alert.alert(
 124      "Supprimer la catégorie ?", `« ${category.name} » sera supprimée.`,
 125      [{ text: "Annuler", style: "cancel" }, { text: "Supprimer", style: "destructive", onPress: async () => {
 126        try { await apiClient.delete(`/api/categories/${category.id}`); await loadCatalogue(); }
 127        catch (err: any) { setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible de supprimer la catégorie."); }
 128      } }]
 129    );
 130  
 131    if (role !== "ADMIN") return null;
 132  
 133    return (
 134      <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
 135        <BrandHeader />
 136        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.content} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
 137          <View style={styles.header}>
 138            <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()}><Text style={styles.back}>‹ Profil</Text></Pressable>
 139            <Text style={styles.title}>Gestion du catalogue</Text>
 140            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>Les changements sont partagés avec les clients de votre boutique.</Text>
 141          </View>
 142          <View style={styles.tabs}>
 143            <TabButton label="Produits" active={tab === "products"} onPress={() => { setTab("products"); setError(""); }} />
 144            <TabButton label="Catégories" active={tab === "categories"} onPress={() => { setTab("categories"); setError(""); }} />
 145          </View>
 146          {error ? <Text accessibilityRole="alert" style={styles.error}>{error}</Text> : null}
 147          {tab === "products" ? (
 148            <>
 149              <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>{editingProduct ? "Modifier le produit" : "Ajouter un produit"}</Text>
 150              <Field label="Nom *" value={productDraft.name} onChangeText={(name) => setProductDraft({ ...productDraft, name })} />
 151              <Field label="Description" value={productDraft.description} onChangeText={(description) => setProductDraft({ ...productDraft, description })} multiline />
 152              <View style={styles.row}>
 153                <Field label="Prix (DH) *" value={productDraft.price} onChangeText={(price) => setProductDraft({ ...productDraft, price })} keyboardType="decimal-pad" style={styles.half} />
 154                <Field label="Stock *" value={productDraft.stock} onChangeText={(stock) => setProductDraft({ ...productDraft, stock })} keyboardType="number-pad" style={styles.half} />
 155              </View>
 156              <Field label="SKU (optionnel)" value={productDraft.sku} onChangeText={(sku) => setProductDraft({ ...productDraft, sku })} />
 157              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>Catégorie</Text>
 158              <View style={styles.chips}>
 159                <Chip label="Aucune" active={productDraft.categoryId === null} onPress={() => setProductDraft({ ...productDraft, categoryId: null })} />
 160                {categories.map((category) => <Chip key={category.id} label={category.name} active={productDraft.categoryId === category.id} onPress={() => setProductDraft({ ...productDraft, categoryId: category.id })} />)}
 161              </View>
 162              <Button title={saving ? "Enregistrement…" : editingProduct ? "Enregistrer" : "Créer le produit"} onPress={() => void saveProduct()} disabled={saving} />
 163              {editingProduct ? <TextButton label="Annuler la modification" onPress={() => { setEditingProduct(null); setProductDraft(emptyProduct); }} /> : null}
 164              <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Produits ({products.length})</Text>
 165              {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color={colorTokens.primary} /> : products.map((product) => (
 166                <View key={product.id} style={styles.item}>
 167                  <View style={styles.itemInfo}>
 168                    <Text style={styles.itemTitle}>{product.name}</Text>
 169                    <Text style={styles.itemMeta}>{Number(product.price).toFixed(2)} DH · Stock {product.stock}{product.categoryId ? ` · ${categoryNames.get(product.categoryId) || "Catégorie"}` : ""}</Text>
 170                  </View>
 171                  <TextButton label="Modifier" onPress={() => editProduct(product)} />
 172                  <TextButton label="Supprimer" danger onPress={() => deleteProduct(product)} />
 173                </View>
 174              ))}
 175            </>
 176          ) : (
 177            <>
 178              <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>{editingCategory ? "Modifier la catégorie" : "Ajouter une catégorie"}</Text>
 179              <Field label="Nom *" value={categoryDraft.name} onChangeText={(name) => setCategoryDraft({ ...categoryDraft, name })} />
 180              <Field label="Description" value={categoryDraft.description} onChangeText={(description) => setCategoryDraft({ ...categoryDraft, description })} multiline />
 181              <Button title={saving ? "Enregistrement…" : editingCategory ? "Enregistrer" : "Créer la catégorie"} onPress={() => void saveCategory()} disabled={saving} />
 182              {editingCategory ? <TextButton label="Annuler la modification" onPress={() => { setEditingCategory(null); setCategoryDraft({ name: "", description: "" }); }} /> : null}
 183              <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Catégories ({categories.length})</Text>
 184              {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color={colorTokens.primary} /> : categories.map((category) => (
 185                <View key={category.id} style={styles.item}>
 186                  <View style={styles.itemInfo}><Text style={styles.itemTitle}>{category.name}</Text><Text style={styles.itemMeta}>{category.description || ""}</Text></View>
 187                  <TextButton label="Modifier" onPress={() => { setEditingCategory(category.id); setCategoryDraft({ name: category.name, description: category.description || "" }); setError(""); }} />
 188                  <TextButton label="Supprimer" danger onPress={() => deleteCategory(category)} />
 189                </View>
 190              ))}
 191            </>
 192          )}
 193        </ScrollView>
 194      </SafeAreaView>
 195    );
 196  }
 197  
 198  function Field({ label, value, onChangeText, multiline, keyboardType, style }: {
 199    label: string; value: string; onChangeText: (value: string) => void; multiline?: boolean;
 200    keyboardType?: "decimal-pad" | "number-pad"; style?: object;
 201  }) {
 202    return <View style={[styles.fieldWrap, style]}><Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>{label}</Text><TextInput
 203      value={value} onChangeText={onChangeText} multiline={multiline} keyboardType={keyboardType}
 204      style={[styles.input, multiline && styles.multiline]} placeholderTextColor={colorTokens.muted}
 205    /></View>;
 206  }
 207  
 208  function TabButton({ label, active, onPress }: { label: string; active: boolean; onPress: () => void }) {
 209    return <Pressable onPress={onPress} style={[styles.tab, active && styles.activeTab]}><Text style={[styles.tabText, active && styles.activeTabText]}>{label}</Text></Pressable>;
 210  }
 211  
 212  function Chip({ label, active, onPress }: { label: string; active: boolean; onPress: () => void }) {
 213    return <Pressable onPress={onPress} style={[styles.chip, active && styles.activeChip]}><Text style={[styles.chipText, active && styles.activeChipText]}>{label}</Text></Pressable>;
 214  }
 215  
 216  function TextButton({ label, onPress, danger }: { label: string; onPress: () => void; danger?: boolean }) {
 217    return <Pressable onPress={onPress} style={styles.textButton}><Text style={[styles.textButtonLabel, danger && styles.dangerText]}>{label}</Text></Pressable>;
 218  }
 219  
 220  const styles = StyleSheet.create({
 221    screen: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colorTokens.surface },
 222    content: { padding: spacingTokens.md, paddingBottom: spacingTokens.xxl, gap: spacingTokens.sm },
 223    header: { marginBottom: spacingTokens.sm }, back: { color: colorTokens.primary, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: "700", marginBottom: spacingTokens.md },
 224    title: { color: colorTokens.ink, fontSize: 25, fontWeight: "800" }, subtitle: { color: colorTokens.muted, fontSize: 13, marginTop: 5 },
 225    tabs: { flexDirection: "row", backgroundColor: colorTokens.surfaceContainer, borderRadius: radiusTokens.lg, padding: 4 },
 226    tab: { flex: 1, paddingVertical: 10, alignItems: "center", borderRadius: radiusTokens.md }, activeTab: { backgroundColor: colorTokens.surfaceContainerLowest },
 227    tabText: { color: colorTokens.muted, fontWeight: "700" }, activeTabText: { color: colorTokens.primary },
 228    sectionTitle: { color: colorTokens.ink, fontSize: 17, fontWeight: "800", marginTop: spacingTokens.md, marginBottom: spacingTokens.xs },
 229    fieldWrap: { marginBottom: spacingTokens.xs }, fieldLabel: { color: colorTokens.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: "700", marginBottom: 5 },
 230    input: { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colorTokens.border, borderRadius: radiusTokens.md, paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 10, color: colorTokens.ink, backgroundColor: colorTokens.surfaceContainerLowest, minHeight: 44 },
 231    multiline: { minHeight: 76, textAlignVertical: "top" }, row: { flexDirection: "row", gap: spacingTokens.sm }, half: { flex: 1 },
 232    chips: { flexDirection: "row", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 8, marginBottom: spacingTokens.md }, chip: { paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 8, borderRadius: radiusTokens.full, backgroundColor: colorTokens.surfaceContainer },
 233    activeChip: { backgroundColor: colorTokens.primary }, chipText: { color: colorTokens.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: "600" }, activeChipText: { color: colorTokens.onPrimary },
 234    item: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, padding: 12, backgroundColor: colorTokens.surfaceContainerLowest, borderRadius: radiusTokens.md, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colorTokens.border, marginBottom: 8 },
 235    itemInfo: { flex: 1 }, itemTitle: { color: colorTokens.ink, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: "700" }, itemMeta: { color: colorTokens.muted, fontSize: 11, marginTop: 3 },
 236    textButton: { padding: 6 }, textButtonLabel: { color: colorTokens.primary, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: "700" }, dangerText: { color: colorTokens.danger },
 237    error: { padding: 10, borderRadius: radiusTokens.md, backgroundColor: colorTokens.errorContainer, color: colorTokens.onErrorContainer, fontSize: 13 },

mobile/app/(tabs)/admin-documents.tsx

كل شاشة كتراجع ADMIN فالواجهة وكتتواصل مع endpoints محمية فالخادم.

   1  import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
   2  import {
   3    ActivityIndicator,
   4    Alert,
   5    Pressable,
   6    ScrollView,
   7    StyleSheet,
   8    Text,
   9    View,
  10  } from "react-native";
  11  import * as DocumentPicker from "expo-document-picker";
  12  import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
  13  import { router } from "expo-router";
  14  import { apiClient } from "@/src/api/client";
  15  import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/stores/authStore";
  16  import { Button } from "@/src/components/Button";
  17  import { BrandHeader } from "@/src/components/BrandHeader";
  18  import { colorTokens, radiusTokens, spacingTokens } from "@/src/theme/tokens";
  19  
  20  type RagDocument = {
  21    id: number;
  22    name: string;
  23    mimeType: string;
  24    status: "pending" | "processing" | "completed" | "failed";
  25    errorMessage?: string | null;
  26    chunksCreated: number;
  27    createdAt: string;
  28  };
  29  
  30  export default function AdminDocumentsScreen() {
  31    const role = useAuthStore((state) => state.user?.role);
  32    const [documents, setDocuments] = useState<RagDocument[]>([]);
  33    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  34    const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  35    const [error, setError] = useState("");
  36    const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");
  37  
  38    const loadDocuments = useCallback(async () => {
  39      setLoading(true);
  40      setError("");
  41      try {
  42        const response = await apiClient.get<{ documents: RagDocument[] }>("/api/documents");
  43        setDocuments(response.data.documents || []);
  44      } catch (err: any) {
  45        setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible de charger les documents.");
  46      } finally {
  47        setLoading(false);
  48      }
  49    }, []);
  50  
  51    useEffect(() => {
  52      if (role !== "ADMIN") {
  53        router.replace("/(tabs)");
  54        return;
  55      }
  56      void loadDocuments();
  57    }, [role, loadDocuments]);
  58  
  59    const uploadDocument = async () => {
  60      setError("");
  61      setSuccess("");
  62      const result = await DocumentPicker.getDocumentAsync({
  63        type: ["text/plain", "text/markdown"],
  64        copyToCacheDirectory: true,
  65        multiple: false,
  66      });
  67      if (result.canceled) return;
  68  
  69      const file = result.assets[0];
  70      if (!/\.(md|txt)$/i.test(file.name)) {
  71        setError("Choisissez un fichier .md ou .txt.");
  72        return;
  73      }
  74      if (file.size !== undefined && file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
  75        setError("Le fichier dépasse la limite de 2 Mo.");
  76        return;
  77      }
  78  
  79      setUploading(true);
  80      try {
  81        const form = new FormData();
  82        form.append("file", {
  83          uri: file.uri,
  84          name: file.name,
  85          type: file.mimeType || (file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith(".md") ? "text/markdown" : "text/plain"),
  86        } as unknown as Blob);
  87        form.append("name", file.name.replace(/\.(md|txt)$/i, ""));
  88  
  89        const response = await apiClient.post<{
  90          message: string;
  91          document: { id: number; name: string; status: string; chunksCreated: number };
  92        }>("/api/documents", form, { headers: { "Content-Type": "multipart/form-data" } });
  93        setSuccess(`Document indexé : ${response.data.document.chunksCreated} chunk(s) créé(s). L’agent peut l’utiliser maintenant.`);
  94        await loadDocuments();
  95      } catch (err: any) {
  96        setError(err.response?.data?.error || err.response?.data?.message || "L’envoi ou l’indexation du document a échoué.");
  97      } finally {
  98        setUploading(false);
  99      }
 100    };
 101  
 102    const deleteDocument = (document: RagDocument) => Alert.alert(
 103      "Supprimer le document ?",
 104      `« ${document.name} » et ses ${document.chunksCreated} chunk(s) seront supprimés.`,
 105      [
 106        { text: "Annuler", style: "cancel" },
 107        {
 108          text: "Supprimer",
 109          style: "destructive",
 110          onPress: async () => {
 111            setError("");
 112            setSuccess("");
 113            try {
 114              await apiClient.delete(`/api/documents/${document.id}`);
 115              setSuccess("Document et chunks supprimés.");
 116              await loadDocuments();
 117            } catch (err: any) {
 118              setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible de supprimer ce document.");
 119            }
 120          },
 121        },
 122      ]
 123    );
 124  
 125    if (role !== "ADMIN") return null;
 126  
 127    return (
 128      <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
 129        <BrandHeader />
 130        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
 131          <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()}><Text style={styles.back}>‹ Profil</Text></Pressable>
 132          <Text style={styles.title}>Documents de connaissance IA</Text>
 133          <Text style={styles.subtitle}>Ajoutez les informations fiables que l’assistant peut utiliser pour répondre aux clients.</Text>
 134          <View style={styles.infoBox}>
 135            <Text style={styles.infoTitle}>Formats acceptés : .md et .txt</Text>
 136            <Text style={styles.infoText}>Maximum 2 Mo. Le texte est découpé et indexé automatiquement. Une fois l’indexation terminée, l’agent peut retrouver ces informations.</Text>
 137          </View>
 138          {error ? <Text accessibilityRole="alert" style={styles.error}>{error}</Text> : null}
 139          {success ? <Text accessibilityLiveRegion="polite" style={styles.success}>{success}</Text> : null}
 140          <Button title={uploading ? "Envoi et indexation…" : "Choisir un document"} onPress={() => void uploadDocument()} disabled={uploading} />
 141          {uploading ? <View style={styles.progress}><ActivityIndicator color={colorTokens.primary} /><Text style={styles.progressText}>Création des chunks et des embeddings…</Text></View> : null}
 142  
 143          <View style={styles.listHeader}>
 144            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Documents ({documents.length})</Text>
 145            <Pressable onPress={() => void loadDocuments()}><Text style={styles.refresh}>Actualiser</Text></Pressable>
 146          </View>
 147          {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color={colorTokens.primary} style={styles.loader} /> : documents.length === 0 ? (
 148            <Text style={styles.empty}>Aucun document pour le moment.</Text>
 149          ) : documents.map((document) => (
 150            <View key={document.id} style={styles.documentCard}>
 151              <View style={styles.documentHeader}>
 152                <Text style={styles.documentName} numberOfLines={2}>{document.name}</Text>
 153                <View style={[styles.statusBadge, document.status === "completed" ? styles.completed : document.status === "failed" ? styles.failed : styles.inProgress]}>
 154                  <Text style={styles.statusText}>{statusLabel(document.status)}</Text>
 155                </View>
 156              </View>
 157              <Text style={styles.documentMeta}>{document.chunksCreated} chunk(s) · {document.mimeType}</Text>
 158              {document.status === "failed" ? <Text style={styles.failedHint}>{document.errorMessage || "Indexation échouée. Supprimez-le et réessayez l’envoi."}</Text> : null}
 159              <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" onPress={() => deleteDocument(document)} style={styles.deleteButton}>
 160                <Text style={styles.deleteLabel}>Supprimer le document</Text>
 161              </Pressable>
 162            </View>
 163          ))}
 164        </ScrollView>
 165      </SafeAreaView>
 166    );
 167  }
 168  
 169  function statusLabel(status: RagDocument["status"]): string {
 170    if (status === "completed") return "Indexé";
 171    if (status === "failed") return "Échec";
 172    if (status === "processing") return "En cours";
 173    return "En attente";
 174  }
 175  
 176  const styles = StyleSheet.create({
 177    screen: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colorTokens.surface },
 178    content: { padding: spacingTokens.md, paddingBottom: spacingTokens.xxl },
 179    back: { color: colorTokens.primary, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: "700", marginBottom: spacingTokens.md },
 180    title: { color: colorTokens.ink, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: "800" },
 181    subtitle: { color: colorTokens.muted, fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 19, marginTop: 6, marginBottom: spacingTokens.md },
 182    infoBox: { borderRadius: radiusTokens.md, backgroundColor: colorTokens.surfaceContainer, padding: spacingTokens.md, marginBottom: spacingTokens.md },
 183    infoTitle: { color: colorTokens.ink, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: "800" }, infoText: { color: colorTokens.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 18, marginTop: 4 },
 184    error: { padding: 10, borderRadius: radiusTokens.md, backgroundColor: colorTokens.errorContainer, color: colorTokens.onErrorContainer, fontSize: 13, marginBottom: spacingTokens.sm },
 185    success: { padding: 10, borderRadius: radiusTokens.md, backgroundColor: colorTokens.secondaryContainer, color: colorTokens.onSecondaryContainer, fontSize: 13, marginBottom: spacingTokens.sm },
 186    progress: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, paddingVertical: spacingTokens.md }, progressText: { color: colorTokens.muted, fontSize: 12 },
 187    listHeader: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", marginTop: spacingTokens.lg, marginBottom: spacingTokens.sm },
 188    sectionTitle: { color: colorTokens.ink, fontSize: 17, fontWeight: "800" }, refresh: { color: colorTokens.primary, fontWeight: "700", fontSize: 12 },
 189    loader: { marginTop: spacingTokens.lg }, empty: { color: colorTokens.muted, textAlign: "center", paddingVertical: spacingTokens.xl },
 190    documentCard: { padding: spacingTokens.md, borderRadius: radiusTokens.md, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colorTokens.border, backgroundColor: colorTokens.surfaceContainerLowest, marginBottom: spacingTokens.sm },
 191    documentHeader: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: spacingTokens.sm }, documentName: { color: colorTokens.ink, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: "700", flex: 1 },
 192    statusBadge: { borderRadius: radiusTokens.full, paddingHorizontal: 9, paddingVertical: 4 }, completed: { backgroundColor: colorTokens.secondaryContainer }, failed: { backgroundColor: colorTokens.errorContainer }, inProgress: { backgroundColor: colorTokens.codGold },
 193    statusText: { color: colorTokens.ink, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: "800" }, documentMeta: { color: colorTokens.muted, fontSize: 11, marginTop: 7 },
 194    failedHint: { color: colorTokens.danger, fontSize: 12, marginTop: 7 }, deleteButton: { alignSelf: "flex-end", paddingTop: 10 }, deleteLabel: { color: colorTokens.danger, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: "700" },
 195  });
mobile/app/(tabs)/chat.tsx

الشاشة كتدير state ديال المحادثة، بينما الخادم هو اللي كينفذ الخدمات.

   1  import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";

  34    name: string;
  35    price: number;
  36    stock?: number;

  38    category?: string | null;

  44    recapText: string;

  46    status: "pending" | "confirmed" | "cancelled";

  55  export interface ChatMessage {

  57    sender: "user" | "assistant";
  58    text: string;
  59    timestamp: string;
  60    isStreaming?: boolean;

  62    products?: ProductCardData[];
  63    orderRecap?: OrderRecapData;
  64    createdOrder?: CreatedOrderData;
  65    error?: boolean;
  66    canRetry?: boolean;
  67    retryText?: string;

  70  const INITIAL_SUGGESTIONS = [
  71    "Quels sont vos délais pour Casablanca ?",

  76  
  77  function formatConversationDate(dateStr: string): string {
  78    try {
  79      const date = new Date(dateStr);
  80      const now = new Date();
  81      const diffMs = now.getTime() - date.getTime();
  82      const diffHours = diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  83  
  84      if (diffHours < 24 && now.getDate() === date.getDate()) {
  85        return `Aujourd'hui à ${date.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}`;
  86      }
  87      if (diffHours < 48) {
  88        return `Hier à ${date.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}`;
  89      }
  90      return date.toLocaleDateString([], { day: "numeric", month: "short" });
  91    } catch {
  92      return dateStr;
  93    }
  94  }
