  96  export default function ChatScreen() {
  97    const router = useRouter();
  98    const user = useAuthStore((state) => state.user);
  99  
 100    const [conversationId, setConversationId] = useState<number | undefined>(undefined);
 101    const [conversations, setConversations] = useState<ConversationSummary[]>([]);
 102    const [isHistoryModalVisible, setIsHistoryModalVisible] = useState(false);
 103    const [isLoadingHistory, setIsLoadingHistory] = useState(false);
 104  
 105    const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([
 106      {
 107        id: "welcome-1",
 108        sender: "assistant",
 109        text: `Bonjour ${user?.name ? user.name : ""} ! Je suis ShopMate AI, votre assistant d'achat en paiement à la livraison (COD). Posez-moi vos questions ou choisissez une conversation précédente.`,
 110        timestamp: "À l'instant",
 111      },
 112    ]);
 113  
 114    const [input, setInput] = useState("");
 115    const [isSending, setIsSending] = useState(false);
 116    const [agentStatus, setAgentStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
 117    const [rateLimitNotice, setRateLimitNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);
 118  
 119    const flatListRef = useRef<FlatList>(null);
 120    const activeStreamRef = useRef<ActiveStream | null>(null);
 121  
 122    // ── Helper to convert DB messages to ChatMessage items ─────────────────────
 123    const mapDbMessagesToChat = useCallback(
 124      (dbMessages: ConversationSummary["messages"]): ChatMessage[] => {
 125        return dbMessages.map((m) => {
 126          let createdOrder: CreatedOrderData | undefined;
 127          const orderMatch = m.content.match(/Commande\s*#(\d+)/i);
 128          if (m.role === "assistant" && orderMatch) {
 129            createdOrder = { orderId: parseInt(orderMatch[1], 10) };
 130          }
 131  
 132          return {
 133            id: `msg-${m.id}`,
 134            sender: m.role,
 135            text: m.content,
 136            timestamp: new Date(m.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString([], {
 137              hour: "2-digit",
 138              minute: "2-digit",
 139            }),
 140            createdOrder,
 141          };
 142        });
 143      },
 144      []
 145    );
 146  
 147    // ── Fetch conversations list from backend ──────────────────────────────────
 148    const refreshConversationsList = useCallback(
 149      async (selectLatestIfEmpty = false) => {
 150        try {
 151          setIsLoadingHistory(true);
 152          const list = await fetchConversationHistory();
 153          setConversations(list || []);
 154  
 155          if (selectLatestIfEmpty && list && list.length > 0) {
 156            const latest = list[0];
 157            setConversationId(latest.id);
 158            if (latest.messages && latest.messages.length > 0) {
 159              setMessages(mapDbMessagesToChat(latest.messages));
 160            }
 161          }
 162        } catch {
 163          // Fallback to initial state if offline
 164        } finally {
 165          setIsLoadingHistory(false);
 166        }
 167      },
 168      [mapDbMessagesToChat]
 169    );
 170  
 171    // ── Initial load on mount ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
 172    useEffect(() => {
 173      let isMounted = true;
 174      if (isMounted) {
 175        refreshConversationsList(true);
 176      }
 177      return () => {
 178        isMounted = false;
 179        activeStreamRef.current?.abort();
 180      };
 181    }, [refreshConversationsList]);
 182  
 183    // ── Switch to an existing conversation ─────────────────────────────────────
 184    const handleSelectConversation = (conv: ConversationSummary) => {
 185      activeStreamRef.current?.abort();
 186      setConversationId(conv.id);
 187      setIsSending(false);
 188      setAgentStatus(null);
 189      setRateLimitNotice(null);
 190  
 191      if (conv.messages && conv.messages.length > 0) {
 192        setMessages(mapDbMessagesToChat(conv.messages));
 193      } else {
 194        setMessages([
 195          {
 196            id: `conv-start-${conv.id}`,
 197            sender: "assistant",
 198            text: `Reprise de la conversation « ${conv.title} ». Comment puis-je vous aider ?`,
 199            timestamp: "À l'instant",
 200          },
 201        ]);
 202      }
 203  
 204      setIsHistoryModalVisible(false);
 205  
 206      setTimeout(() => {
 207        flatListRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: false });
 208      }, 100);
 209    };
 210  
 211    // ── Start a fresh new conversation ─────────────────────────────────────────
 212    const handleStartNewConversation = () => {
 213      activeStreamRef.current?.abort();
 214      setConversationId(undefined);
 215      setIsSending(false);
 216      setAgentStatus(null);
 217      setRateLimitNotice(null);
 218  
 219      setMessages([
 220        {
 221          id: `welcome-${Date.now()}`,
 222          sender: "assistant",
 223          text: `✨ Nouvelle conversation démarrée ! Que recherchez-vous aujourd'hui (produits, suivi de commande, délais COD) ?`,
 224          timestamp: "À l'instant",
 225        },
 226      ]);
 227  
 228      setIsHistoryModalVisible(false);
 229    };
 230  
 231    // ── Send message with real-time SSE streaming ───────────────────────────────
 232    const sendMessage = async (textToSend?: string) => {
 233      const content = (textToSend || input).trim();
 234      if (!content || isSending) return;
 235  
 236      setRateLimitNotice(null);
 237  
 238      const userMessageId = `user-${Date.now()}`;
 239      const userMessage: ChatMessage = {
 240        id: userMessageId,
 241        sender: "user",
 242        text: content,
 243        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {
 244          hour: "2-digit",
 245          minute: "2-digit",
 246        }),
 247      };
 248  
 249      const assistantPlaceholderId = `ai-${Date.now()}`;
 250      const assistantMessage: ChatMessage = {
 251        id: assistantPlaceholderId,
 252        sender: "assistant",
 253        text: "",
 254        isStreaming: true,
 255        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {
 256          hour: "2-digit",
 257          minute: "2-digit",
 258        }),
 259      };
 260  
 261      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMessage, assistantMessage]);
 262      setInput("");
 263      setIsSending(true);
 264      setAgentStatus("ShopMate AI réfléchit...");
 265  
 266      setTimeout(() => {
 267        flatListRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true });
 268      }, 100);
 269  
 270      try {
 271        activeStreamRef.current = await streamAgentChat(
 272          {
 273            message: content,
 274            conversationId,
 275          },
 276          {
 277            onStart: (convId) => {
 278              setConversationId(convId);
 279            },
 280  
 281            onSources: (sources) => {
 282              setMessages((prev) =>
 283                prev.map((m) =>
 284                  m.id === assistantPlaceholderId ? { ...m, sources } : m
 285                )
 286              );
 287            },
 288  
 289            onToolCall: (tool) => {
 290              if (tool.includes("Product") || tool.includes("Catalog") || tool.includes("search")) {
 291                setAgentStatus("🔍 Recherche de produits...");
 292              } else if (tool.includes("Stock")) {
 293                setAgentStatus("📊 Vérification du stock en direct...");
 294              } else if (tool.includes("createOrder")) {
 295                setAgentStatus("📋 Préparation du récapitulatif...");
 296              } else if (tool.includes("cancelOrder")) {
 297                setAgentStatus("⚠️ Préparation de l'annulation...");
 298              } else if (tool.includes("Order")) {
 299                setAgentStatus("📦 Consultation des commandes...");
 300              } else {
 301                setAgentStatus("⚙️ Traitement de votre demande...");
 302              }
 303            },
 304  
 305            onToolResult: (tool, result) => {
 306              // 1. Found products from searchProducts or getProductDetails
 307              if (tool === "searchProducts" && result?.products?.length > 0) {
 308                const productsList: ProductCardData[] = result.products.map((p: any) => ({
 309                  id: p.id,
 310                  name: p.name,
 311                  price: Number(p.price),
 312                  stock: p.stock,
 313                  inStock: p.inStock ?? (p.stock > 0),
 314                  category: p.category,
 315                }));
 316  
 317                setMessages((prev) =>
 318                  prev.map((m) =>
 319                    m.id === assistantPlaceholderId
 320                      ? { ...m, products: productsList }
 321                      : m
 322                  )
 323                );
 324              } else if (tool === "getProductDetails" && result?.product) {
 325                const p = result.product;
 326                setMessages((prev) =>
 327                  prev.map((m) =>
 328                    m.id === assistantPlaceholderId
 329                      ? {
 330                          ...m,
 331                          products: [
 332                            {
 333                              id: p.id,
 334                              name: p.name,
 335                              price: Number(p.price),
 336                              stock: p.stock,
 337                              inStock: p.stock > 0,
 338                              category: p.category?.name,
 339                            },
 340                          ],
 341                        }
 342                      : m
 343                  )
 344                );
 345              }
 346  
 347              // 2. Order Recap requiring explicit confirmation
 348              if (tool === "createOrder" && result?.requiresConfirmation) {
 349                setMessages((prev) =>
 350                  prev.map((m) =>
 351                    m.id === assistantPlaceholderId
 352                      ? {
 353                          ...m,
 354                          orderRecap: {
 355                            action: "createOrder",
 356                            token: result.confirmationToken,
 357                            recapText: result.recap,
 358                            total: result.total,
 359                            status: "pending",
 360                          },
 361                        }
 362                      : m
 363                  )
 364                );
 365              } else if (tool === "cancelOrder" && result?.requiresConfirmation) {
 366                setMessages((prev) =>
 367                  prev.map((m) =>
 368                    m.id === assistantPlaceholderId
 369                      ? {
 370                          ...m,
 371                          orderRecap: {
 372                            action: "cancelOrder",
 373                            token: result.confirmationToken,
 374                            recapText: result.recap,
 375                            status: "pending",
 376                          },
 377                        }
 378                      : m
 379                  )
 380                );
 381              }
 382  
 383              // 3. Executed order result
 384              if (result?.success && result?.orderId) {
 385                setMessages((prev) =>
 386                  prev.map((m) =>
 387                    m.id === assistantPlaceholderId
 388                      ? {
 389                          ...m,
 390                          createdOrder: {
 391                            orderId: result.orderId,
 392                            total: result.total,
 393                            status: result.status,
 394                          },
 395                        }
 396                      : m
 397                  )
 398                );
 399              }
 400            },
 401  
 402            onToken: (token) => {
 403              setAgentStatus(null);
 404              setMessages((prev) =>
 405                prev.map((m) =>
 406                  m.id === assistantPlaceholderId
 407                    ? { ...m, text: m.text + token }
 408                    : m
 409                )
 410              );
 411              flatListRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true });
 412            },
 413  
 414            onConfirmation: (confirmed, orderId) => {
 415              if (confirmed && orderId) {
 416                setMessages((prev) =>
 417                  prev.map((m) =>
 418                    m.id === assistantPlaceholderId
 419                      ? { ...m, createdOrder: { orderId } }
 420                      : m
 421                  )
 422                );
 423              }
 424            },
 425  
 426            onDone: (payload) => {
 427              setMessages((prev) =>
 428                prev.map((m) => {
 429                  if (m.id !== assistantPlaceholderId) return m;
 430                  const updated: ChatMessage = { ...m, isStreaming: false };
 431                  if (payload.orderId) {
 432                    updated.createdOrder = { orderId: payload.orderId };
 433                  }
 434                  return updated;
 435                })
 436              );
 437              setIsSending(false);
 438              setAgentStatus(null);
 439  
 440              // Silently update conversation list in background so title/dates are refreshed
 441              refreshConversationsList(false);
 442            },
 443  
 444            onError: (err) => {
 445              if (err.isRateLimit) {
 446                setRateLimitNotice(err.message);
 447              }
 448              setMessages((prev) =>
 449                prev.map((m) =>
 450                  m.id === assistantPlaceholderId
 451                    ? {
 452                        ...m,
 453                        text:
 454                          m.text ||
 455                          `⚠️ ${err.message || "Une erreur est survenue lors de l'échange."}`,
 456                        isStreaming: false,
 457                        error: true,
 458                        canRetry: true,
 459                        retryText: content,
 460                      }
 461                    : m
 462                )
 463              );
 464              setIsSending(false);
 465              setAgentStatus(null);
 466            },
 467          }
 468        );
 469      } catch {
 470        setMessages((prev) =>
 471          prev.map((m) =>
 472            m.id === assistantPlaceholderId
 473              ? {
 474                  ...m,
 475                  text: "⚠️ Impossible de contacter le serveur. Vérifiez votre connexion.",
 476                  isStreaming: false,
 477                  error: true,
 478                  canRetry: true,
 479                  retryText: content,
 480                }
 481              : m
 482          )
 483        );
 484        setIsSending(false);
 485        setAgentStatus(null);
 486      }
 487    };
 488  
 489    // ── Confirm order button handler ────────────────────────────────────────────
 490    const handleConfirmOrder = (messageId: string) => {
 491      setMessages((prev) =>
 492        prev.map((m) =>
 493          m.id === messageId && m.orderRecap
 494            ? {
 495                ...m,
 496                orderRecap: { ...m.orderRecap, status: "confirmed" },
 497              }
 498            : m
 499        )
 500      );
 501      sendMessage("Oui");
 502    };
 503  
 504    // ── Cancel order recap handler ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 505    const handleCancelOrder = (messageId: string) => {
 506      setMessages((prev) =>
 507        prev.map((m) =>
 508          m.id === messageId && m.orderRecap
 509            ? {
 510                ...m,
 511                orderRecap: { ...m.orderRecap, status: "cancelled" },
 512              }
 513            : m
 514        )
 515      );
 516      sendMessage("Non, annuler");
 517    };
 518  
 519    const handleStopStreaming = () => {
 520      if (activeStreamRef.current) {
 521        activeStreamRef.current.abort();
 522        activeStreamRef.current = null;
 523        setIsSending(false);
 524        setAgentStatus(null);
 525        setMessages((prev) =>
 526          prev.map((m) => (m.isStreaming ? { ...m, isStreaming: false } : m))
 527        );
 528      }
 529    };
 530  
 531    return (
 532      <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
 533        <BrandHeader />
 534        {/* Top Header */}
 535        <View style={styles.header}>
 536          <View style={styles.brandRow}>
 537            <View style={styles.botAvatar}>
 538              <Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>🤖</Text>
 539            </View>
 540            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
 541              <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>ShopMate AI</Text>
 542              <View style={styles.statusRow}>
 543                <View style={styles.onlineDot} />
 544                <Text style={styles.statusText} numberOfLines={1}>
 545                  {agentStatus || "En ligne • Paiement à la livraison (COD)"}
 546                </Text>
 547              </View>
 548            </View>
 549  
 550            {/* Action buttons in header */}
 551            <View style={styles.headerActions}>
 552              {isSending ? (
 553                <Pressable
 554                  style={styles.stopButton}
 555                  onPress={handleStopStreaming}
 556                  accessibilityLabel="Arrêter la réponse"
 557                >
 558                  <Text style={styles.stopButtonText}>Arrêter</Text>
 559                </Pressable>
 560              ) : null}
 561  
 562              <Pressable
 563                style={styles.newChatButton}
 564                onPress={handleStartNewConversation}
 565                accessibilityLabel="Nouvelle conversation"
 566              >
 567                <Text style={styles.newChatButtonText}>+ Nouveau</Text>
 568              </Pressable>
 569  
 570              <Pressable
 571                style={styles.historyButton}
 572                onPress={() => {
 573                  refreshConversationsList(false);
 574                  setIsHistoryModalVisible(true);
 575                }}
 576                accessibilityLabel="Historique des conversations"
 577              >
 578                <Text style={styles.historyButtonText}>📜</Text>
 579              </Pressable>
 580            </View>
 581          </View>
 582  
 583          {rateLimitNotice ? (
 584            <View style={styles.rateLimitBanner}>
 585              <Text style={styles.rateLimitText}>⏱️ {rateLimitNotice}</Text>
 586            </View>
 587          ) : null}
 588        </View>
 589  
 590        <KeyboardAvoidingView
 591          behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : undefined}
 592          keyboardVerticalOffset={Platform.OS === "ios" ? 90 : 0}
 593          style={styles.keyboardContainer}
 594        >
 595          <FlatList
 596            ref={flatListRef}
 597            data={messages}
 598            keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
 599            contentContainerStyle={styles.messageList}
 600            onContentSizeChange={() =>
 601              flatListRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true })
 602            }
 603            renderItem={({ item }) => (
 604              <View
 605                style={[
 606                  styles.messageRow,
 607                  item.sender === "user" ? styles.userRow : styles.assistantRow,
 608                ]}
 609              >
 610                {item.sender === "assistant" ? (
 611                  <View style={styles.bubbleAvatar}>
 612                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 14 }}>🤖</Text>
 613                  </View>
 614                ) : null}
 615  
 616                <View
 617                  style={[
 618                    styles.bubble,
 619                    item.sender === "user"
 620                      ? styles.userBubble
 621                      : styles.assistantBubble,
 622                    item.error && styles.errorBubble,
 623                  ]}
 624                >
 625                  {/* 1. Message Text or Typing Indicator */}
 626                  {item.isStreaming && !item.text ? (
 627                    <View style={styles.typingRow}>
 628                      <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={colorTokens.primary} />
 629                      <Text style={styles.typingText}>
 630                        {agentStatus || "Génération en cours..."}
 631                      </Text>
 632                    </View>
 633                  ) : (
 634                    <Text
 635                      style={[
 636                        styles.bubbleText,
 637                        item.sender === "user"
 638                          ? styles.userText
 639                          : styles.assistantText,
 640                        item.error && styles.errorText,
 641                      ]}
 642                    >
 643                      {item.text}
 644                    </Text>
 645                  )}
 646  
 647                  {/* 2. Tappable Product Cards */}
 648                  {item.products && item.products.length > 0 ? (
 649                    <View style={styles.productsContainer}>
 650                      <Text style={styles.productsSectionTitle}>
 651                        🛍️ Produits disponibles :
 652                      </Text>
 653                      <ScrollView
 654                        horizontal
 655                        showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
 656                        contentContainerStyle={styles.productsScrollContent}
 657                      >
 658                        {item.products.map((p: ProductCardData) => (
 659                          <Pressable
 660                            key={p.id}
 661                            style={({ pressed }) => [
 662                              styles.productCard,
 663                              pressed && styles.buttonPressed,
 664                            ]}
 665                            onPress={() => router.push(`/product/${p.id}` as any)}
 666                          >
 667                            <View style={styles.productCardTop}>
 668                              <View style={styles.productIconBox}>
 669                                <Text style={{ fontSize: 22 }}>📦</Text>
 670                              </View>
 671                              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
 672                                <Text style={styles.productName} numberOfLines={1}>
 673                                  {p.name}
 674                                </Text>
 675                                {p.category ? (
 676                                  <Text style={styles.productCategory}>{p.category}</Text>
 677                                ) : null}
 678                              </View>
 679                            </View>
 680  
 681                            <View style={styles.productCardBottom}>
 682                              <Text style={styles.productPrice}>{p.price} DH</Text>
 683                              <View
 684                                style={[
 685                                  styles.stockPill,
 686                                  p.inStock ? styles.stockPillIn : styles.stockPillOut,
 687                                ]}
 688                              >
 689                                <Text
 690                                  style={[
 691                                    styles.stockPillText,
 692                                    p.inStock
 693                                      ? styles.stockPillTextIn
 694                                      : styles.stockPillTextOut,
 695                                  ]}
 696                                >
 697                                  {p.inStock ? "En stock" : "Rupture"}
 698                                </Text>
 699                              </View>
 700                            </View>
 701  
 702                            <Text style={styles.tapToViewText}>Voir la fiche ➔</Text>
 703                          </Pressable>
 704                        ))}
 705                      </ScrollView>
 706                    </View>
 707                  ) : null}
 708  
 709                  {/* 3. Order Recap Card with Explicit Confirm / Cancel Buttons */}
 710                  {item.orderRecap ? (
 711                    <View style={styles.recapCardContainer}>
 712                      <View style={styles.recapHeaderRow}>
 713                        <Text style={styles.recapHeaderTitle}>
 714                          {item.orderRecap.action === "cancelOrder"
 715                            ? "⚠️ Confirmation d'annulation"
 716                            : "📋 Récapitulatif de commande"}
 717                        </Text>
 718                        <View style={styles.codTag}>
 719                          <Text style={styles.codTagText}>Paiement COD</Text>
 720                        </View>
 721                      </View>
 722  
 723                      <Text style={styles.recapBodyText}>{item.orderRecap.recapText}</Text>
 724  
 725                      {item.orderRecap.total ? (
 726                        <View style={styles.recapTotalRow}>
 727                          <Text style={styles.recapTotalLabel}>
 728                            Total à payer à la livraison :
 729                          </Text>
 730                          <Text style={styles.recapTotalAmount}>
 731                            {item.orderRecap.total} DH
 732                          </Text>
 733                        </View>
 734                      ) : null}
 735  
 736                      {item.orderRecap.status === "pending" ? (
 737                        <View style={styles.recapActionsRow}>
 738                          <Pressable
 739                            style={({ pressed }) => [
 740                              styles.confirmButton,
 741                              pressed && styles.buttonPressed,
 742                              isSending && styles.buttonDisabled,
 743                            ]}
 744                            onPress={() => handleConfirmOrder(item.id)}
 745                            disabled={isSending}
 746                          >
 747                            <Text style={styles.confirmButtonText}>
 748                              {item.orderRecap.action === "cancelOrder"
 749                                ? "Confirmer l'annulation"
 750                                : "✅ Confirmer la commande"}
 751                            </Text>
 752                          </Pressable>
 753  
 754                          <Pressable
 755                            style={({ pressed }) => [
 756                              styles.cancelButton,
 757                              pressed && styles.buttonPressed,
 758                              isSending && styles.buttonDisabled,
 759                            ]}
 760                            onPress={() => handleCancelOrder(item.id)}
 761                            disabled={isSending}
 762                          >
 763                            <Text style={styles.cancelButtonText}>Non, annuler</Text>
 764                          </Pressable>
 765                        </View>
 766                      ) : item.orderRecap.status === "confirmed" ? (
 767                        <View style={styles.confirmedNoticeRow}>
 768                          <Text style={styles.confirmedNoticeText}>
 769                            ✅ Validé par vos soins — Commande en création...
 770                          </Text>
 771                        </View>
 772                      ) : (
 773                        <View style={styles.cancelledNoticeRow}>
 774                          <Text style={styles.cancelledNoticeText}>
 775                            ❌ Action annulée
 776                          </Text>
 777                        </View>
 778                      )}
 779                    </View>
 780                  ) : null}
 781  
 782                  {/* 4. Created Order Success Card */}
 783                  {item.createdOrder ? (
 784                    <View style={styles.createdOrderCard}>
 785                      <View style={styles.createdOrderHeader}>
 786                        <View style={styles.createdOrderIconBadge}>
 787                          <Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>🎉</Text>
 788                        </View>
 789                        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
 790                          <Text style={styles.createdOrderTitle}>
 791                            Commande #{item.createdOrder.orderId} créée !
 792                          </Text>
 793                          <Text style={styles.createdOrderSubtitle}>
 794                            Paiement en espèces à la livraison (COD)
 795                          </Text>
 796                        </View>
 797                      </View>
 798  
 799                      {item.createdOrder.total ? (
 800                        <Text style={styles.createdOrderAmount}>
 801                          Montant total : {item.createdOrder.total} DH
 802                        </Text>
 803                      ) : null}
 804  
 805                      <Pressable
 806                        style={({ pressed }) => [
 807                          styles.viewOrderButton,
 808                          pressed && styles.buttonPressed,
 809                        ]}
 810                        onPress={() => router.push("/orders" as any)}
 811                      >
 812                        <Text style={styles.viewOrderButtonText}>
 813                          📦 Voir ma commande ➔
 814                        </Text>
 815                      </Pressable>
 816                    </View>
 817                  ) : null}
 818  
 819                  {/* 5. Cited Sources under RAG answers */}
 820                  {item.sources && item.sources.length > 0 ? (
 821                    <View style={styles.sourcesContainer}>
 822                      <Text style={styles.sourcesLabel}>Sources vérifiées :</Text>
 823                      <View style={styles.sourcesList}>
 824                        {item.sources.map((src: ChatSource, i: number) => (
 825                          <View key={i} style={styles.sourceBadge}>
 826                            <Text style={styles.sourceBadgeText}>
 827                              📄 {src.name} ({src.score}%)
 828                            </Text>
 829                          </View>
 830                        ))}
 831                      </View>
 832                    </View>
 833                  ) : null}
 834  
 835                  {/* Retry action on error */}
 836                  {item.canRetry && item.retryText ? (
 837                    <Pressable
 838                      style={styles.retryButton}
 839                      onPress={() => sendMessage(item.retryText)}
 840                    >
 841                      <Text style={styles.retryButtonText}>🔄 Réessayer</Text>
 842                    </Pressable>
 843                  ) : null}
 844  
 845                  <Text
 846                    style={[
 847                      styles.timestamp,
 848                      item.sender === "user"
 849                        ? styles.userTimestamp
 850                        : styles.assistantTimestamp,
 851                    ]}
 852                  >
 853                    {item.timestamp}
 854                  </Text>
 855                </View>
 856              </View>
 857            )}
 858            ListFooterComponent={
 859              messages.length <= 2 && !isSending ? (
 860                <View style={styles.suggestionsContainer}>
 861                  <Text style={styles.suggestionsTitle}>Suggestions rapides :</Text>
 862                  <View style={styles.suggestionsList}>
 863                    {INITIAL_SUGGESTIONS.map((suggestion, index) => (
 864                      <Pressable
 865                        key={index}
 866                        style={({ pressed }) => [
 867                          styles.suggestionChip,
 868                          pressed && styles.suggestionChipPressed,
 869                        ]}
 870                        onPress={() => sendMessage(suggestion)}
 871                      >
 872                        <Text style={styles.suggestionText}>{suggestion}</Text>
 873                      </Pressable>
 874                    ))}
 875                  </View>
 876                </View>
 877              ) : null
 878            }
 879          />
 880  
 881          {/* Input Bar */}
 882          <View style={styles.inputBar}>
 883            <TextInput
 884              style={styles.textInput}
 885              placeholder="Posez une question, demandez un produit..."
 886              placeholderTextColor={colorTokens.muted}
 887              value={input}
 888              onChangeText={setInput}
 889              multiline
 890              maxLength={1000}
 891              returnKeyType="send"
 892              onSubmitEditing={() => sendMessage()}
 893              editable={!isSending}
 894            />
 895            <Pressable
 896              style={({ pressed }) => [
 897                styles.sendButton,
 898                (!input.trim() || isSending) && styles.sendButtonDisabled,
 899                pressed && styles.sendButtonPressed,
 900              ]}
 901              onPress={() => sendMessage()}
 902              disabled={!input.trim() || isSending}
 903            >
 904              {isSending ? (
 905                <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={colorTokens.surface} />
 906              ) : (
 907                <Text style={styles.sendButtonText}>➔</Text>
 908              )}
 909            </Pressable>
 910          </View>
 911        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
 912  
 913        {/* ── Modal: Conversation History ────────────────────────────────────── */}
 914        <Modal
 915          visible={isHistoryModalVisible}
 916          animationType="slide"
 917          transparent={true}
 918          onRequestClose={() => setIsHistoryModalVisible(false)}
 919        >
 920          <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
 921            <View style={styles.modalContent}>
 922              {/* Modal Header */}
 923              <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
 924                <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
 925                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>📜</Text>
 926                  <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>Mes conversations</Text>
 927                </View>
 928                <Pressable
 929                  style={styles.modalCloseButton}
 930                  onPress={() => setIsHistoryModalVisible(false)}
 931                >
 932                  <Text style={styles.modalCloseButtonText}>✕</Text>
 933                </Pressable>
 934              </View>
 935  
 936              {/* New Conversation Action */}
 937              <Pressable
 938                style={({ pressed }) => [
 939                  styles.modalNewChatButton,
 940                  pressed && styles.buttonPressed,
 941                ]}
 942                onPress={handleStartNewConversation}
 943              >
 944                <Text style={styles.modalNewChatButtonText}>
 945                  ✨ Démarrer une nouvelle conversation
 946                </Text>
 947              </Pressable>
 948  
 949              {/* Conversation List */}
 950              {isLoadingHistory ? (
 951                <View style={styles.modalLoadingBox}>
 952                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={colorTokens.primary} />
 953                  <Text style={styles.modalLoadingText}>Chargement de l'historique...</Text>
 954                </View>
 955              ) : conversations.length === 0 ? (
 956                <View style={styles.modalEmptyBox}>
 957                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 32, marginBottom: 8 }}>💬</Text>
 958                  <Text style={styles.modalEmptyTitle}>Aucune conversation passée</Text>
 959                  <Text style={styles.modalEmptySubtitle}>
 960                    Posez votre première question pour commencer à échanger avec ShopMate AI.
 961                  </Text>
 962                </View>
 963              ) : (
 964                <FlatList
 965                  data={conversations}
 966                  keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
 967                  contentContainerStyle={styles.modalListContent}
 968                  renderItem={({ item }) => {
 969                    const isActive = item.id === conversationId;
 970                    const lastMsg =
 971                      item.messages && item.messages.length > 0
 972                        ? item.messages[item.messages.length - 1].content
 973                        : "Pas encore de messages";
 974  
 975                    return (
 976                      <Pressable
 977                        style={({ pressed }) => [
 978                          styles.conversationItem,
 979                          isActive && styles.conversationItemActive,
 980                          pressed && styles.buttonPressed,
 981                        ]}
 982                        onPress={() => handleSelectConversation(item)}
 983                      >
 984                        <View style={styles.conversationItemHeader}>
 985                          <Text
 986                            style={[
 987                              styles.conversationItemTitle,
 988                              isActive && styles.conversationItemTitleActive,
 989                            ]}
 990                            numberOfLines={1}
 991                          >
 992                            {item.title || `Conversation #${item.id}`}
 993                          </Text>
 994                          <Text style={styles.conversationItemDate}>
 995                            {formatConversationDate(item.updatedAt)}
 996                          </Text>
 997                        </View>
 998  
 999                        <Text
1000                          style={styles.conversationItemPreview}
1001                          numberOfLines={2}
1002                        >
1003                          {lastMsg}
1004                        </Text>
1005  
1006                        {isActive ? (
1007                          <View style={styles.activeBadge}>
1008                            <Text style={styles.activeBadgeText}>En cours</Text>
1009                          </View>
1010                        ) : null}
1011                      </Pressable>
1012                    );
1013                  }}
1014                />
1015              )}
1016            </View>
1017          </View>
1018        </Modal>
1019      </SafeAreaView>
1020    );
1021  }

mobile/app/(tabs)/index.tsx

الشاشة كتعرض وتجمع الاختيارات، والـstore كيرسل الفلاتر وكيحتافظ بالنتائج.

   1  import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";
   2  import {
   3    ActivityIndicator,
   4    FlatList,
   5    Image,
   6    Pressable,
   7    RefreshControl,
   8    StyleSheet,
   9    Text,
  10    TextInput,
  11    View,
  12  } from "react-native";
  13  import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
  14  import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
  15  import { useCatalogStore, type Product, type Category } from "@/src/stores/catalogStore";
  16  import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/stores/authStore";
  17  import {
  18    colorTokens,
  19    radiusTokens,
  20    spacingTokens,
  21    typographyTokens,
  22  } from "@/src/theme/tokens";
  23  import { BrandHeader, Loader, EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/src/components";
  24  
  25  const PRICE_FILTERS = [
  26    { label: "Tous", min: undefined, max: undefined },
  27    { label: "< 200 DH", min: undefined, max: 200 },
  28    { label: "200 - 500 DH", min: 200, max: 500 },
  29    { label: "> 500 DH", min: 500, max: undefined },
  30  ];
  31  
  32  export default function CatalogScreen() {
  33    const router = useRouter();
  34    const products = useCatalogStore((state) => state.products);
  35    const categories = useCatalogStore((state) => state.categories);
  36    const selectedCategoryId = useCatalogStore((state) => state.selectedCategoryId);
  37    const search = useCatalogStore((state) => state.search);
  38    const minPrice = useCatalogStore((state) => state.minPrice);
  39    const maxPrice = useCatalogStore((state) => state.maxPrice);
  40    const status = useCatalogStore((state) => state.status);
  41    const error = useCatalogStore((state) => state.error);
  42    const hasMore = useCatalogStore((state) => state.hasMore);
  43  
  44    const setSearch = useCatalogStore((state) => state.setSearch);
  45    const setSelectedCategory = useCatalogStore((state) => state.setSelectedCategory);
  46    const setPriceRange = useCatalogStore((state) => state.setPriceRange);
  47    const fetchProducts = useCatalogStore((state) => state.fetchProducts);
  48    const fetchCategories = useCatalogStore((state) => state.fetchCategories);
  49    const loadMore = useCatalogStore((state) => state.loadMore);
  50    const resetFilters = useCatalogStore((state) => state.resetFilters);
  51  
  52    const user = useAuthStore((state) => state.user);
  53  
  54    useEffect(() => {
  55      void fetchCategories();
  56      void fetchProducts(true);
  57    }, []);
  58  
  59    const handleSearchSubmit = () => {
  60      void fetchProducts(true);
  61    };
  62  
  63    const handleSelectCategory = (categoryId: number | null) => {
  64      setSelectedCategory(categoryId);
  65      setTimeout(() => {
  66        void fetchProducts(true);
  67      }, 0);
  68    };
  69  
  70    const handleSelectPrice = (min?: number, max?: number) => {
  71      setPriceRange(min, max);
  72      setTimeout(() => {
  73        void fetchProducts(true);
  74      }, 0);
  75    };
  76  
  77    return (
  78      <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
  79        <BrandHeader />
  80        <FlatList
  81          data={products}
  82          keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
  83          numColumns={2}
  84          columnWrapperStyle={styles.productRow}
  85          contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
  86          onEndReachedThreshold={0.4}
  87          onEndReached={() => {
  88            if (hasMore && status === "success") {
  89              void loadMore();
  90            }
  91          }}
  92          refreshControl={
  93            <RefreshControl
  94              refreshing={status === "loading" && products.length > 0}
  95              onRefresh={() => void fetchProducts(true)}
  96              tintColor={colorTokens.primary}
  97            />
  98          }
  99          ListHeaderComponent={
 100            <CatalogHeader
 101              count={products.length}
 102              search={search}
 103              onSearchChange={setSearch}
 104              onSearchSubmit={handleSearchSubmit}
 105              categories={categories}
 106              selectedCategoryId={selectedCategoryId}
 107              onSelectCategory={handleSelectCategory}
 108              selectedMinPrice={minPrice}
 109              selectedMaxPrice={maxPrice}
 110              onSelectPrice={handleSelectPrice}
 111              user={user}
 112            />
 113          }
 114          renderItem={({ item }) => (
 115            <ProductCard
 116              product={item}
 117              onPress={() => router.push(`/product/${item.id}` as any)}
 118            />
 119          )}
 120          ListFooterComponent={
 121            status === "loadingMore" ? (
 122              <View style={styles.loadingMoreContainer}>
 123                <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={colorTokens.primary} />
 124                <Text style={styles.loadingMoreText}>Chargement des produits...</Text>
 125              </View>
 126            ) : null
 127          }
 128          ListEmptyComponent={
 129            status === "loading" ? (
 130              <Loader message="Chargement du catalogue..." />
 131            ) : status === "error" ? (
 132              <ErrorState
 133                message={error || "Impossible de charger les produits."}
 134                onRetry={() => void fetchProducts(true)}
 135              />
 136            ) : (
 137              <EmptyState
 138                icon="🔍"
 139                title="Aucun produit trouvé"
 140                description="Aucun article ne correspond à vos filtres actuels."
 141                actionTitle="Réinitialiser les filtres"
 142                onAction={() => {
 143                  resetFilters();
 144                  void fetchProducts(true);
 145                }}
 146              />
 147            )
 148          }
 149        />
 150      </SafeAreaView>
 151    );
 152  }
 153  
 154  function CatalogHeader({
 155    count,
 156    search,
 157    onSearchChange,
 158    onSearchSubmit,
 159    categories,
 160    selectedCategoryId,
 161    onSelectCategory,
 162    selectedMinPrice,
 163    selectedMaxPrice,
 164    onSelectPrice,
 165    user,
 166  }: {
 167    count: number;
 168    search: string;
 169    onSearchChange: (val: string) => void;
 170    onSearchSubmit: () => void;
 171    categories: Category[];
 172    selectedCategoryId: number | null;
 173    onSelectCategory: (id: number | null) => void;
 174    selectedMinPrice?: number;
 175    selectedMaxPrice?: number;
 176    onSelectPrice: (min?: number, max?: number) => void;
 177    user: { name: string; email: string; role: string } | null;
 178  }) {
 179    return (
 180      <View style={styles.header}>
 181        {/* Search Input Bar */}
 182        <View style={styles.searchContainer}>
 183          <Text style={styles.searchIcon}>⌕</Text>
 184          <TextInput
 185            accessibilityLabel="Rechercher des produits"
 186            placeholder="Chercher un produit..."
 187            placeholderTextColor={colorTokens.muted}
 188            value={search}
 189            onChangeText={onSearchChange}
 190            onSubmitEditing={onSearchSubmit}
 191            style={styles.searchInput}
 192            returnKeyType="search"
 193          />
 194          {search ? (
 195            <Pressable
 196              style={styles.searchClearBtn}
 197              onPress={() => {
 198                onSearchChange("");
 199                setTimeout(onSearchSubmit, 0);
 200              }}
 201            >
 202              <Text style={{ fontSize: 14, color: colorTokens.muted }}>✕</Text>
 203            </Pressable>
 204          ) : null}
 205          {!search ? <Text style={styles.micIcon}>♩</Text> : null}
 206        </View>
 207  
 208        {/* Category Pills */}
 209        <View style={styles.filterSection}>
 210          <Text style={styles.filterLabel}>CATÉGORIES</Text>
 211          <FlatList
 212            horizontal
 213            data={[{ id: 0, name: "Toutes" }, ...categories]}
 214            keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
 215            showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
 216            contentContainerStyle={styles.categoryList}
 217            renderItem={({ item }) => {
 218              const isSelected =
 219                item.id === 0
 220                  ? selectedCategoryId === null
 221                  : selectedCategoryId === item.id;
 222  
 223              return (
 224                <Pressable
 225                  accessibilityRole="button"
 226                  accessibilityState={{ selected: isSelected }}
 227                  onPress={() => onSelectCategory(item.id === 0 ? null : item.id)}
 228                  style={[styles.categoryButton, isSelected && styles.categoryButtonSelected]}
 229                >
 230                  <Text style={[styles.categoryText, isSelected && styles.categoryTextSelected]}>
 231                    {item.name}
 232                  </Text>
 233                </Pressable>
 234              );
 235            }}
 236          />
 237        </View>
 238  
 239        {/* Price Range Pills */}
 240        <View style={styles.filterSection}>
 241          <Text style={styles.filterLabel}>BUDGET (MAD)</Text>
 242          <View style={styles.pricePillsRow}>
 243            {PRICE_FILTERS.map((f, idx) => {
 244              const isSelected =
 245                selectedMinPrice === f.min && selectedMaxPrice === f.max;
 246  
 247              return (
 248                <Pressable
 249                  key={idx}
 250                  onPress={() => onSelectPrice(f.min, f.max)}
 251                  style={[styles.pricePill, isSelected && styles.pricePillSelected]}
 252                >
 253                  <Text
 254                    style={[styles.pricePillText, isSelected && styles.pricePillTextSelected]}
 255                  >
 256                    {f.label}
 257                  </Text>
 258                </Pressable>
 259              );
 260            })}
 261          </View>
 262        </View>
 263      </View>
 264    );
 265  }
 266  
 267  function ProductCard({
 268    product,
 269    onPress,
 270  }: {
 271    product: Product;
 272    onPress: () => void;
 273  }) {
 274    const isOutOfStock = product.stock <= 0;
 275    const [imageFailed, setImageFailed] = useState(false);
 276  
 277    return (
 278      <Pressable
 279        style={({ pressed }) => [styles.productCard, pressed && styles.productCardPressed]}
 280        onPress={onPress}
 281      >
 282        <View style={styles.productArtwork}>
 283          {product.imageUrl && !imageFailed ? (
 284            <Image
 285              source={{ uri: product.imageUrl }}
 286              style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
 287              resizeMode="cover"
 288              onError={() => setImageFailed(true)}
 289            />
 290          ) : (
 291            <Text style={styles.productArtworkMark}>{product.name.charAt(0)}</Text>
 292          )}
 293          <View style={styles.codBadge}><Text style={styles.codBadgeText}>✦ COD</Text></View>
 294        </View>
 295  
 296        <Text numberOfLines={2} style={styles.productName}>
 297          {product.name}
 298        </Text>
 299        <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.productCategory}>{product.category?.name ?? "Sélection ShopMate"}</Text>
 300  
 301        <View style={styles.productFooter}>
 302          <Text style={styles.productPrice}>{Number(product.price).toFixed(0)} DH</Text>
 303          <View style={[styles.addButton, isOutOfStock && styles.addButtonDisabled]}><Text style={styles.addButtonText}>{isOutOfStock ? "×" : "+"}</Text></View>
 304        </View>
 305      </Pressable>
 306    );
 307  }
 308  
 309  const styles = StyleSheet.create({
 310    screen: {
 311      flex: 1,
 312      backgroundColor: colorTokens.surface,
 313    },
 314    listContent: {

mobile/app/(tabs)/orders.tsx

كل شاشة كتربط state وعناصر العرض بدالة أو API واضحة.

   1  import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";
   2  import {
   3    Alert,
   4    FlatList,
   5    Pressable,
   6    RefreshControl,
   7    StyleSheet,
   8    Text,
   9    View,
  10  } from "react-native";
  11  import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
  12  import { apiClient } from "@/src/api/client";
  13  import {
  14    colorTokens,
  15    radiusTokens,
  16    spacingTokens,
  17    typographyTokens,
  18  } from "@/src/theme/tokens";
  19  import { Card } from "@/src/components/Card";
  20  import { Loader } from "@/src/components/Loader";
  21  import { EmptyState } from "@/src/components/EmptyState";
  22  import { ErrorState } from "@/src/components/ErrorState";
  23  import { Button } from "@/src/components/Button";
  24  import { BrandHeader } from "@/src/components/BrandHeader";
  25  
  26  interface OrderItemData {
  27    id: number;
  28    productId: number;
  29    quantity: number;
  30    unitPrice: number;
  31    product?: {
  32      name: string;
  33      price: number;
  34      sku?: string;
  35    };
  36  }
  37  
  38  interface OrderData {
  39    id: number;
  40    status: "PENDING" | "CONFIRMED" | "SHIPPED" | "DELIVERED" | "CANCELLED";
  41    total: number;
  42    createdAt: string;
  43    items?: OrderItemData[];
  44  }
  45  
  46  const STATUS_STEPS = ["Reçue", "Confirmée", "Expédiée", "En cours", "Livrée"];
  47  
  48  function getStatusStepIndex(status: string): number {
  49    switch (status) {
  50      case "PENDING":
  51        return 0;
  52      case "CONFIRMED":
  53        return 1;
  54      case "SHIPPED":
  55        return 2;
  56      case "DELIVERED":
  57        return 4;
  58      default:
  59        return 0;
  60    }
  61  }
  62  
  63  function getStatusBadge(status: string) {
  64    switch (status) {
  65      case "PENDING":
  66        return { text: "En attente", color: "#F5A623", bg: "rgba(245, 166, 35, 0.12)" };
  67      case "CONFIRMED":
  68        return { text: "Confirmée", color: "#3B82F6", bg: "rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.12)" };
  69      case "SHIPPED":
  70        return { text: "Expédiée", color: "#8B5CF6", bg: "rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.12)" };
  71      case "DELIVERED":
  72        return { text: "Livrée", color: "#10B981", bg: "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12)" };
  73      case "CANCELLED":
  74        return { text: "Annulée", color: colorTokens.danger, bg: colorTokens.errorContainer };
  75      default:
  76        return { text: status, color: colorTokens.muted, bg: colorTokens.surfaceContainerLow };
  77    }
  78  }
  79  
  80  export default function OrdersScreen() {
  81    const [orders, setOrders] = useState<OrderData[]>([]);
  82    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  83    const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  84    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  85  
  86    const fetchOrders = useCallback(async () => {
  87      try {
  88        setError(null);
  89        const res = await apiClient.get<{ orders: OrderData[] }>("/api/orders");
  90        setOrders(res.data.orders || []);
  91      } catch (err: any) {
  92        setError(err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible de charger les commandes.");
  93      } finally {
  94        setIsLoading(false);
  95        setIsRefreshing(false);
  96      }
  97    }, []);
  98  
  99    useEffect(() => {
 100      fetchOrders();
 101    }, [fetchOrders]);
 102  
 103    const onRefresh = () => {
 104      setIsRefreshing(true);
 105      fetchOrders();
 106    };
 107  
 108    const handleCancelOrder = async (orderId: number) => {
 109      Alert.alert(
 110        "Annuler la commande ?",
 111        `Voulez-vous vraiment annuler la commande #${orderId} ?`,
 112        [
 113          { text: "Garder la commande", style: "cancel" },
 114          {
 115            text: "Oui, annuler",
 116            style: "destructive",
 117            onPress: async () => {
 118              try {
 119                await apiClient.post(`/api/orders/${orderId}/cancel`, {
 120                  confirmed: true,
 121                });
 122                fetchOrders();
 123              } catch {
 124                Alert.alert("Erreur", "Impossible d'annuler la commande. Réessayez.");
 125              }
 126            },
 127          },
 128        ]
 129      );
 130    };
 131  
 132    if (isLoading) {
 133      return (
 134        <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
 135          <BrandHeader />
 136          <View style={styles.header}>
 137            <Text style={styles.title}>Mes Commandes</Text>
 138            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>Suivez l'état de vos livraisons.</Text>
 139          </View>
 140          <Loader message="Chargement de vos commandes..." />
 141        </SafeAreaView>
 142      );
 143    }
 144  
 145    if (error && orders.length === 0) {
 146      return (
 147        <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
 148          <BrandHeader />
 149          <View style={styles.header}>
 150            <Text style={styles.title}>Mes Commandes</Text>
 151          </View>
 152          <ErrorState message={error} onRetry={fetchOrders} />
 153        </SafeAreaView>
 154      );
 155    }
 156  
 157    return (
 158      <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
 159        <BrandHeader />
 160        <FlatList
 161          data={orders}
 162          keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
 163          contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
 164          refreshControl={
 165            <RefreshControl
 166              refreshing={isRefreshing}
 167              onRefresh={onRefresh}
 168              tintColor={colorTokens.primary}
 169            />
 170          }
 171          ListHeaderComponent={
 172            <View style={styles.header}>
 173              <View>
 174                <Text style={styles.title}>Mes Commandes</Text>
 175                <Text style={styles.subtitle}>Suivez l'état de vos livraisons.</Text>
 176              </View>
 177              <View style={styles.orderCountBadge}>
 178                <Text style={styles.orderCountText}>{orders.length} commande(s)</Text>
 179              </View>
 180            </View>
 181          }
 182          renderItem={({ item, index }) => (
 183            <OrderCard
 184              order={item}
 185              isFirst={index === 0}
 186              onCancel={() => handleCancelOrder(item.id)}
 187            />
 188          )}
 189          ListEmptyComponent={
 190            <EmptyState
 191              icon="📦"
 192              title="Aucune commande pour le moment"
 193              description="Commandez vos articles préférés avec paiement sécurisé à la réception (COD)."
 194              actionTitle="Explorer la boutique"
 195              onAction={() => {}}
 196            />
 197          }
 198        />
 199      </SafeAreaView>
 200    );
 201  }
 202  
 203  function OrderCard({
 204    order,
 205    isFirst,
 206    onCancel,
 207  }: {
 208    order: OrderData;
 209    isFirst: boolean;
 210    onCancel: () => void;
 211  }) {
 212    const badge = getStatusBadge(order.status);
 213    const activeStep = getStatusStepIndex(order.status);
 214    const isCancelled = order.status === "CANCELLED";
 215  
 216    return (
 217      <Card variant="surface" style={styles.orderCard}>
 218        {/* Top row */}
 219        <View style={styles.orderCardHeader}>
 220          <View>
 221            <View style={styles.orderNumberRow}>
 222              <Text style={styles.orderNumber}>Commande #{order.id}</Text>
 223              <View style={[styles.badge, { backgroundColor: badge.bg }]}>
 224                <View style={[styles.badgeDot, { backgroundColor: badge.color }]} />
 225                <Text style={[styles.badgeText, { color: badge.color }]}>{badge.text}</Text>
 226              </View>
 227            </View>
 228            <Text style={styles.orderDate}>
 229              {new Date(order.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("fr-FR", {
 230                day: "numeric",
 231                month: "short",
 232                year: "numeric",
 233              })}
 234            </Text>
 235          </View>
 236  
 237          <View style={styles.totalBlock}>
 238            <Text style={styles.totalPrice}>{Number(order.total).toFixed(2)} MAD</Text>
 239            <View style={styles.codBadge}>
 240              <Text style={styles.codIcon}>🚚</Text>
 241              <Text style={styles.codText}>COD</Text>
 242            </View>
 243          </View>
 244        </View>
 245  
 246        {/* Progress Timeline (for active / non-cancelled orders) */}
 247        {!isCancelled ? (
 248          <View style={styles.timelineContainer}>
 249            <Text style={styles.timelineLabel}>ÉTAT D'AVANCEMENT</Text>
 250            <View style={styles.timelineBar}>
 251              {STATUS_STEPS.map((step, idx) => {
 252                const isPast = idx <= activeStep;
 253                const isCurrent = idx === activeStep;
 254  
 255                return (
 256                  <View key={step} style={styles.timelineStep}>
 257                    <View
 258                      style={[
 259                        styles.stepDot,
 260                        isPast && styles.stepDotActive,
 261                        isCurrent && styles.stepDotCurrent,
 262                      ]}
 263                    >
 264                      {isPast ? <Text style={styles.stepCheckmark}>✓</Text> : null}
 265                    </View>
 266                    <Text
 267                      style={[
 268                        styles.stepText,
 269                        isCurrent && styles.stepTextCurrent,
 270                        !isPast && styles.stepTextInactive,
 271                      ]}
 272                    >
 273                      {step}
 274                    </Text>
 275                  </View>
 276                );
 277              })}
 278            </View>
 279          </View>
 280        ) : null}
 281  
 282        {/* Items preview */}
 283        {order.items && order.items.length > 0 ? (
 284          <View style={styles.itemsList}>
 285            {order.items.map((it, idx) => (
 286              <View key={idx} style={styles.itemRow}>
 287                <Text style={styles.itemQty}>{it.quantity}x</Text>
 288                <Text style={styles.itemName} numberOfLines={1}>
 289                  {it.product?.name || `Produit #${it.productId}`}
 290                </Text>
 291                <Text style={styles.itemPrice}>
 292                  {(Number(it.unitPrice) * it.quantity).toFixed(2)} MAD
 293                </Text>
 294              </View>
 295            ))}
 296          </View>
 297        ) : null}
 298  
 299        {/* Actions */}
 300        {order.status === "PENDING" ? (
 301          <Button
 302            title="Annuler la commande"
 303            onPress={onCancel}
 304            variant="outline"
 305            size="sm"
 306            style={styles.cancelBtn}
 307            textStyle={{ color: colorTokens.danger }}
 308          />
 309        ) : null}
 310      </Card>
 311    );
 312  }
 313

 316      flex: 1,

 321      paddingBottom: spacingTokens.xxl,
 322    },
 323    header: {
 324      flexDirection: "row",

mobile/app/(tabs)/profile.tsx

كل شاشة كتربط state وعناصر العرض بدالة أو API واضحة.

   1  import React from "react";
   2  import {
   3    Alert,
   4    Image,
   5    Pressable,
   6    ScrollView,
   7    StyleSheet,
   8    Text,
   9    View,
  10  } from "react-native";
  11  import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
  12  import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/stores/authStore";
  13  import {
  14    colorTokens,
  15    radiusTokens,
  16    spacingTokens,
  17    typographyTokens,
  18  } from "@/src/theme/tokens";
  19  import { Card } from "@/src/components/Card";
  20  import { Button } from "@/src/components/Button";
  21  import { BrandHeader } from "@/src/components/BrandHeader";
  22  import { router, type Href } from "expo-router";
  23  
  24  export default function ProfileScreen() {
  25    const user = useAuthStore((state) => state.user);
  26    const logout = useAuthStore((state) => state.logout);
  27  
  28    const handleLogout = () => {
  29      Alert.alert(
  30        "Déconnexion",
  31        "Voulez-vous vraiment vous déconnecter de ShopMate AI ?",
  32        [
  33          { text: "Annuler", style: "cancel" },
  34          { text: "Déconnexion", style: "destructive", onPress: () => void logout() },
  35        ]
  36      );
  37    };
  38  
  39    const displayName = user?.name || "Client ShopMate";
  40    const initials = displayName
  41      .split(" ")
  42      .map((w) => w[0])
  43      .join("")
  44      .slice(0, 2)
  45      .toUpperCase();
  46  
  47    return (
  48      <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
  49        <BrandHeader />
  50        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
  51          {/* Profile Card Header */}
  52          <View style={styles.profileHeader}>
  53            <View style={styles.avatarContainer}>
  54              <Image source={require("../../assets/images/profile-demo.png")} style={styles.avatar} accessibilityLabel={`Photo de ${displayName}`} />
  55              <View style={styles.roleBadge}>
  56                <Text style={styles.roleText}>{user?.role || "CUSTOMER"}</Text>
  57              </View>
  58            </View>
  59  
  60            <Text style={styles.userName}>{displayName}</Text>
  61            <Text style={styles.userEmail}>{user?.email}</Text>
  62  
  63            <View style={styles.locationBadge}>
  64              <Text style={styles.locationIcon}>📍</Text>
  65              <Text style={styles.locationText}>Casablanca • Boutique Démo</Text>
  66            </View>
  67          </View>
  68  
  69          {/* Quick Stats */}
  70          <View style={styles.statsRow}>
  71            <Card variant="surface" style={styles.statCard}>
  72              <Text style={styles.statValue}>100%</Text>
  73              <Text style={styles.statLabel}>PAIEMENT COD</Text>
  74            </Card>
  75            <Card variant="surface" style={styles.statCard}>
  76              <Text style={[styles.statValue, { color: colorTokens.secondary }]}>✨</Text>
  77              <Text style={styles.statLabel}>CLIENT PRIVILÈGE</Text>
  78            </Card>
  79          </View>
  80  
  81          {/* Settings Group: Compte */}
  82          <View style={styles.menuSection}>
  83            {user?.role === "ADMIN" ? (
  84              <>
  85                <MenuItem
  86                  icon="🧰"
  87                  title="Gestion du catalogue"
  88                  subtitle="Produits, catégories, prix et stock"
  89                  onPress={() => router.push("/(tabs)/admin-catalogue" as Href)}
  90                />
  91                <MenuItem
  92                  icon="📚"
  93                  title="Documents de connaissance IA"

  95                  onPress={() => router.push("/(tabs)/admin-documents" as Href)}
  96                />
  97                <MenuItem

 166  function MenuItem({
 167    icon,
 168    title,
 169    subtitle,
 170    badge,
 171    onPress,
 172  }: {
 173    icon: string;
 174    title: string;
 175    subtitle: string;
 176    badge?: string;
 177    onPress: () => void;
 178  }) {
 179    return (
 180      <Pressable
 181        style={({ pressed }) => [styles.menuItem, pressed && styles.menuItemPressed]}
 182        onPress={onPress}
 183      >
 184        <View style={styles.menuIconContainer}>
 185          <Text style={styles.menuIcon}>{icon}</Text>
 186        </View>
 187        <View style={styles.menuTextContainer}>
 188          <Text style={styles.menuTitle}>{title}</Text>
 189          <Text style={styles.menuSubtitle}>{subtitle}</Text>
 190        </View>
 191        {badge ? (
 192          <View style={styles.itemBadge}>
 193            <Text style={styles.itemBadgeText}>{badge}</Text>
 194          </View>
 195        ) : null}
 196        <Text style={styles.chevron}>›</Text>
 197      </Pressable>
 198    );
 199  }
