mobile/app/_layout.tsx

Expo Router كيربط بنية الملفات بالشاشات، وRootLayout كيتابع حالة الجلسة.

   1  import { useEffect } from "react";
   2  import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, Text, View } from "react-native";
   3  import { Stack, useRouter, useSegments } from "expo-router";
   4  import { StatusBar } from "expo-status-bar";
   5  import { SafeAreaProvider } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
   6  import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/stores/authStore";
   7  import {
   8    colorTokens,
   9    radiusTokens,
  10    spacingTokens,
  11    typographyTokens,
  12  } from "@/src/theme/tokens";
  13  
  14  function SplashScreen() {
  15    return (
  16      <View style={styles.splashContainer}>
  17        <View style={styles.logoBadge}>
  18          <Text style={styles.logoIcon}>🛍️</Text>
  19        </View>
  20        <Text style={styles.splashTitle}>ShopMate AI</Text>
  21        <Text style={styles.splashSubtitle}>Assistant Shopping Intelligent</Text>
  22        <ActivityIndicator
  23          size="large"
  24          color={colorTokens.brand}
  25          style={styles.spinner}
  26        />
  27      </View>
  28    );
  29  }
  30  
  31  export default function RootLayout() {
  32    const segments = useSegments();
  33    const router = useRouter();
  34    const isAuthenticated = useAuthStore((state) => state.isAuthenticated);
  35    const isLoading = useAuthStore((state) => state.isLoading);
  36    const initializeAuth = useAuthStore((state) => state.initializeAuth);
  37  
  38    // Initialize auth tokens from SecureStore on app startup
  39    useEffect(() => {
  40      void initializeAuth();
  41    }, [initializeAuth]);
  42  
  43    // Route Guard: enforce authentication state vs public auth screens
  44    useEffect(() => {
  45      if (isLoading) return;
  46  
  47      const inAuthGroup = segments[0] === "(auth)";
  48  
  49      if (!isAuthenticated && !inAuthGroup) {
  50        // Unauthenticated user trying to access protected screens -> redirect to login
  51        router.replace("/(auth)/login");
  52      } else if (isAuthenticated && inAuthGroup) {
  53        // Authenticated user trying to access login/register -> redirect to tabs
  54        router.replace("/(tabs)");
  55      }
  56    }, [isAuthenticated, isLoading, segments, router]);
  57  
  58    if (isLoading) {
  59      return (
  60        <SafeAreaProvider>
  61          <StatusBar style="dark" />
  62          <SplashScreen />
  63        </SafeAreaProvider>
  64      );
  65    }
  66  
  67    return (
  68      <SafeAreaProvider>
  69        <StatusBar style="dark" />
  70        <Stack screenOptions={{ headerShown: false }}>
  71          <Stack.Screen name="(auth)" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
  72          <Stack.Screen name="(tabs)" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
  73          <Stack.Screen name="product/[id]" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
  74        </Stack>
  75      </SafeAreaProvider>
  76    );
  77  }
  78  
  79  const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  80    splashContainer: {
  81      flex: 1,
  82      backgroundColor: colorTokens.surface,
  83      alignItems: "center",
  84      justifyContent: "center",
  85      paddingHorizontal: spacingTokens.lg,
  86    },
  87    logoBadge: {
  88      width: 80,
  89      height: 80,
  90      borderRadius: radiusTokens.lg,
  91      backgroundColor: colorTokens.surfaceContainer,
  92      alignItems: "center",
  93      justifyContent: "center",
  94      marginBottom: spacingTokens.md,
  95    },
  96    logoIcon: {
  97      fontSize: 40,
  98    },
  99    splashTitle: {
 100      fontSize: typographyTokens.title.fontSize,
 101      fontWeight: typographyTokens.title.fontWeight,
 102      color: colorTokens.ink,
 103      marginBottom: spacingTokens.xs,
 104    },
 105    splashSubtitle: {
 106      fontSize: typographyTokens.body.fontSize,
 107      color: colorTokens.muted,
 108    },
 109    spinner: {
 110      marginTop: spacingTokens.xl,
 111    },
 112  });
mobile/app/modal.tsx

كل شاشة كتربط state وعناصر العرض بدالة أو API واضحة.

mobile/app/product/[id].tsx
الشاشة كتعرض وتجمع الاختيارات، والـstore كيرسل الفلاتر وكيحتافظ بالنتائج.
   1  import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";
   2  import {
   3    ActivityIndicator,
   4    Alert,
   5    Image,
   6    Pressable,
   7    ScrollView,
   8    StyleSheet,
   9    Text,
  10    View,
  11  } from "react-native";
  12  import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
  13  import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from "expo-router";
  14  import { apiClient } from "@/src/api/client";
  15  import { useCatalogStore, type Product } from "@/src/stores/catalogStore";
  16  import {
  17    colorTokens,
  18    radiusTokens,
  19    spacingTokens,
  20    typographyTokens,
  21  } from "@/src/theme/tokens";
  22  import { Button } from "@/src/components/Button";
  23  import { Card } from "@/src/components/Card";
  24  import { Loader } from "@/src/components/Loader";
  25  import { ErrorState } from "@/src/components/ErrorState";
  26  
  27  const DEFAULT_SIZES = ["S", "M", "L", "XL"];
  28  const SHOE_SIZES = ["40", "41", "42", "43", "44"];
  29  
  30  export default function ProductDetailScreen() {
  31    const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  32    const router = useRouter();
  33  
  34    const [product, setProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);
  35    const [liveStock, setLiveStock] = useState<number | null>(null);
  36    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  37    const [imageFailed, setImageFailed] = useState(false);
  38    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  39  
  40    const [selectedSize, setSelectedSize] = useState<string>("M");
  41    const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState<number>(1);
  42    const [isOrdering, setIsOrdering] = useState<boolean>(false);
  43  
  44    const productId = Number(id);
  45  
  46    const fetchDetails = useCallback(async () => {
  47      if (!productId || isNaN(productId)) {
  48        setError("Identifiant de produit invalide.");
  49        setIsLoading(false);
  50        return;
  51      }
  52  
  53      setIsLoading(true);
  54      setError(null);
  55  
  56      try {
  57        // 1. Fetch full product details
  58        const response = await apiClient.get<{ product: Product }>(
  59          `/api/products/${productId}`
  60        );
  61        const prod = response.data.product;
  62        setProduct(prod);
  63  
  64        // 2. Fetch live stock and price check (never hallucinated)
  65        try {
  66          const stockRes = await apiClient.get<{ stock: number; inStock: boolean }>(
  67            `/api/products/${productId}/stock`
  68          );
  69          setLiveStock(stockRes.data.stock);
  70        } catch {
  71          setLiveStock(prod.stock);
  72        }
  73      } catch (err: any) {
  74        setError(
  75          err.response?.data?.error ||
  76            "Impossible de charger les détails de ce produit."
  77        );
  78      } finally {
  79        setIsLoading(false);
  80      }
  81    }, [productId]);
  82  
  83    useEffect(() => {
  84      fetchDetails();
  85    }, [fetchDetails]);
  86  
  87    const handleOrderCOD = async () => {
  88      if (!product) return;
  89  
  90      const currentStock = liveStock ?? product.stock;
  91      if (currentStock < quantity) {
  92        Alert.alert(
  93          "Stock insuffisant",
  94          `Désolé, il ne reste que ${currentStock} exemplaire(s) en stock.`
  95        );
  96        return;
  97      }
  98  
  99      Alert.alert(
 100        "Confirmation de commande COD",
 101        `Confirmez-vous votre commande de ${quantity}x "${product.name}" pour un total de ${(
 102          Number(product.price) * quantity
 103        ).toFixed(2)} MAD payable à la livraison ?`,
 104        [
 105          { text: "Annuler", style: "cancel" },
 106          {
 107            text: "Confirmer la commande",
 108            onPress: async () => {
 109              setIsOrdering(true);
 110              try {
 111                await apiClient.post("/api/orders", {
 112                  confirmed: true,
 113                  items: [{ productId: product.id, quantity }],
 114                });
 115  
 116                Alert.alert(
 117                  "Commande confirmée ! 🎉",
 118                  "Votre commande en paiement à la livraison a été enregistrée avec succès. Vous pouvez la suivre dans l'onglet Commandes.",
 119                  [
 120                    {
 121                      text: "Voir mes commandes",
 122                      onPress: () => router.push("/(tabs)/orders" as any),
 123                    },
 124                  ]
 125                );
 126              } catch (err: any) {
 127                Alert.alert(
 128                  "Erreur",
 129                  err.response?.data?.error ||
 130                    "Une erreur est survenue lors de la création de votre commande."
 131                );
 132              } finally {
 133                setIsOrdering(false);
 134              }
 135            },
 136          },
 137        ]
 138      );
 139    };
 140  
 141    if (isLoading) {
 142      return (
 143        <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
 144          <View style={styles.topBar}>
 145            <Pressable
 146              style={({ pressed }) => [styles.backBtn, pressed && styles.pressed]}
 147              onPress={() => router.back()}
 148            >
 149              <Text style={styles.backBtnText}>‹ Retour</Text>
 150            </Pressable>
 151          </View>
 152          <Loader message="Chargement des détails du produit..." />
 153        </SafeAreaView>
 154      );
 155    }
 156  
 157    if (error || !product) {
 158      return (
 159        <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
 160          <View style={styles.topBar}>
 161            <Pressable
 162              style={({ pressed }) => [styles.backBtn, pressed && styles.pressed]}
 163              onPress={() => router.back()}
 164            >
 165              <Text style={styles.backBtnText}>‹ Retour</Text>
 166            </Pressable>
 167          </View>
 168          <ErrorState
 169            message={error || "Produit introuvable."}
 170            onRetry={fetchDetails}
 171          />
 172        </SafeAreaView>
 173      );
 174    }
 175  
 176    const stockAvailable = liveStock !== null ? liveStock : product.stock;
 177    const isOutOfStock = stockAvailable <= 0;
 178    const isShoe = product.category?.name?.toLowerCase().includes("chaussure");
 179    const availableSizes = isShoe ? SHOE_SIZES : DEFAULT_SIZES;
 180  
 181    return (
 182      <SafeAreaView style={styles.screen} edges={["top"]}>
 183        {/* Navigation Top Bar */}
 184        <View style={styles.topBar}>
 185          <Pressable
 186            style={({ pressed }) => [styles.backBtn, pressed && styles.pressed]}
 187            onPress={() => router.back()}
 188          >
 189            <Text style={styles.backBtnText}>‹ Boutique</Text>
 190          </Pressable>
 191          <View style={styles.topBadge}>
 192            <Text style={styles.topBadgeText}>PAIEMENT À LA LIVRAISON</Text>
 193          </View>
 194        </View>
 195  
 196        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
 197          {/* Product Artwork / Image Banner */}
 198          <View style={styles.artworkContainer}>
 199            {product.imageUrl && !imageFailed ? (
 200              <Image
 201                source={{ uri: product.imageUrl }}
 202                style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
 203                resizeMode="cover"
 204                onError={() => setImageFailed(true)}
 205              />
 206            ) : (
 207              <View style={styles.artworkPattern}>
 208                <Text style={styles.artworkInitial}>{product.name.charAt(0)}</Text>
 209              </View>
 210            )}
 211            <View style={styles.categoryPill}>
 212              <Text style={styles.categoryPillText}>
 213                {product.category?.name || "Boutique ShopMate"}
 214              </Text>
 215            </View>
 216          </View>
 217  
 218          {/* Content Section */}
 219          <View style={styles.detailsContent}>
 220            {/* Title & Price */}
 221            <Text style={styles.productTitle}>{product.name}</Text>
 222            <View style={styles.priceRow}>
 223              <Text style={styles.price}>{Number(product.price).toFixed(2)} MAD</Text>
 224              {product.sku ? (
 225                <Text style={styles.skuText}>RÉF: {product.sku}</Text>
 226              ) : null}
 227            </View>
 228  
 229            {/* Stock Status Indicator */}
 230            <View style={styles.stockStatusRow}>
 231              <View
 232                style={[
 233                  styles.stockDot,
 234                  isOutOfStock ? styles.stockDotEmpty : styles.stockDotSuccess,
 235                ]}
 236              />
 237              <Text
 238                style={[
 239                  styles.stockStatusText,
 240                  isOutOfStock ? styles.stockTextEmpty : styles.stockTextSuccess,
 241                ]}
 242              >
 243                {isOutOfStock
 244                  ? "Rupture de stock"
 245                  : `En stock (${stockAvailable} disponible${stockAvailable > 1 ? "s" : ""})`}
 246              </Text>
 247            </View>
 248  
 249            {/* Sizes Selection */}
 250            <View style={styles.sectionBlock}>
 251              <Text style={styles.sectionLabel}>CHOIX DE LA TAILLE</Text>
 252              <View style={styles.sizesRow}>
 253                {availableSizes.map((size) => {
 254                  const isSelected = selectedSize === size;
 255                  return (
 256                    <Pressable
 257                      key={size}
 258                      style={({ pressed }) => [
 259                        styles.sizeOption,
 260                        isSelected && styles.sizeOptionSelected,
 261                        pressed && styles.pressed,
 262                      ]}
 263                      onPress={() => setSelectedSize(size)}
 264                    >
 265                      <Text
 266                        style={[
 267                          styles.sizeText,
 268                          isSelected && styles.sizeTextSelected,
 269                        ]}
 270                      >
 271                        {size}
 272                      </Text>
 273                    </Pressable>
 274                  );
 275                })}
 276              </View>
 277            </View>
 278  
 279            {/* Quantity Selector */}
 280            <View style={styles.sectionBlock}>
 281              <Text style={styles.sectionLabel}>QUANTITÉ</Text>
 282              <View style={styles.quantityRow}>
 283                <Pressable
 284                  style={({ pressed }) => [
 285                    styles.qtyButton,
 286                    quantity <= 1 && styles.qtyButtonDisabled,
 287                    pressed && styles.pressed,
 288                  ]}
 289                  onPress={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.max(1, q - 1))}
 290                  disabled={quantity <= 1}
 291                >
 292                  <Text style={styles.qtyButtonText}>−</Text>
 293                </Pressable>
 294                <Text style={styles.qtyNumber}>{quantity}</Text>
 295                <Pressable
 296                  style={({ pressed }) => [
 297                    styles.qtyButton,
 298                    quantity >= stockAvailable && styles.qtyButtonDisabled,
 299                    pressed && styles.pressed,
 300                  ]}
 301                  onPress={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.min(stockAvailable, q + 1))}
 302                  disabled={quantity >= stockAvailable}
 303                >
 304                  <Text style={styles.qtyButtonText}>+</Text>
 305                </Pressable>
 306                <Text style={styles.totalCalc}>
 307                  Total: {(Number(product.price) * quantity).toFixed(2)} MAD
 308                </Text>
 309              </View>
 310            </View>
 311  
 312            {/* Description */}
 313            <View style={styles.sectionBlock}>
 314              <Text style={styles.sectionLabel}>DESCRIPTION DU PRODUIT</Text>
 315              <Text style={styles.description}>
 316                {product.description ||
 317                  "Produit de haute qualité sélectionné avec soin pour votre satisfaction. Livré partout au Maroc avec paiement sécurisé à la réception."}
 318              </Text>
 319            </View>
 320  
 321            {/* COD Reassurance Card */}
 322            <Card variant="highlight" style={styles.codCard}>
 323              <View style={styles.codRow}>
 324                <Text style={{ fontSize: 24 }}>🚚</Text>
 325                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
 326                  <Text style={styles.codTitle}>Paiement à la livraison (COD)</Text>
 327                  <Text style={styles.codSubtitle}>
 328                    Payez en dirhams uniquement à la réception de votre colis. Livraison express sous 24 à 48h.
 329                  </Text>
 330                </View>
 331              </View>
 332            </Card>
 333          </View>
 334        </ScrollView>
 335  
 336        {/* Bottom Sticky Action Bar */}
 337        <View style={styles.bottomBar}>
 338          <View style={styles.bottomPriceBlock}>
 339            <Text style={styles.bottomTotalLabel}>Total commande</Text>
 340            <Text style={styles.bottomTotalPrice}>
 341              {(Number(product.price) * quantity).toFixed(2)} MAD
 342            </Text>
 343          </View>
 344          <Button
 345            title={isOutOfStock ? "Rupture de stock" : "Commander en COD"}
 346            onPress={handleOrderCOD}
 347            disabled={isOutOfStock || isOrdering}
 348            loading={isOrdering}
 349            size="lg"
 350            style={styles.orderButton}
 351            icon={<Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>🛍️</Text>}
 352          />
 353        </View>
 354      </SafeAreaView>
 355    );
 356  }

mobile/src/api/agentChat.ts
الاتصال واحد، والخادم كيكتب فيه أحداث JSON مفصولة بسطرين.
   1  import { API_BASE_URL } from "../config/api";
   2  import { getTokens } from "../auth/tokenStorage";
   3  import { apiClient } from "./client";
   4  
   5  export interface ChatSource {
   6    name: string;
   7    score: number;
   8  }
   9  
  10  export interface StreamEventPayload {
  11    type:
  12      | "start"
  13      | "source"
  14      | "tool_call"
  15      | "tool_result"
  16      | "token"
  17      | "confirmation"
  18      | "done"
  19      | "error";
  20    conversationId?: number;
  21    sources?: ChatSource[];
  22    tool?: string;
  23    args?: any;
  24    result?: any;
  25    token?: string;
  26    confirmed?: boolean;
  27    orderId?: number;
  28    messageId?: number;
  29    message?: string;
  30  }
  31  
  32  export interface StreamChatCallbacks {
  33    onStart?: (conversationId: number) => void;
  34    onSources?: (sources: ChatSource[]) => void;
  35    onToolCall?: (tool: string, args: any) => void;
  36    onToolResult?: (tool: string, result: any) => void;
  37    onToken?: (token: string) => void;
  38    onConfirmation?: (confirmed: boolean, orderId?: number) => void;
  39    onDone?: (payload: {
  40      conversationId: number;
  41      messageId?: number;
  42      orderId?: number;
  43    }) => void;
  44    onError?: (error: {
  45      status?: number;
  46      message: string;
  47      isRateLimit?: boolean;
  48    }) => void;
  49  }
  50  
  51  export interface ActiveStream {
  52    abort: () => void;
  53  }
  54  
  55  /**
  56   * Robust React Native / Expo Server-Sent Events (SSE) client using XMLHttpRequest.
  57   * Supports token streaming, real-time tool telemetry, source citation, cancellation on unmount,
  58   * and handles rate limits (HTTP 429) & network disconnects.
  59   */
  60  export async function streamAgentChat(
  61    params: {
  62      message: string;
  63      conversationId?: number;
  64    },
  65    callbacks: StreamChatCallbacks
  66  ): Promise<ActiveStream> {
  67    const { accessToken } = await getTokens();
  68    const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
  69    let lastIndex = 0;
  70    let buffer = "";
  71    let isAborted = false;
  72  
  73    xhr.open("POST", `${API_BASE_URL}/api/agent/chat`, true);
  74    xhr.setRequestHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");
  75    xhr.setRequestHeader("Accept", "text/event-stream");
  76    if (accessToken) {
  77      xhr.setRequestHeader("Authorization", `Bearer ${accessToken}`);
  78    }
  79  
  80    const processBuffer = () => {
  81      const newText = xhr.responseText.slice(lastIndex);
  82      lastIndex = xhr.responseText.length;
  83      buffer += newText;
  84  
  85      const parts = buffer.split("\n\n");
  86      // Keep incomplete trailing event chunk in buffer
  87      buffer = parts.pop() ?? "";
  88  
  89      for (const part of parts) {
  90        const trimmed = part.trim();
  91        if (!trimmed) continue;
  92  
  93        const lines = trimmed.split("\n");
  94        for (const line of lines) {
  95          if (line.startsWith("data:")) {
  96            const raw = line.slice(5).trim();
  97            if (!raw) continue;
  98            try {
  99              const event: StreamEventPayload = JSON.parse(raw);
 100              switch (event.type) {
 101                case "start":
 102                  if (event.conversationId) callbacks.onStart?.(event.conversationId);
 103                  break;
 104                case "source":
 105                  if (event.sources) callbacks.onSources?.(event.sources);
 106                  break;
 107                case "tool_call":
 108                  if (event.tool) callbacks.onToolCall?.(event.tool, event.args);
 109                  break;
 110                case "tool_result":
 111                  if (event.tool) callbacks.onToolResult?.(event.tool, event.result);
 112                  break;
 113                case "token":
 114                  if (event.token) callbacks.onToken?.(event.token);
 115                  break;
 116                case "confirmation":
 117                  callbacks.onConfirmation?.(!!event.confirmed, event.orderId);
 118                  break;
 119                case "done":
 120                  callbacks.onDone?.({
 121                    conversationId: event.conversationId ?? params.conversationId ?? 0,
 122                    messageId: event.messageId,
 123                    orderId: event.orderId,
 124                  });
 125                  break;
 126                case "error":
 127                  callbacks.onError?.({
 128                    status: xhr.status,
 129                    message: event.message || "Une erreur est survenue.",
 130                  });
 131                  break;
 132              }
 133            } catch {
 134              // Ignore incomplete or transient JSON parsing errors
 135            }
 136          }
 137        }
 138      }
 139    };
 140  
 141    xhr.onprogress = () => {
 142      if (isAborted) return;
 143      if (xhr.status === 200) {
 144        processBuffer();
 145      }
 146    };
 147  
 148    xhr.onreadystatechange = () => {
 149      if (isAborted) return;
 150  
 151      if (xhr.readyState === 3 && xhr.status === 200) {
 152        processBuffer();
 153      } else if (xhr.readyState === 4) {
 154        if (xhr.status === 200) {
 155          processBuffer();
 156        } else if (xhr.status === 429) {
 157          callbacks.onError?.({
 158            status: 429,
 159            isRateLimit: true,
 160            message:
 161              "Trop de requêtes vers l'assistant IA (max 20 messages / minute). Veuillez patienter quelques instants.",
 162          });
 163        } else if (xhr.status === 401) {
 164          callbacks.onError?.({
 165            status: 401,
 166            message: "Session expirée. Veuillez vous reconnecter.",
 167          });
 168        } else if (xhr.status > 0) {
 169          let errorMsg = "Erreur de communication avec le serveur.";
 170          try {
 171            const parsed = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
 172            errorMsg = parsed.error || parsed.message || errorMsg;
 173          } catch {}
 174          callbacks.onError?.({
 175            status: xhr.status,
 176            message: errorMsg,
 177          });
 178        }
 179      }
 180    };
 181  
 182    xhr.onerror = () => {
 183      if (isAborted) return;
 184      callbacks.onError?.({
 185        message: "Erreur de connexion réseau. Vérifiez votre connexion Internet.",
 186      });
 187    };
 188  
 189    xhr.send(
 190      JSON.stringify({
 191        message: params.message,
 192        conversationId: params.conversationId,
 193      })
 194    );
 195  
 196    return {
 197      abort: () => {
 198        isAborted = true;
 199        xhr.abort();
 200      },
 201    };
 202  }
 203  
 204  export interface ConversationSummary {
 205    id: number;
 206    title: string;
 207    updatedAt: string;
 208    messages: Array<{
 209      id: number;
 210      role: "user" | "assistant";
 211      content: string;
 212      createdAt: string;
 213    }>;
 214  }
 215  
 216  /**
 217   * Fetch customer's past conversation history from the backend.
 218   */
 219  export async function fetchConversationHistory(): Promise<ConversationSummary[]> {
 220    const response = await apiClient.get<{
 221      conversations: ConversationSummary[];
 222    }>("/api/agent/conversations");
 223    return response.data.conversations;
 224  }
 225  
 226  /**
 227   * Fetch a single conversation with all its messages.
 228   */
 229  export async function fetchSingleConversation(id: number): Promise<ConversationSummary> {
 230    const response = await apiClient.get<{
 231      conversation: ConversationSummary;
 232    }>(`/api/agent/conversations/${id}`);
 233    return response.data.conversation;
 234  }
mobile/src/api/client.ts
باش ما نكتبوش Authorization ومعالجة401 فكل شاشة، client كيجمعهم.
   1  import axios, { AxiosError, InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from "axios";
   2  import { API_BASE_URL } from "../config/api";
   3  import { clearTokens, getTokens, saveTokens } from "../auth/tokenStorage";
   4  
   5  export const apiClient = axios.create({
   6    baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
   7    headers: {
   8      "Content-Type": "application/json",
   9    },
  10    timeout: 10000,
  11  });
  12  
  13  // Callback to notify auth store when a clean logout is needed
  14  let onAuthFailedCallback: (() => void) | null = null;
  15  let onTokensUpdatedCallback:
  16    | ((tokens: { accessToken: string; refreshToken: string }) => void)
  17    | null = null;
  18  
  19  export function setAuthCallbacks(callbacks: {
  20    onAuthFailed: () => void;
  21    onTokensUpdated: (tokens: { accessToken: string; refreshToken: string }) => void;
  22  }) {
  23    onAuthFailedCallback = callbacks.onAuthFailed;
  24    onTokensUpdatedCallback = callbacks.onTokensUpdated;
  25  }
  26  
  27  // Request Interceptor: Attach access token
  28  apiClient.interceptors.request.use(
  29    async (config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
  30      const { accessToken } = await getTokens();
  31      if (accessToken && config.headers) {
  32        config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${accessToken}`;
  33      }
  34      return config;
  35    },
  36    (error) => Promise.reject(error)
  37  );
  38  
  39  // Active refresh promise to deduplicate concurrent refresh calls
  40  let refreshPromise: Promise<string> | null = null;
  41  
  42  // Response Interceptor: Handle 401, refresh token once, retry or log out
  43  apiClient.interceptors.response.use(
  44    (response) => response,
  45    async (error: AxiosError) => {
  46      const originalRequest = error.config as InternalAxiosRequestConfig & {
  47        _retry?: boolean;
  48      };
  49  
  50      // If no response or status is not 401, reject immediately
  51      if (!error.response || error.response.status !== 401 || !originalRequest) {
  52        return Promise.reject(error);
  53      }
  54  
  55      // Do not retry refresh or login endpoints to prevent infinite loops
  56      const requestUrl = originalRequest.url || "";
  57      if (
  58        requestUrl.includes("/api/auth/refresh") ||
  59        requestUrl.includes("/api/auth/login") ||
  60        originalRequest._retry
  61      ) {
  62        await clearTokens();
  63        if (onAuthFailedCallback) {
  64          onAuthFailedCallback();
  65        }
  66        return Promise.reject(error);
  67      }
  68  
  69      originalRequest._retry = true;
  70  
  71      try {
  72        if (!refreshPromise) {
  73          refreshPromise = (async () => {
  74            const { refreshToken } = await getTokens();
  75  
  76            if (!refreshToken) {
  77              throw new Error("No refresh token available");
  78            }
  79  
  80            // Call refresh endpoint with raw axios to bypass interceptor
  81            const refreshResponse = await axios.post<{
  82              accessToken: string;
  83              refreshToken: string;
  84            }>(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/refresh`, {
  85              refreshToken,
  86            });
  87  
  88            const { accessToken: newAccessToken, refreshToken: newRefreshToken } =
  89              refreshResponse.data;
  90  
  91            // Persist newly rotated tokens
  92            await saveTokens({
  93              accessToken: newAccessToken,
  94              refreshToken: newRefreshToken,
  95            });
  96  
  97            if (onTokensUpdatedCallback) {
  98              onTokensUpdatedCallback({
  99                accessToken: newAccessToken,
 100                refreshToken: newRefreshToken,
 101              });
 102            }
 103  
 104            return newAccessToken;
 105          })().finally(() => {
 106            refreshPromise = null;
 107          });
 108        }
 109  
 110        const newAccessToken = await refreshPromise;
 111  
 112        // Update failed request's Authorization header and retry
 113        if (originalRequest.headers) {
 114          originalRequest.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${newAccessToken}`;
 115        }
 116  
 117        return apiClient(originalRequest);
 118      } catch (refreshError) {
 119        // Refresh failed or revoked: log out cleanly
 120        await clearTokens();
 121        if (onAuthFailedCallback) {
 122          onAuthFailedCallback();
 123        }
 124        return Promise.reject(refreshError);
 125      }
 126    }
 127  );
mobile/src/auth/tokenStorage.ts
Zustand كيجمع حالة الجلسة والدوال اللي كتغيرها فبلاصة واحدة.
   1  import * as SecureStore from "expo-secure-store";
   2  import { Platform } from "react-native";
   3  
   4  const ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY = "shopmate_access_token";
   5  const REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY = "shopmate_refresh_token";
   6  
   7  export interface StoredTokens {
   8    accessToken: string | null;
   9    refreshToken: string | null;
  10  }
  11  
  12  export async function saveTokens(tokens: {
  13    accessToken: string;
  14    refreshToken: string;
  15  }): Promise<void> {
  16    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
  17      try {
  18        localStorage.setItem(ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY, tokens.accessToken);
  19        localStorage.setItem(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY, tokens.refreshToken);
  20      } catch {
  21        // Ignore web storage errors
  22      }
  23      return;
  24    }
  25  
  26    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY, tokens.accessToken);
  27    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY, tokens.refreshToken);
  28  }
  29  
  30  export async function getTokens(): Promise<StoredTokens> {
  31    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
  32      try {
  33        return {
  34          accessToken: localStorage.getItem(ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY),
  35          refreshToken: localStorage.getItem(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY),
  36        };
  37      } catch {
  38        return { accessToken: null, refreshToken: null };
  39      }
  40    }
  41  
  42    const [accessToken, refreshToken] = await Promise.all([
  43      SecureStore.getItemAsync(ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY),
  44      SecureStore.getItemAsync(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY),
  45    ]);
  46  
  47    return { accessToken, refreshToken };
  48  }
  49  
  50  export async function clearTokens(): Promise<void> {
  51    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
  52      try {
  53        localStorage.removeItem(ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY);
  54        localStorage.removeItem(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY);
  55      } catch {
  56        // Ignore web storage errors
  57      }
  58      return;
  59    }
  60  
  61    await Promise.all([
  62      SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY),
  63      SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY),
  64    ]);
  65  }

TypeScript كيوصف البيانات، وJavaScript هو السلوك اللي كيتنفذ. TSX كيخلينا نكتب واجهة React مع الكود.
   1  import React from "react";
   2  import {
   3    ActivityIndicator,
   4    Pressable,
   5    StyleSheet,
   6    Text,
   7    ViewStyle,
   8    TextStyle,
   9  } from "react-native";
  10  import { colorTokens, radiusTokens, spacingTokens } from "../theme/tokens";
  11  
  12  export type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "outline" | "danger" | "ghost";
  13  export type ButtonSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  14  
  15  export interface ButtonProps {
  16    title: string;
  17    onPress: () => void;
  18    variant?: ButtonVariant;
  19    size?: ButtonSize;
  20    loading?: boolean;
  21    disabled?: boolean;
  22    icon?: React.ReactNode;
  23    style?: ViewStyle;
  24    textStyle?: TextStyle;
  25  }
  26  
  27  export function Button({
  28    title,
  29    onPress,
  30    variant = "primary",
  31    size = "md",
  32    loading = false,
  33    disabled = false,
  34    icon,
  35    style,
  36    textStyle,
  37  }: ButtonProps) {
  38    const isDisabled = disabled || loading;
  39  
  40    return (
  41      <Pressable
  42        onPress={onPress}
  43        disabled={isDisabled}
  44        style={({ pressed }) => [
  45          styles.base,
  46          styles[variant],
  47          styles[`size_${size}`],
  48          pressed && styles.pressed,
  49          isDisabled && styles.disabled,
  50          style,
  51        ]}
  52      >
  53        {loading ? (
  54          <ActivityIndicator
  55            size="small"
  56            color={variant === "outline" || variant === "ghost" ? colorTokens.primary : colorTokens.surface}
  57          />
  58        ) : (
  59          <>
  60            {icon ? <>{icon}</> : null}
  61            <Text
  62              style={[
  63                styles.text,
  64                styles[`text_${variant}`],
  65                styles[`textSize_${size}`],
  66                textStyle,
  67              ]}
  68            >
  69              {title}
  70            </Text>
  71          </>
  72        )}
  73      </Pressable>
  74    );
  75  }
  76  
  77  const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  78    base: {
  79      flexDirection: "row",
  80      alignItems: "center",
  81      justifyContent: "center",
  82      borderRadius: radiusTokens.lg,
  83      gap: spacingTokens.sm,
  84    },
  85    pressed: {
  86      opacity: 0.85,
  87      transform: [{ scale: 0.99 }],
  88    },
  89    disabled: {
  90      opacity: 0.5,
  91    },
  92    // Variants
  93    primary: {
  94      backgroundColor: colorTokens.primary,
  95      shadowColor: colorTokens.primary,
  96      shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 2 },
  97      shadowOpacity: 0.18,
  98      shadowRadius: 4,
  99      elevation: 2,
 100    },
 101    secondary: {
 102      backgroundColor: colorTokens.secondaryContainer,
 103    },
 104    outline: {
 105      backgroundColor: "transparent",
 106      borderWidth: 1,
 107      borderColor: colorTokens.border,
 108    },
 109    danger: {
 110      backgroundColor: colorTokens.errorContainer,
 111    },
 112    ghost: {
 113      backgroundColor: "transparent",
 114    },
 115    // Sizes
 116    size_sm: {
 117      height: 36,
 118      paddingHorizontal: spacingTokens.md,
 119    },
 120    size_md: {
 121      height: 48,
 122      paddingHorizontal: spacingTokens.lg,
 123    },
 124    size_lg: {
 125      height: 54,
 126      paddingHorizontal: spacingTokens.xl,
 127    },
 128    // Text Styles
 129    text: {
 130      fontWeight: "700",
 131    },
 132    text_primary: {
 133      color: colorTokens.onPrimary,
 134    },
 135    text_secondary: {

mobile/src/components/Loader.tsx
القيم المشتركة كتخلي الشاشات تستعمل نفس اللغة البصرية.
   1  import React from "react";
   2  import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, Text, View, ViewStyle } from "react-native";
   3  import { colorTokens, spacingTokens } from "../theme/tokens";
   4  
   5  export interface LoaderProps {
   6    message?: string;
   7    size?: "small" | "large";
   8    color?: string;
   9    style?: ViewStyle;
  10  }
  11  
  12  export function Loader({
  13    message,
  14    size = "large",
  15    color = colorTokens.primary,
  16    style,
  17  }: LoaderProps) {
  18    return (
  19      <View style={[styles.container, style]}>
  20        <ActivityIndicator size={size} color={color} />
  21        {message ? <Text style={styles.message}>{message}</Text> : null}
  22      </View>
  23    );
  24  }
  25  
  26  const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  27    container: {
  28      flex: 1,
  29      alignItems: "center",
  30      justifyContent: "center",
  31      padding: spacingTokens.xl,
  32      minHeight: 180,
  33    },
  34    message: {
  35      marginTop: spacingTokens.md,
  36      fontSize: 14,
  37      color: colorTokens.muted,
  38      textAlign: "center",
  39    },
  40  });
mobile/src/config/api.ts
باش ما نكتبوش Authorization ومعالجة401 فكل شاشة، client كيجمعهم.
   1  import { Platform } from "react-native";
   2  
   3  // In development:
   4  // Android Emulator uses 10.0.2.2 instead of localhost
   5  // Physical phone uses the computer's LAN IP (e.g. http://192.168.1.X:4000)
   6  const defaultHost =
   7    Platform.OS === "android" ? "http://10.0.2.2:4000" : "http://localhost:4000";
   8  
   9  export const API_BASE_URL: string =
  10    process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL || defaultHost;
mobile/src/stores/authStore.ts
Zustand كيجمع حالة الجلسة والدوال اللي كتغيرها فبلاصة واحدة.
   1  import { create } from "zustand";
   2  import { apiClient, setAuthCallbacks } from "../api/client";
   3  import { clearTokens, getTokens, saveTokens } from "../auth/tokenStorage";
   4  
   5  export interface AuthUser {
   6    id: number;
   7    storeId: number;
   8    name: string;
   9    email: string;
  10    role: "ADMIN" | "CUSTOMER";
  11  }
  12  
  13  interface AuthState {
  14    user: AuthUser | null;
  15    accessToken: string | null;
  16    refreshToken: string | null;
  17    isAuthenticated: boolean;
  18    isLoading: boolean;
  19    error: string | null;
  20  
  21    initializeAuth: () => Promise<void>;
  22    login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  23    register: (name: string, email: string, password: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  24    logout: () => Promise<void>;
  25    setTokens: (tokens: { accessToken: string; refreshToken: string }) => void;
  26    clearError: () => void;
  27  }
  28  
  29  export const useAuthStore = create<AuthState>((set, get) => {
  30    // Wire up the axios interceptor callbacks
  31    setAuthCallbacks({
  32      onAuthFailed: () => {
  33        set({
  34          user: null,
  35          accessToken: null,
  36          refreshToken: null,
  37          isAuthenticated: false,
  38          error: "Session expired. Please log in again.",
  39        });
  40      },
  41      onTokensUpdated: ({ accessToken, refreshToken }) => {
  42        set({ accessToken, refreshToken });
  43      },
  44    });
  45  
  46    return {
  47      user: null,
  48      accessToken: null,
  49      refreshToken: null,
  50      isAuthenticated: false,
  51      isLoading: true,
  52      error: null,
  53  
  54      clearError: () => set({ error: null }),
  55  
  56      setTokens: ({ accessToken, refreshToken }) => {
  57        set({ accessToken, refreshToken });
  58      },
  59  
  60      initializeAuth: async () => {
  61        set({ isLoading: true, error: null });
  62        try {
  63          const { accessToken, refreshToken } = await getTokens();
  64  
  65          if (!accessToken && !refreshToken) {
  66            set({
  67              user: null,
  68              accessToken: null,
  69              refreshToken: null,
  70              isAuthenticated: false,
  71              isLoading: false,
  72            });
  73            return;
  74          }
  75  
  76          // Fetch user profile from /me endpoint
  77          const response = await apiClient.get<{
  78            user: {
  79              userId: number;
  80              storeId: number;
  81              role: "ADMIN" | "CUSTOMER";
  82              email: string;
  83            };
  84          }>("/api/auth/me");
  85  
  86          const userData = response.data.user;
  87          const currentTokens = await getTokens();
  88  
  89          set({
  90            user: {
  91              id: userData.userId,
  92              storeId: userData.storeId,
  93              name: userData.email.split("@")[0], // Fallback name
  94              email: userData.email,
  95              role: userData.role,
  96            },
  97            accessToken: currentTokens.accessToken ?? accessToken,
  98            refreshToken: currentTokens.refreshToken ?? refreshToken,
  99            isAuthenticated: true,
 100            isLoading: false,
 101          });
 102        } catch {
 103          await clearTokens();
 104          set({
 105            user: null,
 106            accessToken: null,
 107            refreshToken: null,
 108            isAuthenticated: false,
 109            isLoading: false,
 110          });
 111        }
 112      },
 113  
 114      login: async (email: string, password: string) => {
 115        set({ isLoading: true, error: null });
 116        try {
 117          const response = await apiClient.post<{
 118            message: string;
 119            user: AuthUser;
 120            accessToken: string;
 121            refreshToken: string;
 122          }>("/api/auth/login", {
 123            email,
 124            password,
 125          });
 126  
 127          const { user, accessToken, refreshToken } = response.data;
 128  
 129          await saveTokens({ accessToken, refreshToken });
 130  
 131          set({
 132            user,
 133            accessToken,
 134            refreshToken,
 135            isAuthenticated: true,
 136            isLoading: false,
 137            error: null,
 138          });
 139  
 140          return true;
 141        } catch (err: any) {
 142          const message =
 143            err.response?.data?.error ||
 144            err.response?.data?.message ||
 145            "Unable to connect to server";
 146  
 147          set({
 148            isLoading: false,
 149            error: message,
 150          });
 151          return false;
 152        }
 153      },
 154  
 155      register: async (name: string, email: string, password: string) => {
 156        set({ isLoading: true, error: null });
 157        try {
 158          const response = await apiClient.post<{
 159            message: string;
 160            user: AuthUser;
 161            accessToken: string;
 162            refreshToken: string;
 163          }>("/api/auth/register", {
 164            name,
 165            email,
 166            password,
 167          });
 168  
 169          const { user, accessToken, refreshToken } = response.data;
 170  
 171          await saveTokens({ accessToken, refreshToken });
 172  
 173          set({
 174            user,
 175            accessToken,
 176            refreshToken,
 177            isAuthenticated: true,
 178            isLoading: false,
 179            error: null,
 180          });
 181  
 182          return true;
 183        } catch (err: any) {
 184          const message =
 185            err.response?.data?.error ||
 186            err.response?.data?.message ||
 187            "Unable to register account";
 188  
 189          set({
 190            isLoading: false,
 191            error: message,
 192          });
 193          return false;
 194        }
 195      },
 196  
 197      logout: async () => {
 198        const { refreshToken } = get();
 199  
 200        // Fire-and-forget logout on server to invalidate token
 201        if (refreshToken) {
 202          try {
 203            await apiClient.post("/api/auth/logout", { refreshToken });
 204          } catch {
 205            // Ignore network errors on logout
 206          }
 207        }
 208  
 209        await clearTokens();
 210  
 211        set({
 212          user: null,
 213          accessToken: null,
 214          refreshToken: null,
 215          isAuthenticated: false,
 216          isLoading: false,
 217          error: null,
 218        });
 219      },
 220    };
 221  });
mobile/src/stores/catalogStore.ts
الشاشة كتعرض وتجمع الاختيارات، والـstore كيرسل الفلاتر وكيحتافظ بالنتائج.
   1  import { create } from "zustand";
   2  import { apiClient } from "../api/client";
   3  
   4  export interface Category {
   5    id: number;
   6    name: string;
   7    description?: string;
   8  }
   9  
  10  export interface Product {
  11    id: number;
  12    storeId?: number;
  13    name: string;
  14    description?: string;
  15    imageUrl?: string | null;
  16    price: number;
  17    stock: number;
  18    sku?: string;
  19    categoryId?: number;
  20    category?: {
  21      id: number;
  22      name: string;
  23      description?: string;
  24    };
  25  }
  26  
  27  interface PaginationData {
  28    total: number;
  29    page: number;
  30    limit: number;
  31    totalPages: number;
  32  }
  33  
  34  interface CatalogState {
  35    products: Product[];
  36    categories: Category[];
  37    selectedCategoryId: number | null;
  38    search: string;
  39    minPrice?: number;
  40    maxPrice?: number;
  41    page: number;
  42    hasMore: boolean;
  43    status: "idle" | "loading" | "loadingMore" | "success" | "error";
  44    error: string | null;
  45  
  46    setSearch: (search: string) => void;
  47    setSelectedCategory: (categoryId: number | null) => void;
  48    setPriceRange: (min?: number, max?: number) => void;
  49    fetchCategories: () => Promise<void>;
  50    fetchProducts: (refresh?: boolean) => Promise<void>;
  51    loadMore: () => Promise<void>;
  52    fetchProductDetails: (id: number) => Promise<Product | null>;
  53    resetFilters: () => void;
  54  }
  55  
  56  export const useCatalogStore = create<CatalogState>((set, get) => ({
  57    products: [],
  58    categories: [],
  59    selectedCategoryId: null,
  60    search: "",
  61    minPrice: undefined,
  62    maxPrice: undefined,
  63    page: 1,
  64    hasMore: true,
  65    status: "idle",
  66    error: null,
  67  
  68    setSearch: (search: string) => {
  69      set({ search });
  70    },
  71  
  72    setSelectedCategory: (categoryId: number | null) => {
  73      set({ selectedCategoryId: categoryId });
  74    },
  75  
  76    setPriceRange: (min?: number, max?: number) => {
  77      set({ minPrice: min, maxPrice: max });
  78    },
  79  
  80    resetFilters: () => {
  81      set({
  82        search: "",
  83        selectedCategoryId: null,
  84        minPrice: undefined,
  85        maxPrice: undefined,
  86        page: 1,
  87      });
  88    },
  89  
  90    fetchCategories: async () => {
  91      try {
  92        const res = await apiClient.get<{ categories: Category[] }>("/api/categories");
  93        set({ categories: res.data.categories || [] });
  94      } catch {
  95        // Fallback categories if offline
  96      }
  97    },
  98  
  99    fetchProducts: async (refresh = false) => {
 100      const { search, selectedCategoryId, minPrice, maxPrice, status } = get();
 101      if (!refresh && status === "loading") return;
 102  
 103      set({ status: "loading", error: null, page: 1 });
 104  
 105      try {
 106        const params: Record<string, string | number> = {
 107          page: 1,
 108          limit: 10,
 109        };
 110  
 111        if (search.trim()) {
 112          params.q = search.trim();
 113        }
 114        if (selectedCategoryId) {
 115          params.categoryId = selectedCategoryId;
 116        }
 117        if (minPrice !== undefined && minPrice > 0) {
 118          params.minPrice = minPrice;
 119        }
 120        if (maxPrice !== undefined && maxPrice > 0) {
 121          params.maxPrice = maxPrice;
 122        }
 123  
 124        const response = await apiClient.get<{
 125          products: Product[];
 126          pagination: PaginationData;
 127        }>("/api/products", { params });
 128  
 129        const products = response.data.products || [];
 130        const pagination = response.data.pagination;
 131  
 132        set({
 133          products,
 134          hasMore: pagination ? pagination.page < pagination.totalPages : false,
 135          page: 1,
 136          status: "success",
 137          error: null,
 138        });
 139      } catch (err: any) {
 140        set({
 141          status: "error",
 142          error: err.response?.data?.error || "Impossible de charger les produits.",
 143        });
 144      }
 145    },
 146  
 147    loadMore: async () => {
 148      const {
 149        page,
 150        hasMore,
 151        status,
 152        products,
 153        search,
 154        selectedCategoryId,
 155        minPrice,
 156        maxPrice,
 157      } = get();
 158  
 159      if (!hasMore || status === "loading" || status === "loadingMore") return;
 160  
 161      set({ status: "loadingMore" });
 162  
 163      try {
 164        const nextPage = page + 1;
 165        const params: Record<string, string | number> = {
 166          page: nextPage,
 167          limit: 10,
 168        };
 169  
 170        if (search.trim()) {
 171          params.q = search.trim();
 172        }
 173        if (selectedCategoryId) {
 174          params.categoryId = selectedCategoryId;
 175        }
 176        if (minPrice !== undefined) {
 177          params.minPrice = minPrice;
 178        }
 179        if (maxPrice !== undefined) {
 180          params.maxPrice = maxPrice;
 181        }
 182  
 183        const response = await apiClient.get<{
 184          products: Product[];
 185          pagination: PaginationData;
 186        }>("/api/products", { params });
 187  
 188        const newProducts = response.data.products || [];
 189        const pagination = response.data.pagination;
 190  
 191        set({
 192          products: [...products, ...newProducts],
 193          page: nextPage,
 194          hasMore: pagination ? pagination.page < pagination.totalPages : false,
 195          status: "success",
 196        });
 197      } catch {
 198        set({ status: "success" });
 199      }
 200    },
 201  
 202    fetchProductDetails: async (id: number): Promise<Product | null> => {
 203      try {
 204        const res = await apiClient.get<{ product: Product }>(`/api/products/${id}`);
 205        return res.data.product || null;
 206      } catch {
 207        return null;
 208      }
 209    },
 210  }));

   2    "extends": "expo/tsconfig.base",
   3    "compilerOptions": {
   4      "strict": true,
   5      "paths": {
   6        "@/*": [
   7          "./*"
   8        ]
   9      }
  10    },
  11    "include": [
  12      "**/*.ts",
  13      "**/*.tsx",
  14      ".expo/types/**/*.ts",
  15      "expo-env.d.ts"
